# 零压拆船厂 · 界面文字（玩家现在看得见的；不含角色台词，台词在 data/台词.txt）
#
# 怎么改：
#   每条以 [编号] 开头。「中：」后面就是游戏现在显示的中文，直接改成你要的；「英：」同理。
#   「英：」空着的是这处现在只有中文（英文版也显示中文）；可以补，也可以先不管。
#   %s、%d、%.1f、{name} 这类是游戏会填进去的数字或名字，要原样留着（个数和顺序都别变）。
#   \n 是换行，留着或按需要加减都行。[%s]、Enter、Esc 这类是按键名，留着。
#   编号不要改；不想改的条目原样留着就行。# 开头的是说明，不用改。
#   按玩家碰到的顺序分章；每章、每个界面开头写了这些字出现在哪里、是干什么的。
#   最后一章是短标签（按钮、栏目名）：同样的字只列一次，改一条所有地方一起改。
#
# 已停用的功能（梁工、关掉的剧情层、取消的耗材）、开发者面板、只打印到日志的字不在这里。
#
# 本文件共 9783 条。

============================================================
# 一、标题、菜单与设置（118 条）
# 开游戏第一眼看到的：标题终端（继续 / 新生涯 / 训练 / 设置）、暂停菜单、设置页、按键说明、提示档位。
# 这些字要短，像机器面板上的字。
============================================================

============================================================
# 二、新手：序章与 TS-1 训练（101 条）
# 新生涯一开始：小行星带序章（把第一艘拖船拖回港），然后 TS-1 训练舱段（9 课基础 + 专项课），最后考核。
# 玩家这时什么都不懂：字要告诉他“现在做什么、为什么”。
============================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
# 【考核】
# 出现在：学徒考核的目标、已知条件、求提示。
------------------------------------------------------------

[txt:ex_g_A]
中：独立完成 · 安全回收 · 设备完整
英：UNAIDED · SAFE · INTACT

[txt:ex_g_B]
中：已回收 · 用过提示或有轻微损坏
英：RECOVERED · WITH A HINT OR LIGHT DAMAGE

[txt:ex_g_C]
中：已拆下 · 设备严重损坏
英：REMOVED · BADLY DAMAGED

------------------------------------------------------------
# 【考核扣分理由】
# 出现在：考核结束时每一处扣分写的原因。
------------------------------------------------------------

[txt:rv_w_pressure]
中：舱室尚未完成泄压，就直接开启了舱门。
英：COMPARTMENT OPENED BEFORE DEPRESSURIZATION WAS COMPLETE.

[txt:rv_y_pressure]
中：开启前未通过与舱室连通的测点确认内部压力，也没有完成泄压处理。舱门开启时仍存在压差，违反安全操作要求。
英：INTERNAL PRESSURE WAS NOT VERIFIED AT A TEST POINT CONNECTED TO THE COMPARTMENT, AND DEPRESSURIZATION WAS NOT COMPLETED. A PRESSURE DIFFERENTIAL REMAINED WHEN THE DOOR WAS OPENED.

[txt:rv_w_power]
中：电气回路尚未断电，就直接拆除了连接部件。
英：ELECTRICAL CONNECTION REMOVED WHILE THE CIRCUIT WAS STILL ENERGIZED.

[txt:rv_y_power]
中：设备停止运行后仍可能带电。拆卸前应先切断所有供电及储能回路，释放残余电能，并在测点确认电压为 0 V。
英：EQUIPMENT MAY REMAIN ENERGIZED EVEN WHEN STOPPED. ISOLATE ALL POWER SOURCES, DISCHARGE STORED ENERGY, AND VERIFY 0 V AT THE TEST POINT BEFORE DISCONNECTING.

[txt:rv_w_tied]
中：部件尚有连接未解除，强行拖拽导致连接处断裂（%s）。
英：PART WAS PULLED BEFORE ALL CONNECTIONS WERE RELEASED, CAUSING DAMAGE AT THE ATTACHMENT POINTS (%s).

[txt:rv_y_tied]
中：拖动前未确认部件是否完全脱离船体。应先使用检测工具检查固定点和连接管线，全部解除后再进行拖运。
英：THE PART WAS NOT FULLY DETACHED BEFORE MOVING. USE THE INSPECTION TOOL TO LOCATE ALL MOUNTING POINTS AND CONNECTED LINES, THEN RELEASE THEM BEFORE TOWING.

[txt:rv_w_tied_lines]
中：拖动部件时仍有管道或电缆连接，强行拖拽导致管线断裂（%s）。
英：PART MOVED WITH PIPES OR CABLES STILL ATTACHED, CAUSING CONNECTIONS TO TEAR (%s).

[txt:rv_y_tied_lines]
中：拆除了固定件，但没有检查剩余的管道和电缆连接。应分别确认所有连接点，全部解除后才能拖动部件。
英：MOUNTING POINTS WERE RELEASED, BUT PIPE AND CABLE CONNECTIONS WERE NOT FULLY CHECKED. ALL CONNECTIONS MUST BE INSPECTED AND DISCONNECTED BEFORE MOVING THE PART.

[txt:rv_w_tied_hidden]
中：检修面板后仍有隐藏固定点未解除，强行拖动导致连接处断裂（%s）。
英：PART MOVED WITH A HIDDEN MOUNTING POINT STILL ATTACHED BEHIND THE ACCESS PANEL, CAUSING DAMAGE (%s).

[txt:rv_y_tied_hidden]
中：检测结果显示的固定点数量多于当前可见数量，但没有进一步排查。应先拆除检修面板，找到隐藏的固定点并解除连接，再进行拖运。
英：THE INSPECTION REPORTED MORE MOUNTING POINTS THAN WERE VISIBLE, BUT THE REMAINING CONNECTION WAS NOT LOCATED. REMOVE THE ACCESS PANEL, FIND AND RELEASE THE HIDDEN MOUNT BEFORE MOVING THE PART.

[txt:rv_w_tank]
中：储气罐内压力过高，拆解过程中发生爆裂。
英：GAS TANK RUPTURED UNDER EXCESSIVE INTERNAL PRESSURE DURING DISMANTLING.

[txt:rv_y_tank]
中：舱室已经泄压，但内部储气罐仍存有高压气体。拆解前应单独连接回收软管，确认罐内气体已排空并完成泄压。
英：THE COMPARTMENT WAS DEPRESSURIZED, BUT THE GAS TANK INSIDE REMAINED PRESSURIZED. CONNECT A SEPARATE RECOVERY HOSE AND VERIFY THE TANK IS EMPTY AND DEPRESSURIZED BEFORE DISMANTLING.

[txt:rv_w_drain]
中：管路尚未排空就拆除了接头，导致内部液体泄漏。
英：PIPE CONNECTION REMOVED BEFORE DRAINING WAS COMPLETE, CAUSING RESIDUAL FLUID TO LEAK.

[txt:rv_y_drain]
中：拆卸前未完成管路排液和冷却处理。应先排空内部液体，等待管路温度降至安全范围，再拆除连接件。
英：THE LINE WAS NOT FULLY DRAINED OR COOLED BEFORE DISCONNECTION. DRAIN ALL REMAINING FLUID AND ALLOW THE LINE TO REACH A SAFE TEMPERATURE BEFORE REMOVING THE FITTINGS.

[txt:rv_w_cut]
中：激光切割时误伤设备本体，造成部件损坏（%s）。
英：LASER CUTTING DAMAGED THE EQUIPMENT ITSELF (%s).

[txt:rv_y_cut]
中：设备原本可以通过解除固定件和连接管线完整拆下，但直接使用激光切割，造成了不必要的损坏。下次先检查连接结构，尽量完整拆卸。
英：THE EQUIPMENT COULD HAVE BEEN REMOVED INTACT BY RELEASING ITS MOUNTS AND CONNECTIONS. USING THE LASER CAUSED AVOIDABLE DAMAGE. INSPECT ALL CONNECTIONS AND TRY TO REMOVE THE EQUIPMENT IN ONE PIECE.

[txt:rv_w_impact]
中：部件在搬运过程中发生剧烈碰撞，导致结构受损（%s）。
英：PART DAMAGED BY A HEAVY IMPACT DURING HANDLING (%s).

[txt:rv_y_impact]
中：搬运速度过快，接近目标位置时未及时减速，导致部件碰撞受损。下次应控制拖运速度，并提前制动。
英：THE PART WAS MOVED TOO FAST AND WAS NOT SLOWED DOWN IN TIME, RESULTING IN IMPACT DAMAGE. CONTROL YOUR HAULING SPEED AND BRAKE EARLIER.

[txt:rv_w_hazard]
中：危险物品被送入熔炉，或在回收过程中发生起火事故。
英：HAZARDOUS MATERIAL WAS SENT INTO THE FURNACE OR CAUGHT FIRE DURING RECOVERY.

[txt:rv_y_hazard]
中：未确认物品的危险残留及回收去向。含有危险物质的部件应送往危险品回收口，禁止直接投入熔炉。
英：HAZARDOUS RESIDUES AND THE CORRECT DISPOSAL ROUTE WERE NOT VERIFIED. PARTS CONTAINING HAZARDOUS MATERIALS MUST GO TO THE HAZMAT COLLECTION BAY, NOT THE FURNACE.

[txt:rv_w_hint]
中：本次考核共使用 %s 次提示。
英：HINTS USED DURING ASSESSMENT: %s.

[txt:rv_y_hint]
中：考核期间使用了系统提示，最终评级最高只能获得 B 级。
英：USING HINTS DURING THE ASSESSMENT CAPS YOUR FINAL GRADE AT B.

[txt:rv_title]
中：本次合同收益损失明细
英：CONTRACT LOSS BREAKDOWN

[txt:rv_none]
中：本次作业没有发生事故，也没有产生回收折价损失。
英：NO INCIDENTS OR SALVAGE VALUE LOSSES RECORDED.

[drill:step:6]

[drill:step:7]

英：TRAINING ORDER: GO TO THE SORTING STATION AND SORT THE 1 TONNE OF MIXED SCRAP METAL PROVIDED BY THE PORT AUTHORITY.

英：Read the assessment objective. Inspect connections and measurements before isolating, discharging, draining, dismantling and hauling. Shutdown or an old reading alone does not prove safety.

[tutorial_overview:refresh:3]

英：The terminal now has EQUIPMENT: sixteen things in Berth 07 are broken; mend them with salvaged parts or money.

[onboarding:顶层:2]
中：账本开放：缺钱可以借，结算时自动扣分期
英：THE LEDGER OPENS: BORROW WHEN SHORT, INSTALMENTS TAKEN AT SETTLEMENT

[onboarding:顶层:3]
中：终端顶上多了“声望”：船拆得越多越好，别人越信你，才肯把更大更难的船交给你。
英：REPUTATION now shows at the top: the more ships you strip well, the bigger the jobs people trust you with.

[onboarding:顶层:4]
中：交易行开了：仓库里的东西可以拿去卖；“成长与建设”里可以修港区的其他地方。
英：The exchange is open: sell what is in the warehouse. Growth and Construction mends the rest of the port.

[onboarding:顶层:5]
中：训练舱开了：遇到不熟的船，可以先来这里练一下，不练也能接。
英：The training bay is open: practise on a ship type you don't know yet, or skip it.

[onboarding:顶层:6]
中：工具与技术开放
英：TOOLS AND TECHNIQUES OPEN

[onboarding:顶层:7]
中：消耗品开放
英：SUPPLIES OPEN

[onboarding:顶层:8]
中：无线电开放：回看终端发过的消息
英：THE RADIO LOG OPENS: THE TERMINAL'S MESSAGES

# 【操作小卡（op_cards.gd）】
# 出现在：第一次做某个动作时右边弹出的小卡：键位 + 一句说明。
------------------------------------------------------------

[op_cards:顶层:4]
中：终端与信息
英：TERMINAL AND INFORMATION

[op_cards:顶层:5]
中：WASD 移动无人机。太空里没有摩擦，松开方向键也会一直漂。
英：WASD moves the drone. Nothing slows you in space: it drifts on when you let go.

[op_cards:顶层:6]
中：按住冲刺，推力更大，也更费电。
英：Hold to dash: more thrust, more power.

[op_cards:顶层:7]
中：反推刹停：按住直到停下。
英：Fire against the drift until you stop.

[op_cards:顶层:8]
中：飞行稳定：开着时松开方向键会自动减速。
英：Flight assist: when on, letting go slows you down by itself.

[op_cards:顶层:9]
中：按住锁定在原地，切割、对接时手不抖。
英：Hold to stay put while cutting or docking.

[op_cards:顶层:10]
中：按住拉远，看整艘船。
英：Hold to pull back and see the whole ship.

[op_cards:顶层:11]
中：按住让时间走快，等抽气、等施工时用。
英：Hold to speed time up while pumping or waiting for the crew.

[op_cards:顶层:12]
中：测量仪：左键点零件或测试点，读质量、材质、电压和压力。动手前先读数。
英：Meter: click a piece or a test point for its mass, material, voltage and pressure. Read before you touch.

[op_cards:顶层:13]
中：激光切割：按住左键沿着要切的地方划过去；固定件切开，零件就松了。
英：Laser: hold the button and sweep along the cut; cut the fixings and the piece comes loose.

[op_cards:顶层:14]
中：磁力抓钩：左键抓住松开的零件拖着走，松开放下，甩一下能扔出去。
英：Grapple: grab a loose piece and tow it; let go to drop it, flick to throw it.

[op_cards:顶层:15]
中：维护工具：拧螺栓、开关阀门和电闸、开舱门；指着它们时自动换上。
英：Wrench: bolts, valves, switches and hatches. It comes to hand by itself when you point at one.

[op_cards:顶层:16]
中：气液回收：左键把软管接到气口或液口，把气体和液体抽回来，也能卖钱。
英：Hose: put it on a gas or fluid port and pump it back; that sells too.

[op_cards:顶层:17]
中：部署：把带来的设备（支架、吊臂等）放到船上用。
英：Deploy: set the gear you brought (stands, arms) on the ship.

[op_cards:顶层:18]
中：维修包：补无人机的损伤。
英：Repair kit: mend the drone's damage.

[op_cards:顶层:19]
中：爆破切割环：贴在厚件上一次切开，要先清空周围。
英：Breaching ring: fixed on a thick piece, it cuts through at once; clear the area first.

[op_cards:顶层:20]
中：图纸：看整船的结构、设备和管线在哪。
英：Drawing: the ship's structure, machines and lines.

[op_cards:顶层:21]
中：合同：这一单要拆什么、回收率目标和时间。
英：Contract: what this job wants, the recovery targets and the time.

[op_cards:顶层:22]
中：互动：用船坞电脑、上车、操作身边的面板。
英：Use: berth computers, vehicles, the panel next to you.

[op_cards:顶层:23]
中：把回收口移到无人机下方，近一点好送件。
英：Moves the mouth under the drone, so pieces have less way to go.

[op_cards:顶层:24]
中：右键标记零件，让搬运无人机去搬。
英：Right-click marks a piece for the hauler drone.

[op_cards:顶层:25]
中：划一块禁入区，船坞机器不进去。
英：Draws a keep-out zone the berth's machines stay out of.

[op_cards:顶层:26]
中：停下 / 恢复船坞机器。
英：Stops / resumes the berth's machines.

[op_cards:顶层:27]
中：在船坞里切换零压港全景。
英：At the berth, toggles the whole port's view.

[op_cards:顶层:28]
中：终端：找船、修港区、交易、看下一步。
英：Terminal: find ships, mend the port, trade, see the next step.

[op_cards:顶层:29]
中：星图：扫描小行星带找船，看拖船和货船在哪。
英：Star map: sweep the belt for hulks, see the tugs and freighters.

[op_cards:顶层:30]
中：在作业艇上：靠近漂浮舱段的拖眼挂上拖缆，拖到船坞外的交付区再摘缆。
英：Aboard the work boat: hitch the line to a drifting section's tow eye, tow it to the delivery box off the berth, cast off.

[op_cards:顶层:31]
中：抓钩在单体和范围模式间切换：范围模式一次抓起圈内多件散料。Shift+X 开关防护。
英：Switches the grapple between one piece and its field: the field catches several loose pieces at once. Shift+X toggles its guard.

[op_cards:顶层:32]
中：量子隧穿开关：开着时，抓钩抓着的件穿过墙壁、船体和设施；关掉或放手时卡在墙里，会等它完全出来再恢复碰撞。
英：Quantum tunnelling: on, what the grapple holds passes through walls, hull and facilities; switched off or let go inside a wall, it touches again only once it is clear.

[op_cards:顶层:33]
中：图鉴：见过的设备、危险和做法。
英：Codex: the machines, hazards and methods you have met.

[op_cards:顶层:34]
中：无线电记录：之前说过的话。
英：Radio log: what was said.

[op_cards:顶层:35]
中：开关屏幕提示。
英：Turns the on-screen hints on or off.

============================================================
# 四、新手：港区现场导览（154 条）
# 第一艘船拆完、船坞空了以后，强制带玩家亲自出坞、坐接驳车跑一遍港区每个区，在每个面板上逐个框出部件讲用途。
# 卡片上的字只写“去哪、按什么”（一行）；框选的短注释写“这个部件干什么用”（一句）。
# “港区图卡”是暂停菜单里的速查卡（旧版导览）。
============================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
# 【港区图卡（速查）（port_tour.gd）】
# 出现在：暂停菜单“港区图卡”：镜头飞到每个区，一张卡讲这个区是什么、对你有什么用、现在、修好以后、怎么修。
------------------------------------------------------------

[port_tour:_stage_line:1]
中：第 %d 期 / 共 2 期
英：PART %d OF 2

[port_tour:_stage_line:2]
中：还没修（0 / 2 期）
英：NOT MENDED YET (0 OF 2)

[port_tour:_next_line:1]
中：两期都修好了。
英：BOTH PARTS MENDED.

[port_tour:_how_line:1]
中：已经修完。
英：DONE.

[port_tour:_how_line:2]
中：在终端“成长与建设”里下单，施工队修完才算数。
英：Order it in the terminal under Growth & Build; the crew builds it, it works once built.

[port_tour:_how_line:3]
中： 现在还缺：%s
英： Still missing: %s

[port_tour:_how_line:4]
中： 材料和钱都够，可以下单。
英： You have what it takes.

[port_tour:rows:1]
中：零压港 · 全貌
英：ZERO PRESSURE PORT

[port_tour:rows:2]
中：废港，六个区围着中央转运环。你只有七号船坞，别的区都停着。
英：An abandoned port: six districts round the central ring. You have Berth 07 in the salvage district; most of the rest stands idle.

[port_tour:rows:3]
中：拆船挣钱，挣了修港。港修得越好，拆得越快，卖得越贵，船接得越大。最后让转运环转起来。
英：The whole game is one loop: strip ships for money → mend the port with money and materials → mended districts strip faster, sell dearer, take bigger ships → at last the central ring turns again.

[port_tour:rows:4]
中：第一艘拆完了。挨个看看各区干什么。
英：You have stripped your first ship. Now each district, one by one.

[port_tour:rows:5]
中： · 你的船坞
英： · YOUR BERTH

[port_tour:rows:6]
中：拆船的地方。拖回来的船推进七号船坞，你在这里断电、泄压、切割、抓取。
英：Where ships are taken apart. A hulk is pushed into Berth 07; here you isolate, vent, cut and grab.

[port_tour:rows:7]
中：钱基本从这儿来。整件设备送设备口，太宽的送重载架，碎料进熔炉炼钢锭。
英：Nearly all your money starts here: whole machines through the equipment mouth sell as machines, wide ones go to the heavy rack, scrap goes to the furnace as ingots (the port and the works need them).

[port_tour:rows:8]
中：。船坞里的照明、熔炉主火、碎屑网等设施坏着，要一项项修。
英：. The berth's lights, the furnace's main fire, the debris net and more are broken: mend them one by one.

[port_tour:rows:9]
中：全港的电从这儿出。按顺序分给各区；电力不足时，排后面的功率先降。
英：The whole port's power. The grid shares it out district by district; when it runs short the last in line get less.

[port_tour:rows:10]
中：激光、传送带、熔炉都吃电。屏幕上出“电力不足—功率 xx%”，就是这儿的事。
英：Cutting, the conveyors and the furnace all draw power. The \"short of power\" line is this district talking: more power, faster cutting and melting.

[port_tour:rows:11]
中：拖船从这里出发去小行星带，把你在星图上找到的废船拖回来；拖回来的船先停在这里的泊位上。
英：The tugs leave from here for the belt and bring home the hulks you find on the star map; they wait at its berths.

[port_tour:rows:12]
中：几个泊位就能停几艘。站越好拖得越长：44 米、88 米、不限。自家拖船，运费减半。
英：Its berths say how many hulks can wait; the better it is, the longer the ships it can tow (44 m → 88 m → any). Your own tugs halve the tow.

[port_tour:rows:13]
中：港里的仓库：一排排货架，叉车搬货。钢锭、零件、你卖出的货都从这里进出。
英：The port's warehouse: racks and forklifts. Ingots, parts and what you sell all pass through here.

[port_tour:rows:14]
中：卖掉的货从这儿发走，熔炉也从这儿取料。
英：Sold goods leave from here by forklift; the furnace draws its feed from here. The smoother it runs, the sooner you are paid.

[port_tour:rows:15]
中：港里做买卖的地方：交易行、上门的客人、公司的长期合同。
英：Where the port does business: the exchange (F8 in the terminal), visiting buyers, company contracts.

[port_tour:rows:16]
中：在这儿卖钢坯、材料、零件，买修港缺的零件。修好了，设备卖得更贵，还能买二手船。
英：Billets, materials and parts are sold here; parts you lack for repairs are bought here. Mended, it pays more for whole machines and opens the hulk market.

[port_tour:rows:17]
中：再加工的地方。钢锭炼钢坯，钢坯轧钢板。混料在这儿分开，抽回的气在这儿做液氧。
英：Where what you strip is worked again: the furnace desk makes billets, the mill rolls plate and sections, sorting and assay desks part mixed metal; the gas line separates recovered gas into liquid oxygen.

[port_tour:rows:18]
中：加工过的值钱。钢坯卖给公司，钢板型材能修港。
英：Worked goods pay far better than raw: billets sell to the firms, plate and sections also mend the port.

[port_tour:rows:19]
中：中央转运环
英：THE CENTRAL RING

[port_tour:rows:20]
中：港的中心：一圈连着所有区的环，走廊和接驳车从这里通到各区。
英：The heart of the port: a ring joining every district; the corridors and the shuttle run from here.

[port_tour:rows:21]
中：最后要让它转起来，那时才接得了远航者那样的大船。先修动力区和拖船站。
英：Getting it turning again is the game's last goal — only then can giants like the Ark come in. The power district and the tug station come first.

[port_tour:_draw_card:1]
中：港区导览 · %d / %d
英：PORT TOUR · %d OF %d

[port_tour:_draw_card:3]
中：对你有什么用
英：WHAT IT DOES FOR YOU

[port_tour:_draw_card:6]
中：怎么修 · 怎么去
英：HOW TO MEND IT

[port_tour:_draw_card:7]
中：空格 / 点一下：下一个
英：SPACE / CLICK: NEXT

[port_tour:_draw_card:8]
中：看完了。下一步看左上角。
英：SPACE / CLICK: DONE — THE MAIN-LINE CARD TAKES IT FROM HERE

[port_tour:_draw_card:9]
中：请先看一下……
英：READ IT FIRST…

[port_tour:_draw_card:10]
中： · Esc 结束导览
英： · ESC ENDS IT

# 【港区现场导览（port_walk.gd）】
# 出现在：强制导览：卡片写“去哪、按什么”；打开面板后一个个框出部件，旁边一句话讲用途；每一步完成写一句“学会了什么”。
------------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------
# 【卡住时的提示】
# 出现在：玩家卡住时弹出的“先做什么”的提示。
------------------------------------------------------------

[txt:hint_ta_turning]
中：环还在转（或没锁死），转运气闸联锁着。先在环毂里消旋、锁轴承。
英：The ring is still turning (or not locked), so its transfer airlock is interlocked. Despin it in the hub and lock the bearing first.

[txt:hint_leak_lng]
中：低温货没排空就切开了。先用货泵接口抽液，再抽蒸发气，罐子空了再拆。
英：Cut while there was cargo in it. Draw the liquid off through the cargo port, then the vapour, and open it only once it is empty.

[txt:hint_mat_armor]
中：军规装甲钢：普通激光要慢三到四倍。升级切割功率对它提速最明显；能拆螺栓就整块卸下
英：MILITARY ARMOUR: THREE TO FOUR TIMES SLOWER FOR A PLAIN LASER. MORE CUTTING POWER HELPS IT MOST; UNBOLT IT WHOLE WHERE YOU CAN

[txt:hint_mat_ti]
中：钛合金：每切一米熔掉一点价值，切缝很久才冷。整件卸下最值钱
英：TITANIUM: EVERY METRE OF KERF MELTS SOME VALUE AWAY AND STAYS HOT. WHOLE PARTS FETCH THE MOST

[txt:hint_mat_ceramic]
中：陶瓷复合装甲：连续切会受热开裂、掉价。切一小段停几秒让它散热，或者整块卸下
英：CERAMIC ARMOUR: CUT ON AND IT CRACKS FROM THE HEAT AND LOSES VALUE. CUT A LITTLE, LET IT COOL A FEW SECONDS, OR TAKE IT OFF WHOLE

[txt:hint_mat_shield]
中：隔热反射层：反光、不吸热，切得慢；这层不值钱，先剥掉再切里面
英：HEAT-SHIELD LAYER: IT REFLECTS AND SHRUGS OFF HEAT, SO IT CUTS SLOWLY; IT IS WORTH LITTLE, STRIP IT FIRST

[txt:hint_mat_al]
中：铝合金：反光、散热快，火花少熔渣多，比钢略慢；回收价比钢高
英：ALUMINIUM: REFLECTIVE AND QUICK TO SHED HEAT, FEW SPARKS AND MUCH DROSS, A LITTLE SLOWER THAN STEEL; IT FETCHES MORE THAN STEEL

[txt:hint_mat_composite]
中：碳纤维复合材料：切得快但冒烟，回收价低
英：CARBON COMPOSITE: CUTS FAST BUT SMOKES, AND FETCHES LITTLE

[txt:hint_degauss_live]
中：退磁之前要先断掉牵引线圈的供电（供电柜读数归零）。
英：Kill the tow coils' supply first (the supply cabinet must read zero), then degauss.

[txt:hint_equip_rail]
中：设备口挂在天花板导轨上：按 %s 让它移到你头顶。
英：The equipment bay runs on a ceiling rail: press %s to bring it overhead.

[txt:hint_start]
中：先别急着切。用 1 测量工具点外壳上的测压口和测点，看看这艘船现在的真实状态。
英：Don't cut yet. Take tool 1 to the ports and test points on the hull — find out what this ship is really like now.

[txt:hint_dead_leg]
中：读数来自测压口实际连通的那一段。按 Tab 看图纸：测压口和舱室之间可能还隔着阀门。
英：A gauge reads whatever its port is connected to. Press Tab: there may be a valve between the port and the room.

[txt:hint_dead_leg_hose]
中：阀门关着，软管只能抽到阀门外侧那一小段空管。
英：With the valve shut the hose only reaches the empty stub outside it.

[txt:hint_not_off]
中：控制台显示的是指令，不是实测：母线仍然有电。
英：The panel shows what was commanded, not what is true: the bus is still live.

[txt:hint_remote]
中：指令已发出。它真的停了吗？用测量工具去测试点验证。
英：Command sent. Did it actually stop? Verify at a test point.

[txt:hint_measured]
中：读数会标出仪器、测点和时间；系统一变化，旧读数就会变灰过期。
英：Readings carry instrument, point and time; when the system changes they turn grey and stale.

[txt:hint_equip_cut]
中：激光靠近设备时会闪红光。切进去设备就成了废料——想完整回收，用 4 维护工具拆它的固定件。
英：The beam flashes red near equipment. Cutting into it turns it to scrap — use tool 4 on its fixings to take it out whole.

[txt:hint_arc]
中：带电部件一碰就是电弧。断开电源、放掉电容，再用电气检测仪确认 0 V。
英：Live parts arc. Isolate the sources, bleed the capacitor, then confirm 0 V with the tester.

[txt:hint_burst]
中：储气罐和舱室是两回事：舱室抽空了，罐子里可能还有压力。
英：A bottle is not its room: the room can be empty while the bottle is still full.

[txt:hint_leak_fuel]
中：推进剂泄漏会在附近形成危险区：别在里面用激光。
英：Leaking propellant makes a hazard cloud — keep the laser out of it.

[txt:hint_leak_coolant]
中：冷却液会污染附近设备。先关两侧阀门、抽空、等温度降下来再拆。
英：Coolant fouls nearby equipment. Close the valves, drain it and let it cool before you open the line.

[txt:hint_fire]
中：推进剂起火了。泄漏的地方离激光远一点。
英：The propellant caught fire. Keep the laser away from leaks.

[txt:hint_fixings]
中：还连着东西。用 1 检查它有几个固定件，用 4 拆掉或用 2 切断。
英：Something still holds it. Inspect it with 1, then undo the fixings with 4 or cut them with 2.

[txt:hint_heavy]
中：抓钩最多 3 t。切小一点，或者先拆下附属件。
英：The grapple tops out at 3 t. Cut it smaller or take parts off first.

[txt:hint_torn]
中：硬扯会扯断锁扣和线缆，设备也跟着受损。下次先用 4 解开。
英：Tearing breaks locks and lines and hurts the equipment. Undo them with 4 first.

[txt:hint_pipe_full]
中：管里还有流体。先关阀、用 5 抽空。
英：Still full. Close the valves and drain it with tool 5.

[txt:hint_pipe_hot]
中：冷却回路很烫——发电机还在运转时它不会凉下来。
英：The coolant loop is hot — it won't cool while the generator keeps running.

[txt:hint_no_power]
中：舱门电机没电。看看船坞有没有外接辅助电源的插座。
英：No power for the door motors. Look for a dock aux power socket.

[txt:hint_hatch_jam]
中：舱门变形卡住了，先用 4 解除卡滞。
英：The hatch is bent and jammed — clear the jam with tool 4 first.

[txt:hint_hose]
中：软管在后台抽，不用守着。按 5 可以切换回收 / 排放。
英：The hose works in the background. Press 5 again to switch recover / vent.

[txt:hint_blowout]
中：带压舱室被切开会爆发性失压：货物被推撞，气体收益也没了。
英：Cutting into a pressurised room blows it out: cargo gets thrown and the gas is lost.

[txt:hint_discharge_refused]
中：发电机或蓄电池还在给母线供电，先断开它们的开关。
英：A generator or battery still feeds the bus — open their switches first.

[txt:hint_free_body]
中：零件脱离了。用 3 抓住它，拖或甩进对应的回收口。
英：It's free. Grab it with 3 and haul or fling it into the right mouth.

[txt:hint_impact]
中：撞击会让设备折价。慢一点，或者用 Space 刹停。
英：Impacts cost value. Slow down — Space brakes.

[txt:hint_field]
中：泊位上方和右侧是开放的。边缘有牵引场，会把漂出去的东西（包括你）轻轻拉回来。
英：The berth is open above and to the right. A tractor field at the edge eases anything drifting out — you included — back in.

[txt:hint_drone_lost]
中：R-02 损毁，新机已弹射。回到左侧机库可以免费修复。
英：R-02 lost; a new one is on its way. The bay on the left repairs for free.

[gear:hint_gear_anchor_set]
中：锚已打上。把这一段切离，绞盘就会把整段拉进熔炉槽——不用切碎。整段进炉后锚留在绞盘处，记得捡。
英：Anchor set. Cut this section free and the winch hauls the whole of it into the scrap trough — no need to cut it up. The anchor stays at the post afterwards: fetch it.

[gear:hint_gear_anchor_not_scrap]
中：传送带不收带完整设备或危险品的整段。先把设备拆下来，或把危险品排空。
英：The belt won't take a section with whole equipment or a hazard on it. Take the equipment off first, or drain the hazard.

[gear:hint_gear_pump_set]
中：泵在自己抽，不用守着。零件进回收口前记得把泵收回。
英：The pump works by itself. Take it back before the piece goes down a mouth.

[gear:hint_gear_prop_set]
中：撑杆已顶住：这一列和外侧各列的重量都压在撑杆上，里侧的列可以先卸了。旁边的读数是它扛着多少吨，过了 60 t 会弯、会断。
英：Prop set: this bay and the bays outboard bear on it now, so the bays inboard can come off first. The figure by it is the tonnes it carries: past 60 t it bows, then buckles.

[gear:hint_gear_probe_set]
中：探头留在这里，读数会一直更新，有变化会在无线电里报。
英：The probe stays here: its reading keeps updating, and a change is called out on the radio.

[gear:hint_gear_dropped]
中：掉落的装备在事故位置漂着。用抓钩或部署工具点一下捡回。
英：Dropped gear is drifting at the crash site. Pick it up with the grapple or the deploy tool.

[gear:hint_rp_part_in]
中：完整送进设备口的供电、热工、驱动控制类设备进了零件仓库，用来修船坞；不想留可以在终端出售。
英：Power, thermal and drive equipment delivered whole goes onto the parts shelf for the berth's repairs; sell it at the terminal if you would rather have the money.

[gear:hint_rp_net]
中：碎屑网破了，碎块会漏掉。在终端的船坞修缮里修它。
英：The debris net is torn and cut-offs slip through. Repair it under Berth Repairs at the terminal.

[gear:hint_cs_out]
中：%s用完了。终端里能买新的，回机库才装上。
英：The %s is worn out. Buy one at the terminal; it is fitted back at the bay.

[gear:hint_cs_cheap]
中：先锋型的精度与稳定性有限。高风险作业建议选用重装型或泰坦型。
英：Vanguard parts have limited precision and reliability. Choose Bastion or Titan for high-risk work.

[gear:hint_g4_anchor_locked]
中：钢索长度已锁止。绷紧时产生张力，松弛时不施力；抓钩可接管，回收牵引锚可解除。
英：CABLE LENGTH LOCKED. TENSION ONLY WHEN TAUT; SLACK CABLE EXERTS NO FORCE. GRAPPLE TO TAKE OVER, OR RETRIEVE THE ANCHOR TO RELEASE IT.

# 【技能书条目（growth.gd）】
# 出现在：技能书里每项升级的名字和一句效果说明。
------------------------------------------------------------

[growth:顶层:8]
中：承受的所有伤害；越低越耐用。
英：All incoming damage; lower means tougher.

[growth:顶层:10]
中：巡航、冲刺、加速和制动同步增强。
英：Cruise, boost, acceleration and braking improve together.

[growth:顶层:12]
中：电弧和带电拔线伤害；仍需断电。
英：Arc and live unplug damage; isolate power first.

[growth:顶层:14]
中：材料内的实际切割速度。
英：Actual beam travel speed through material.

[growth:顶层:16]
中：切口半径；更少材料被烧掉，仍会切断管线。
英：Kerf radius; wastes less metal, still severs lines.

[growth:顶层:18]
中：烫伤、火焰和爆燃对无人机的伤害。
英：Scald, fire and combustion damage to the drone.

[growth:顶层:20]
中：提高可牵引吨位；高阶同时增强最大牵引力。
英：Raises the mass limit; advanced volumes also strengthen towing force.

[growth:顶层:22]
中：抓取、拖曳目标和断绳距离同步增加。
英：Acquisition, towing target and snap distance grow together.

[growth:顶层:25]
中：按 X 切换单体/范围：一次抓起半径内的多件散料，各自保持质量和碰撞；每阶件数、半径、总承重和稳定性一起提高。
英：X switches single/field: catch several loose pieces within a radius at once, each keeping its mass and collisions; each volume adds pieces, radius, total mass and steadiness.

[growth:顶层:27]
中：增加牵引阻尼并减轻无人机受到的反作用力。
英：More tether damping and less recoil on the drone.

[growth:顶层:29]
中：牵引力更大，重物拖得更快；无人机受到的拖拽更小。
英：More towing force: heavy loads move faster and drag the drone less.

[growth:顶层:31]
中：可见目标的读数距离；不透视未打开的舱室。
英：Probe visible targets farther away; no seeing through sealed rooms.

[growth:顶层:33]
中：检测耗时；首级含原精密套件，可查裂纹和设备状况。读数仍会过期。
英：Probe time; volume I includes crack and condition inspection. Readings can still go stale.

[growth:顶层:35]
中：每条软管的气体与液体回收速度。
英：Gas and liquid recovery speed for each attached hose.

[growth:顶层:37]
中：远处接口的接管距离；已接软管照常独立工作。
英：Connect more distant ports; attached hoses keep working independently.

[growth:顶层:39]
中：螺栓、锁、夹具、接头及控制件的操作速度。
英：Operate bolts, locks, clamps, connectors and controls faster.

[growth:顶层:40]
中：维修剂配方
英：Repair compound

[growth:顶层:41]
中：每份维修剂的恢复量；机库维修依旧免费。
英：Integrity restored per repair kit; bay repair remains free.

[growth:顶层:43]
中：一次扳手动作同时处理同一部件上相邻的多颗固定件。
英：One wrench pass handles several fixings on the same unit at once.

[growth:display:1]
中：同时 %d 颗
英：%d at once

[growth:reason:3]
中：需要完成 %d 单、声望 %d
英：Requires %d completed jobs, %d reputation

[growth:buy:1]
中：技能书已停用：数值卷已退款，能力来自工程成果与消耗件。
英：The skill book is withdrawn: its volumes were refunded; abilities come from achievements and parts.

# 九、港区：各区现场与面板（704 条）
# 船坞空着时飞出去的港区：中央环、动力区、拖船站、货运区、贸易港、加工区。
# 每区有业务终端（按 E）、区里的机器（轧机、货架、柜台）、接驳车。

中：蒸汽经主汽门、调节阀进高压缸、低压缸推动转子，带发电机发电。负荷一加上，转速和频率先往下掉，调节阀开大补上，几秒后回到 50 Hz。
英：Steam through the stop and governor valves drives the HP and LP casings and the generator. A load put on drops the speed and frequency until the governor valve opens to match; in a few seconds it is back at 50 Hz.

[power_station_art:hover_items:11]

英：

[power_station_art:hover_items:13]
中：把汽轮机排出的蒸汽冷成水，再由给水泵送回蒸汽发生器。它的热由散热回路带到外面的散热板排到太空。
英：Turns the turbine's exhaust back into water for the feed pumps; its heat goes by the radiator loop to the radiators outside, out into space.

[power_station_art:hover_items:14]

英：Nothing to do.

英：Drive the radiator loop: the condenser's heat out to the radiators on the booms. In vacuum heat can only be radiated away.

[power_station_art:hover_items:23]

英：Nothing to do. Outside, the redder the radiators, the more heat goes out.

英：HALL CRANE 20 t

[process_art:hover_items:2]

英：The bridge crane running under the gallery. While scrap waits in the furnace's bunker it lifts the charging basket off its stand on the left, hangs it over the furnace's side chute and lets the scrap feed in, then takes the empty basket back; on a mould change it swaps the blocks between the seat under the tap and the rack.

[process_art:hover_items:3]

英：Nothing to do: it lifts when the furnace is charged or a mould change is due.

[process_art:hover_items:4]
中：电弧炉 EAF-3
英：ARC FURNACE EAF-3

[process_art:hover_items:5]
中：把废钢熔成钢水。三根石墨电极从炉盖伸进去起弧（满功率 24 MW），钢水到出钢温度后炉体倾转，从偏心底出钢口浇进右边座上的模具，冷却后顶出成坯。
英：Melts scrap into steel: three graphite electrodes strike arcs through the roof (24 MW flat out); at tapping heat the vessel tilts and taps from its eccentric bottom hole into the mould in the seat on the right, where the pieces cool and are pushed out.

[process_art:hover_items:6]
中：在观察廊上的炉体操作台（E）打开加工队列。
英：The furnace console on the gallery (E) opens the processing queue.

[process_art:hover_items:7]
中：炉用变压器
英：FURNACE TRANSFORMER

[process_art:hover_items:8]

英：Steps the port's high voltage down to the arcs' heavy current, through the copper bus and the water-cooled cables to the electrode arms. Its fans speed up with the load.

[process_art:hover_items:10]
中：不用操作。动力区的供电决定电弧炉能开多大功率。
英：Nothing to do; the power district's supply sets how hard the furnace can burn.

[process_art:hover_items:11]
中：除尘布袋室 BH-1
英：BAGHOUSE BH-1

[process_art:hover_items:12]
中：电弧炉的烟气经炉顶烟罩和行车梁下的排烟道进来，滤袋截住粉尘，脉冲喷吹按顺序把灰抖进灰斗，引风机把净化后的气从后墙排出去。
英：The furnace's fume comes along the duct under the crane's runway; the bags catch the dust, pulses shake it down in turn into the hopper, and the fan sends the clean air out through the back wall.

[process_art:hover_items:15]
中：不用操作，跟电弧炉一起运行。
英：Nothing to do; it runs with the furnace.

[process_art:hover_items:16]
中：加热炉 + 轧机 ROLL-1
英：REHEAT FURNACE + ROLLING MILL

[process_art:hover_items:17]

英：A billet heats through in the reheating furnace; at the pass the door lifts and the pusher sends it out onto the feed table, the descaler blasts its scale off, the four-high stand rolls it into plate or sections, and it cools on the run-out on its way to the racks.

[process_art:hover_items:18]
中：在观察廊上的轧机操作台（E）选配方、开轧；钢坯从货运区自动送来。
英：At the mill console on the gallery (E) pick the recipe and roll; billets come up from the cargo district.

# 【交易与生产的引导页（data/onboarding_copy.json）】
# 出现在：“去交易行买炉料 → 等货到 → 炼钢”这类引导里每一页的标题和正文。
------------------------------------------------------------

[guidepage:M01:title]
中：钢锭从哪来
英：Where the ingots come from

[guidepage:M01:body]
中：拆船时，船壳、甲板这些废料掉进船坞熔炉，炼成钢锭。\n\n结算时把钢锭拖进“我的仓库”。F3 看有多少吨。\n\n交易行里也能买钢锭（等级 1 黑色炉料）。
英：Scrap from the hull goes into the berth furnace and comes out as ingots. Keep them at settlement and they go to the cargo warehouse (row A1); F3 shows how many tonnes. The exchange sells ingots too.

[guidepage:M02:title]
中：把钢锭送到炉口
英：Send the ingots to the furnace

[guidepage:M02:body]
中：加工区要先修好一期。\n\n在引导卡上按「送料到炉口」，或者到加工区炉台按 E，填好这一炉的吨数（0.25～2 吨），点「把仓库炉料送到炉口」。\n\n料自己送过去，大约 20 秒。在哪按都行。
英：The processing district must be mended first. Press Send the feed on the guide card, or at the furnace desk set 0.25–2 t and send it. The forklift and the rail trolley bring it in about 20 s; it travels while you fly over.

[guidepage:M03:body]
中：料到了，按「通电熔化」。温度它自己管。\n\n熔得多快，看炉台上的功率百分比，旁边写着大约多久能出钢。\n\n动力区没修时，全港只有一台旧主机，约 6 兆瓦，电弧炉功率只有 15% 左右，一炉 2 吨要十几分钟。
英：At the desk, Strike the arcs: it charges, strikes and holds the temperature by itself. How fast depends on the power it gets; the desk shows what it draws, what it is given and how long is left. With the power district unmended the port has about 6 MW, and the furnace gets 3–4 of its 24 MW: a 2 t heat takes over ten minutes.

[guidepage:M04:title]
中：电力不足怎么办
英：Short of power

[guidepage:M04:body]
中：三条路：\n\n· 买块电池顶着；\n· 在供电屏把加工区排到拆船区前面，拆船现场功率会降；\n· 修动力区：一期能发 30 兆瓦，从此够用。\n\n拆船时，船坞熔炉排在前面会把电拿走，电弧炉功率掉到 0% 就停下降温；拆完再回来，它接着熔。
英：Three ways, each with a price: buy power (five times dearer than our own), move processing ahead of salvage at the power control screen (salvage slows), or mend the power district (30 MW at stage 1). While you strip a ship the berth furnace comes first and the arc furnace may stop and cool; it carries on afterwards.

[guidepage:M05:title]
中：测温取样，再出钢
英：Sample, then tap

[guidepage:M05:body]
中：全部熔化、温度到 1640 °C 以后，炉台按「测量温度并采集样本」。等测温探头完全收回，再按「倾斜炉体，排出钢水」。\n\n超过 1710 °C 会把成品烧成低等级。装了自动测温出钢以后，这两步由机器自己做。
英：Once all is melted at 1640 °C, Take a sample; when the probe is up, Tilt and tap. Over 1710 °C downgrades the product. With the auto unit fitted, the machine does both.

[guidepage:M06:title]
中：钢坯冷却、运回仓库
英：Billets cool and go to the warehouse

[guidepage:M06:body]
中：出完钢炉子空了，温度往下掉。\n\n钢坯凉两三分钟，吊轨车运回仓库。完成时弹出“本次加工完成”，写着多少吨、放在哪一排。\n\n连续轧开着的话，钢坯一入库就直接轧成钢板。
英：After tapping the furnace is empty and draws nothing; its temperature falls, as it should. The billets cool on the line for two or three minutes and the trolley takes them home. A card says how many tonnes and which row; F3 shows them under Products. With the mill rolling on, billet is rolled into plate as soon as it comes in.

[guidepage:M07:title]
中：卖钢坯和钢板
英：Selling billet and plate

[guidepage:M07:body]
中：按 F8 打开交易行，在「出售」里选货、填数量和价格挂牌；有人买就成交，钱当场到账，货由叉车和码头送走。\n\n也可以在公司仓库（F3）点那样东西，按「去交易行挂牌卖」。\n\n完整设备不用挂牌：在公司仓库或零件架上直接出售。
英：F8 opens the exchange: under Sell, pick the goods, quantity and price; when someone buys, the cash is there at once and the goods ship. From the stores (F3), a thing's List it button does the same. Whole equipment sells straight from the stores or the parts rack.

[guidepage:M08:title]
中：拆下来的东西怎么算
英：What salvage becomes

[guidepage:M08:body]
中：船壳、甲板、龙骨这些废料进熔炉，炼成钢锭；完整设备走设备口，结算时放进“我的仓库”就进了零件架，也可以当场卖掉；危险品走危险品口。\n\n没分选的废金属要先在分选台分开、化验位定级，才能当炉料。
英：Hull, decks and keel become scrap and then ingots in the furnace; whole equipment goes in by the equipment mouth and, kept at settlement, onto the parts rack (or sold there and then); hazards use theirs. Unsorted scrap must be sorted and assayed before it is furnace feed.

============================================================
# 十二、名称：零件、舱室、设备、工具（1264 条）
# 船上每个零件、舱室的名字（图纸上、抓起来时、结算单上显示），装备与工具的名字和说明。
# 名字要准、短；说明一句话讲用途。
============================================================

[codex:cx_bolt]
中：螺栓
英：BOLT

[codex:cx_bolt_a]
中：螺栓用来固定船上的零件，可以使用维护工具拧下来。这样拆除不会损坏零件，设备也能保持完整。
英：Bolts hold parts onto the ship and can be undone with the maintenance tool. Removing a part this way does not damage it, so the equipment stays whole.

[codex:cx_bolt_b]
中：拧下最后一颗螺栓以后，零件就失去了固定。如果没有提前固定好，零件可能会带着惯性漂走。
英：Once the last bolt is out, nothing holds the part any more. If it has not been secured beforehand, it can drift away with whatever motion it had.

[codex:cx_bolt_c]
中：拆除之前，先用抓钩固定住零件，确认不会漂走以后，再拧下最后一颗螺栓。
英：Before removing it, secure the part with the grapple. Once you are sure it will not drift off, take out the last bolt.

[codex:cx_bolt_x]
中：船上常用的是 M12–M24 高强度螺栓，拧紧以后会产生预紧力，把连接的两个零件压在一起。连接处主要依靠接触面的摩擦力防止零件滑动，螺栓本身也能承受一定的拉力和剪切力。\n拆除螺栓不会损坏设备，所以能用维护工具拆下来的零件，就尽量不要使用切割工具。完整拆下来的设备可以保留全部价值，一旦切开，就只能按照废金属的价格回收。\n还要注意，失重环境下没有地板帮你接住松开的零件。一块重达 0.5 吨的金属板，即使只有 0.1 米每秒的剩余速度，经过 10 秒也会漂出去 1 米，所以拆除最后一颗螺栓之前，一定要先把零件固定好。
英：Ship bolts are mostly high-strength M12–M24. Tightening them creates a preload that presses the two joined parts together. The joint mainly relies on friction between the contact faces to stop the parts sliding, and the bolt itself can also carry some tension and shear.\nRemoving a bolt does not damage the equipment, so use the maintenance tool rather than the cutting tool wherever a part can be unbolted. Equipment removed whole keeps its full value; once it is cut, it can only be recovered at scrap-metal price.\nAlso note that in weightlessness there is no floor to catch a part you have loosened. A 0.5 t metal plate with just 0.1 m/s of residual speed drifts 1 m away in 10 seconds, so always secure the part before removing the last bolt.

[codex:cx_weld]
中：焊缝
英：WELD

[codex:cx_weld_a]
中：两块金属通过焊接连在一起，已经成为一个整体，没办法用扳手拆开。
英：Two pieces of metal are joined by welding and have become one body. A wrench cannot undo it.

[codex:cx_weld_b]
中：焊缝只能通过切割拆除，切割时如果损坏了旁边的设备，还会影响回收价值。
英：A weld can only be removed by cutting. If the cut damages the equipment beside it, the recovery value drops.

[codex:cx_weld_c]
中：沿着焊缝或者标记好的切割线慢慢切开，注意不要切到设备本体。
英：Cut slowly along the weld seam or the marked cutting line, and take care not to cut into the equipment itself.

[codex:cx_weld_x]
中：结构焊缝的强度和连接的金属差不多，所以激光需要切透整个截面，才能把两边彻底分开。\n不同材料的切割速度也不一样，普通钢板比较容易切开，装甲和厚梁需要更长时间，冰和复合材料则有各自的切割要求。\n切割时，激光会按照材料允许的速度跟随光标移动。尽量贴着切割线缓慢移动，等激光把材料完全切透以后再继续向前。如果移动得太快，激光就只能在表面留下浅浅的切痕，需要重新切割。
英：A structural weld is about as strong as the metal it joins, so the laser has to cut through the whole cross-section before the two sides come apart.\nCutting speed also varies with the material. Ordinary steel plate is fairly easy to cut, armour and heavy beams take longer, and ice and composites each have their own cutting requirements.\nWhile cutting, the laser follows the cursor at whatever speed the material allows. Move slowly along the cutting line and wait until the laser has cut right through before moving on. If you move too fast, the laser only leaves a shallow groove on the surface and you have to cut again.

[codex:cx_lock]
中：锁扣
英：LOCK

[codex:cx_lock_a]
中：锁扣是一种带有锁销的固定零件，拆卸起来比较方便，通常安装在检修盖或其他零件的背面。由于安装位置比较隐蔽，需要仔细检查才能找到。
英：A lock is a fixing part with a locking pin. It is easy to remove and is usually fitted on the back of a service cover or another part. Because it is hidden, you need to look carefully to find it.

[codex:cx_lock_b]
中：如果还有锁扣没有拆除，就直接使用抓钩拉扯零件，可能会损坏零件的连接位置，拉力过大时还会把锁扣直接扯断。
英：If a lock has not been removed and you pull the part with the grapple anyway, you may damage the part’s mounting point, and with too much pull the lock snaps outright.

[codex:cx_lock_c]
中：使用抓钩拉扯零件之前，先查看光标上的提示，确认还有多少个固定点没有拆除，全部处理完成后再进行拆卸。
英：Before pulling a part with the grapple, check the hint on the cursor to see how many fixing points are still left, and pull only after all of them are dealt with.

[codex:cx_lock_x]
中：锁扣通常安装在需要经常拆卸的地方，像检修盖、可更换的设备和货物托盘，都可能使用锁扣固定。拆卸方法和螺栓差不多，使用维护工具就可以将锁扣打开。\n如果没有提前拆除锁扣，就直接使用抓钩强行拉扯，拉力超过锁扣的承受范围后，锁扣就会断裂，零件的安装位置也可能受到损坏。\n抓钩可以强行扯断锁扣和管线，但无法扯断螺栓。如果零件还有螺栓固定，就需要先使用维护工具将螺栓拆除，才能继续拆卸零件。
英：Locks are usually fitted where parts need to be removed often. Service covers, replaceable equipment and cargo pallets may all be held by locks. They are removed much like bolts: the maintenance tool opens them.\nIf a lock has not been removed and you pull with the grapple anyway, the lock breaks once the pull exceeds what it can take, and the part’s mounting position may also be damaged.\nThe grapple can forcibly break locks and lines, but it cannot break bolts. If a part is still held by bolts, remove them with the maintenance tool first before you can continue taking the part off.

[codex:cx_clamp]
中：船坞夹具
英：BERTH CLAMP

[codex:cx_clamp_a]
中：船坞里的液压夹具，用来把整艘船固定在泊位上，防止船体在拆解过程中发生移动。
英：The hydraulic clamps in the berth hold the whole ship in its bay so the hull cannot move during dismantling.

[codex:cx_clamp_b]
中：夹具只固定着船体上的几处结构，如果提前把这些结构拆掉，船体就会失去固定，可能在泊位里发生漂移。
英：The clamps grip only a few structures on the hull. If you remove those structures too early, the hull comes loose and may drift inside the bay.

[codex:cx_clamp_c]
中：拆解船体的时候，先保留被夹具固定的结构，等其他部分都拆得差不多了，再处理这些位置。
英：When dismantling the hull, keep the structures held by the clamps until last. Deal with those positions only when most of the rest has been taken apart.

[codex:cx_clamp_x]
中：船坞夹具一般固定在龙骨或底板上，虽然船体的移动速度可能很慢，但一艘几百吨重的船就算只以 0.05 m/s 的速度撞上泊位端墙，也可能因为撞击而损坏舱内没有固定好的设备。\n蓝图上会标出夹具固定的位置，拆解进入后半段以后，记得检查剩下的船体是否还被夹具固定，确认安全以后再继续拆解。
英：Berth clamps are usually fixed to the keel or the floor plates. The hull may move slowly, but even a ship of several hundred tonnes hitting the end wall of the bay at only 0.05 m/s can damage any loose equipment inside the rooms.\nThe positions held by the clamps are marked on the blueprint. Once the job is in its second half, check whether the remaining hull is still held by the clamps, and carry on only when it is safe.

[codex:cx_hull]
中：外壳板
英：HULL PLATE

[codex:cx_hull_a]
中：外壳板是船体外侧的金属板，负责保护内部结构，同时保持舱室密封，防止舱内气体泄漏。
英：Hull plates are the metal plates on the outside of the hull. They protect the internal structure and keep the rooms sealed so that gas cannot leak out.

[codex:cx_hull_b]
中：如果舱室里面还有气压就直接切开外壳板，内部的高压气体会从切口快速喷出，可能把切开的板材和舱内没有固定好的设备一起冲出去。
英：If you cut a hull plate while a room still holds air pressure, the high-pressure gas inside shoots out through the cut and can blow the cut plate out together with any loose equipment in the room.

[codex:cx_hull_c]
中：拆除外壳板之前，先检查舱室的气压，等气压降低到 5 kPa 以下以后，再开始切割。
英：Before removing a hull plate, check the pressure in the room, and start cutting only after it has dropped below 5 kPa.

[codex:cx_hull_x]
中：一个标准大气压大约是 101 kPa，相当于每平方米承受约 10 吨的压力作用力。如果一块长 2 米、宽 1 米的外壳板承受着 100 kPa 的压差，那么整块板受到的推力就相当于 20 吨重物产生的重力。\n这种情况下直接切开外壳板，高压气体就会从切口快速喷出，气流速度甚至可能达到音速，几秒钟内就能把舱室里没有固定好的零件和碎屑冲出去。\n所以拆解之前一定要先完成泄压，确认舱室气压已经降低到 5 kPa 以下，再进行切割。
英：One standard atmosphere is about 101 kPa, which is a force of about 10 tonnes on every square metre. If a hull plate 2 m long and 1 m wide has a pressure difference of 100 kPa across it, the thrust on the whole plate equals the weight of a 20 t load.\nCutting the plate in that state lets the high-pressure gas shoot out through the cut, at speeds that can even reach the speed of sound, and within seconds it blows out the loose parts and debris in the room.\nSo always vent the pressure before dismantling, and cut only after you have confirmed the room pressure is below 5 kPa.

[codex:cx_bulkhead]
中：舱壁
英：BULKHEAD

[codex:cx_bulkhead_a]
中：舱壁是船体内部的隔墙，负责把船舱分成多个独立的密封区域，防止一个舱室发生事故以后影响到其他舱室。
英：A bulkhead is an internal wall of the hull. It divides the ship into several separate sealed areas so that an accident in one room does not affect the others.

[codex:cx_bulkhead_b]
中：如果舱壁两侧的气压不同，直接切开舱壁就会让高压一侧的气体迅速进入低压舱室，还可能把附近没有固定好的设备和零件一起冲过去。
英：If the pressure is different on the two sides of a bulkhead, cutting it lets gas from the high-pressure side rush into the low-pressure room, and it may sweep nearby loose equipment and parts along with it.

[codex:cx_bulkhead_c]
中：切割舱壁之前，先检查两侧舱室的气压，如果存在压差，就先进行泄压或者平衡两侧的气压，确认安全以后再开始拆除。
英：Before cutting a bulkhead, check the pressure in the rooms on both sides. If there is a pressure difference, vent or equalise the two sides first, and start removal only when it is safe.

[codex:cx_bulkhead_x]
中：舱壁可以把不同舱室隔开，这样即使其中一个舱室发生泄漏，也不会直接影响整艘船。拆解的时候也要注意，即使你已经把一个舱室的气体排空，隔壁的舱室也可能还保持着原来的气压。\n真正需要注意的是两侧的压差。如果两个舱室都是 100 kPa，切开中间的舱壁一般不会因为气压差而发生喷气，但两个舱室也会从此连通。\n如果之后还需要泄压，就得把连通的舱室一起处理，所以切割之前最好确认两侧的气压，避免增加后面的拆解工作。
英：Bulkheads separate the rooms, so even if one room leaks, the whole ship is not directly affected. Remember this when dismantling too: even after you have emptied one room of gas, the next room may still hold its original pressure.\nWhat really matters is the pressure difference between the two sides. If both rooms are at 100 kPa, cutting the bulkhead between them generally will not cause a blast of gas, but the two rooms become connected from then on.\nIf you still need to vent afterwards, you have to treat the connected rooms together, so it is best to check the pressure on both sides before cutting, to avoid adding to the dismantling work later.

[codex:cx_frame]
中：承力结构
英：LOAD-BEARING FRAME

[codex:cx_frame_a]
中：船体内部的龙骨、肋骨、纵梁和支撑杆都属于承力结构，它们组成了船体的主要骨架，负责支撑和固定其他设备。
英：The keel, ribs, longitudinals and struts inside the hull are all load-bearing structures. Together they form the main skeleton of the hull and support and hold the other equipment.

[codex:cx_frame_b]
中：有些承力结构还在支撑其他设备，如果直接把它们切断，原本固定好的设备和船体结构就可能突然移动，甚至发生弹开、扭曲或断裂的情况。
英：Some load-bearing structures are still supporting other equipment. If you cut them directly, equipment and hull structure that were held firmly may suddenly move, and may even spring off, twist or break.

[codex:cx_frame_c]
中：拆解之前，先检查承力结构连接着哪些设备，把它支撑的重量卸下来，确认没有其他结构需要依靠它固定以后，再进行切割。
英：Before dismantling, check what equipment the load-bearing structure is connected to. Take off the weight it supports, make sure no other structure depends on it, and then cut.

[codex:cx_frame_x]
中：虽然太空里没有正常的重力载荷，但船体内部仍然存在预紧力、惯性作用，以及船坞夹具传递过来的外力。有些梁甚至需要同时固定两台几十吨重的设备，直接切断就可能让这些设备失去支撑。\n承力结构本身通常也比较重，有些会超过抓钩 3 t 的承重上限，所以拆解时需要沿着标记好的切割线分成几段，再分别运走。
英：There is no normal gravity load in space, but the hull still has preload, inertia, and external forces passed on by the berth clamps. Some beams even hold two machines weighing tens of tonnes at once, and cutting them straight through can leave those machines unsupported.\nLoad-bearing structures are usually heavy themselves, and some exceed the grapple’s 3 t load limit, so they must be cut into several sections along the marked cutting lines and carried away one by one.

[codex:cx_door]
中：舱门与货舱门
英：DOORS AND HATCHES

[codex:cx_door_a]
中：船上的舱门用来连接不同的舱室，大部分都需要通过船体总线供电，才能正常开启和关闭。
英：Doors on the ship connect the different rooms. Most of them need power from the ship’s bus to open and close properly.

[codex:cx_door_b]
中：如果舱室里面还有气压，直接打开舱门可能会让舱门受到气压冲击，严重时甚至会被直接冲飞。有些舱门还需要电力才能开启，一旦提前切断电源，就可能再也无法正常打开。
英：If a room still holds air pressure, opening its door may subject the door to a pressure shock, and in bad cases it is blown off outright. Some doors also need power to open, so if you cut the power too early, they may never open normally again.

[codex:cx_door_c]
中：打开舱门之前，先确认舱室里面的气压已经排空。需要使用电力开启的舱门，最好在切断船体电源之前就打开。
英：Before opening a door, make sure the pressure in the room has been vented. For doors that need power to open, it is best to open them before cutting the ship’s power.

[codex:cx_door_x]
中：一扇面积为 2 m² 的舱门，如果两侧存在 60 kPa 的气压差，舱门就会承受相当于约 12 吨重物的压力。这种情况下，驱动电机可能无法正常开启舱门，一旦解除门锁，舱门甚至可能被气压直接冲飞。\n大型货舱门一般需要电机或者液压系统驱动，如果提前拆除了供电电池，后面可能就只能使用切割工具强行打开。不过船坞也提供了外部供电插座，即使已经切断船体电源，也可以通过外部供电临时启动舱门。
英：If a 2 m² door has a pressure difference of 60 kPa across it, the door bears a force equal to about 12 tonnes. In that case the drive motor may not be able to open the door, and once the latch is released, the door may even be blown off by the pressure.\nLarge cargo doors usually need a motor or a hydraulic system to drive them. If you remove the power battery too early, the only way left may be to force them open with the cutting tool. However, the berth also provides an external power socket, so even after the ship’s power is cut, a door can be started temporarily from external power.

[codex:cx_rail]
中：轨道
英：RAIL

[codex:cx_rail_a]
中：安装在船体内部的导轨，主要用来运输货物，或者让吊车和滑架沿着固定的路线移动。
英：A guide rail installed inside the hull, used mainly to carry cargo, or to let cranes and cradles move along a fixed route.

[codex:cx_rail_b]
中：如果轨道上还有货物或者设备，直接切断轨道可能会让它们失去支撑，导致设备损坏，严重时还可能直接掉落。
英：If a rail still carries cargo or equipment, cutting it directly may leave them unsupported, damaging the equipment, and in bad cases they may drop.

[codex:cx_rail_c]
中：拆除轨道之前，先把上面的货物和设备全部移走，确认轨道上没有剩余载荷以后，再开始拆解。
英：Before removing a rail, move all the cargo and equipment off it, and start dismantling only once you have confirmed no load is left on the rail.

[codex:cx_rail_x]
中：轨道通常由多个支座共同承受重量，这样就能把货物的重量分散到不同位置。如果直接切断其中一段，原本由整条轨道承担的部分重量就会集中到断口附近的支座上，一旦超过它们的承重能力，就可能发生变形或者断裂。\n如果货物通过锁轨固定在轨道上，需要先沿着轨道把货物运出去，不能直接使用扳手拆除固定装置。具体操作可以查看对应船型的锁轨说明。
英：A rail is usually carried by several mounts, which spread the weight of the cargo over different positions. If you cut one section, part of the weight the whole rail was bearing is concentrated on the mounts next to the break, and if it exceeds what they can take, they may deform or break.\nIf cargo is fixed to the rail by a rail lock, it has to be driven out along the rail first, and the fixing cannot simply be removed with the wrench. For the exact procedure, see the rail-lock entry for the ship type concerned.

[codex:cx_film]
中：薄膜
英：FILM

[codex:cx_film_a]
中：一种只有几微米厚的聚酰亚胺薄膜，主要用在飞船的帆面、气囊外壁和遮阳膜上。
英：A polyimide film only a few microns thick, used mainly for sail panels, gasbag walls and sunshades on ships.

[codex:cx_film_b]
中：薄膜在绷紧的时候很容易被撕裂，如果直接使用切割工具，切口可能会在张力作用下迅速扩大，最后导致整片薄膜破裂。
英：Film tears easily when taut. If you use the cutting tool directly, the cut may spread rapidly under tension and eventually tear the whole film.

[codex:cx_film_c]
中：拆解薄膜之前，先解除固定装置，让薄膜完全松弛下来，再把整片薄膜拆下来。
英：Before dismantling film, release the fixings so the film goes completely slack, and then take the whole film off.

[codex:cx_film_x]
中：这种薄膜的抗撕裂强度只有每米几牛，而绷紧的帆面每米可能承受几十牛的张力。如果直接使用激光切割，切口就可能成为裂纹的起点，在张力作用下，裂纹甚至能以接近声速的速度扩展。\n薄膜本身非常轻，基本不会阻挡物体碰撞，也不会影响正常的移动路径，不过它仍然能够遮挡光线，而且表面容易积累静电，拆解的时候也需要注意。
英：The tear strength of this film is only a few newtons per metre, while a taut sail panel may carry tens of newtons per metre of tension. If you cut with the laser directly, the cut can become the start of a crack, and under tension the crack can spread at close to the speed of sound.\nThe film itself is very light. It basically does not block collisions or affect normal movement paths, but it can still block light and easily builds up static charge, so take care of this when dismantling.

[codex:cx_cable]
中：电缆
英：CABLE

[codex:cx_cable_a]
中：连接船体电网和各种设备的供电电缆，两端都有可以拆卸的接头，用来给设备提供电力。
英：A power cable that connects the ship’s grid to various equipment. Both ends have removable plugs, which supply power to the equipment.

[codex:cx_cable_b]
中：如果电缆还带着电，直接拔掉接头或者使用切割工具，就可能产生电弧，损坏附近的设备，甚至伤到正在作业的无人机。
英：If a cable is still live, pulling the plug or using the cutting tool directly can produce an arc, damaging nearby equipment and even hurting the drone at work.

[codex:cx_cable_c]
中：拆卸电缆之前，先用验电工具确认电压已经降到 0 V，再拔掉两端的接头，之后就可以正常搬走设备了。
英：Before removing a cable, check with the voltage tester that the voltage has dropped to 0 V, then unplug the connectors at both ends. After that the equipment can be carried away as normal.

[codex:cx_cable_x]
中：30 V 以下的电压通常不会对无人机造成伤害，不过船上的供电总线一般在 28–400 V 之间，部分电容组的电压甚至能达到上千伏，拆卸之前一定要确认电路已经断电。\n直流电弧没有交流电那种周期性的电流过零点，一旦产生电弧，就可能持续放电，直到电流被切断。\n搬运设备的时候也要注意，如果连接设备的电缆还没有拔掉，抓钩就可能无法正常拖动设备。继续增加拉力的话，还可能直接把电缆接头扯坏。
英：Voltages below 30 V generally do not harm the drone, but the ship’s power buses are usually between 28 and 400 V, and some capacitor banks even reach over a thousand volts, so always confirm the circuit is dead before removal.\nA DC arc has no periodic current zero crossing like AC, so once an arc forms, it can keep discharging until the current is cut off.\nAlso take care when moving equipment: if the cable connecting the equipment has not been unplugged, the grapple may be unable to drag the equipment properly. If you keep increasing the pull, it may even tear the cable plug off.

[codex:cx_pipe_line]
中：管线
英：PIPE

[codex:cx_pipe_line_a]
中：船上的管线主要用来输送冷却液、燃料和气体，两端都有可以拆卸的接头，方便连接设备或者其他管路。
英：Lines on the ship mainly carry coolant, fuel and gas. Both ends have removable couplings so they can be connected to equipment or to other pipes.

[codex:cx_pipe_line_b]
中：如果管线里面还残留着液体或者气体，直接切开就可能造成泄漏。燃料泄漏以后可能形成易燃的气体云团，而温度过高的管线还可能损坏正在作业的无人机。
英：If a line still holds liquid or gas, cutting it directly may cause a leak. Leaked fuel can form a flammable gas cloud, and an over-hot line can damage the drone at work.

[codex:cx_pipe_line_c]
中：拆卸管线之前，先通过低处的排放口把内部的液体抽干净，再检查管线温度，确认已经冷却到安全范围以后，才能拔掉接头或者进行切割。
英：Before removing a line, draw the contents off through the low-point drain, then check the line temperature, and uncouple or cut it only after confirming it has cooled to a safe range.

[codex:cx_pipe_line_x]
中：关闭阀门以后，管线里面可能仍然残留着液体，尤其是两个阀门之间的管段，即使两端都已经关闭，中间的液体也不会自动排出去，所以拆卸之前还需要单独排空。\n在失重环境下，泄漏的液体不会流到地面，而是会形成漂浮的液团。如果这些液体接触到带电设备，就可能造成短路。\n管线的温度如果超过 60°C，还可能损坏负责抓取和搬运的无人机，所以拆卸之前要先测量温度，确认安全以后再继续操作。
英：After a valve is closed, the line may still hold liquid. This is especially so for the length between two valves: even with both ends closed, the liquid in the middle does not drain by itself, so it needs to be drained separately before removal.\nIn weightlessness, leaked liquid does not run to the floor but forms floating globules. If these touch live equipment, they can cause a short circuit.\nIf a line is hotter than 60°C, it may also damage the drone that grips and moves it, so measure the temperature before removal and continue only when it is safe.

[codex:cx_cut_line]
中：标线（分段线）
英：MARKED CUT LINE

[codex:cx_cut_line_a]
中：一些大型设备的重量超过了抓钩的搬运上限，所以设备表面会提前标出可以安全切割的位置，方便拆解时将设备分成几个部分。
英：Some large equipment weighs more than the grapple can carry, so safe cutting positions are marked on its surface in advance, to let you split the equipment into several parts when dismantling.

[codex:cx_cut_line_b]
中：如果随便选择位置切割，很容易损坏设备内部的零件，导致回收价值大幅下降。但如果不进行分段，整台设备又可能因为重量太大，无法使用抓钩搬运。
英：If you pick a position at random to cut, it is easy to damage the parts inside the equipment, and the recovery value drops sharply. But if you do not split it into sections, the whole piece may be too heavy for the grapple to move.

[codex:cx_cut_line_c]
中：按照设备表面的标线进行切割，把设备分成几个部分，并确认每个部分的重量都没有超过抓钩的搬运上限。
英：Cut along the marked lines on the equipment surface to split it into several parts, and confirm that the weight of each part does not exceed the grapple’s carrying limit.

[codex:cx_cut_line_x]
中：抓钩最初的搬运上限是 3 吨，升级以后可以提高到 5 吨。对于重量超过这个上限的设备，设计时一般都会预留能够安全分段的位置，通常位于连接法兰、模块接缝或者没有管线经过的地方。\n只要沿着标线进行切割，拆下来的每个部分仍然可以按照设备部件回收，保留原有的回收价值。如果随意切割，就可能切坏内部的线圈绕组、管束或者电路板，到时候损坏的部分就只能按照废金属的价格回收了。
英：The grapple’s original carrying limit is 3 t, and after an upgrade it rises to 5 t. For equipment heavier than this limit, safe splitting positions are normally built in at the design stage, usually at connecting flanges, module seams, or places no line passes through.\nAs long as you cut along the marked lines, each part removed can still be recovered as equipment parts and keep its original recovery value. If you cut at random, you may ruin internal coil windings, tube bundles or circuit boards, and the damaged parts can then only be recovered at scrap-metal price.

[codex:cx_glass]
中：玻璃与舷窗
英：GLASS AND WINDOWS

[codex:cx_glass_a]
中：船上的舷窗和精密玻璃罩板，一般通过压条和螺栓固定在船体上，拆解时可以将整块玻璃完整取下来。
英：Ship windows and precision glass cover plates are usually held to the hull by clamping strips and bolts, and can be taken off whole when dismantling.

[codex:cx_glass_b]
中：如果玻璃背后还有气压，直接拆除固定螺栓可能会让整块玻璃被气压推出去，造成玻璃破碎。在玻璃附近使用激光切割工具，也可能因为温度变化过大而导致玻璃开裂。
英：If there is still pressure behind the glass, removing the fixing bolts directly may let the pressure push the whole pane out and shatter it. Using the laser cutting tool near the glass may also crack it through too large a temperature change.

[codex:cx_glass_c]
中：拆除玻璃之前，先确认舱室已经完成泄压，切割周围结构的时候也要和玻璃保持距离。等附近的拆解工作完成以后，再拧下固定压条的螺栓，把整块玻璃取出来。
英：Before removing glass, make sure the room has been vented, and keep your distance from the glass when cutting the surrounding structure. Once the nearby dismantling is finished, undo the bolts of the clamping strips and take the whole pane out.

[codex:cx_glass_x]
中：玻璃能够承受较大的压力，但受到拉力或者局部温度变化时就容易出现裂纹，表面原本存在的划痕也会增加破裂的风险。\n如果在距离玻璃 0.7 m 以内使用激光切割工具，切割产生的热辐射和高温飞溅物可能让玻璃两侧的温差超过 100°C，产生的热应力足以让玻璃开裂。\n完整的光学玻璃可以卖出很高的价格，一旦发生破碎，基本就没有多少回收价值了。
英：Glass can bear high compression, but it cracks easily under tension or local temperature change, and scratches already on the surface increase the risk of breaking.\nIf you use the laser cutting tool within 0.7 m of the glass, the thermal radiation and hot spatter from cutting can put a temperature difference of over 100°C between the two faces, and the resulting thermal stress is enough to crack the glass.\nIntact optical glass sells for a high price, but once it breaks, there is almost no recovery value left.

[codex:cx_armor]
中：装甲
英：ARMOUR

[codex:cx_armor_a]
中：船体外部安装的厚重防护板，包括分层装甲、隔热瓦和辐射屏蔽层，主要用来保护船体和内部设备。
英：Thick protective plates on the outside of the hull, including layered armour, heat tiles and radiation shielding, used mainly to protect the hull and the equipment inside.

[codex:cx_armor_b]
中：装甲的厚度和硬度都比较高，使用切割工具拆除时会花费很长时间，而且切割产生的热量容易集中在切口附近。如果周围安装了其他设备，持续产生的高温就可能把这些设备损坏。
英：Armour is thick and hard, so removing it with the cutting tool takes a long time, and the heat from cutting tends to collect around the cut. If other equipment is installed around it, the continuing high temperature may damage that equipment.

[codex:cx_armor_c]
中：拆解装甲之前，先检查有没有固定螺栓，能够直接拆卸的就尽量不要使用切割工具。如果必须进行切割，最好选择远离其他设备的位置，避免高温造成额外损失。
英：Before dismantling armour, check for fixing bolts, and wherever it can be unbolted, avoid the cutting tool. If you must cut, choose a position away from other equipment to avoid extra losses from the heat.

[codex:cx_armor_x]
中：装甲主要依靠自身的厚度和硬度来吸收冲击能量，所以切割起来会比较困难，有些装甲的切割速度甚至只有普通钢板的十分之一。\n部分船体还安装了钨或氢化锂制成的辐射屏蔽层，这些材料主要用来阻挡辐射，通常重量较大，而且比较容易发生脆裂。\n船体外部的装甲条一般通过螺栓固定，如果能够找到固定位置，直接拆除螺栓通常会比切穿整块装甲快得多，也能减少切割过程中产生的热量。
英：Armour absorbs impact energy mainly through its own thickness and hardness, so it is hard to cut, and some armour cuts at only a tenth of the speed of ordinary steel plate.\nSome hulls also carry radiation shielding made of tungsten or lithium hydride. These materials mainly block radiation, are usually heavy, and are fairly prone to brittle cracking.\nArmour strips on the outside of the hull are generally held by bolts. If you can find the fixing positions, removing the bolts is usually much faster than cutting through the whole armour plate, and also reduces the heat produced by cutting.

[codex:cx_insul]
中：隔热层
英：INSULATION

[codex:cx_insul_a]
中：船体内部用来隔绝热量的结构，有些是在两层金属蒙皮之间填充隔热泡沫，有些则使用多层隔热毯，主要用来保持冷藏舱和低温储罐的温度。
英：Structures inside the hull that block heat. Some have insulating foam filled between two metal skins, others use multilayer insulation blankets. They mainly keep cold rooms and cryogenic tanks at temperature.

[codex:cx_insul_b]
中：如果提前拆除了隔热层，外部的热量就会更快进入冷藏舱和低温储罐，导致内部温度上升。低温储罐里的液体还可能因为受热而加快蒸发，让罐内压力不断增加。
英：If the insulation is removed too early, heat from outside reaches cold rooms and cryogenic tanks faster, and the temperature inside rises. The liquid in a cryogenic tank may also boil off faster when heated, so the pressure in the tank keeps rising.

[codex:cx_insul_c]
中：拆解隔热层之前，先确认附近的冷藏设备和低温储罐已经处理完成。如果里面还有需要保持低温的货物，就暂时保留隔热层，等货物转移完成以后再拆除。
英：Before dismantling insulation, make sure the nearby refrigeration equipment and cryogenic tanks have been dealt with. If there is still cargo inside that must be kept cold, leave the insulation in place for now and remove it after the cargo has been moved.

[codex:cx_insul_x]
中：真空环境中的多层隔热毯通常由几十层镀铝薄膜组成，可以通过反射热辐射来减少热量传递。隔热层保持完整时，每平方米的漏热量通常只有零点几瓦。\n如果在隔热毯上划开一道缺口，受损区域的漏热量就可能增加到原来的十倍以上，导致内部温度上升得更快。\n对于装有低温货物的储罐来说，隔热层的完整程度会直接影响货物的升温和蒸发速度，所以最好等低温货物全部处理完成以后，再开始拆除隔热结构。
英：In vacuum, a multilayer insulation blanket is usually made of dozens of layers of aluminised film, which reduces heat transfer by reflecting thermal radiation. While the insulation is intact, the heat leak is usually only a fraction of a watt per square metre.\nIf you slit the blanket, the heat leak in the damaged area may rise to more than ten times the original, and the temperature inside rises faster.\nFor a tank holding cryogenic cargo, how intact the insulation is directly affects how fast the cargo warms and boils off, so it is best to start removing insulation only after all the cryogenic cargo has been dealt with.

[codex:cx_cover]
中：检修盖与护板
英：SERVICE COVERS

[codex:cx_cover_a]
中：船体上用来保护设备或遮挡固定位置的盖板，有些设备和螺栓安装在盖板后面，需要拆除盖板以后才能接触到。
英：Panels on the hull that protect equipment or hide fixing points. Some equipment and bolts sit behind a cover and can only be reached after it is removed.

[codex:cx_cover_b]
中：有些固定螺栓藏在盖板后面，从外面看不到，也无法直接拆卸。如果没有注意到这些位置，就可能漏掉固定螺栓，导致后面的设备无法正常拆除。
英：Some fixing bolts are hidden behind covers, so they cannot be seen from outside and cannot be removed directly. If you do not notice these positions, you may miss a fixing bolt, and then the equipment behind cannot be removed properly.

[codex:cx_cover_c]
中：遇到检修盖或者护板时，可以先把盖板拆下来，再检查后面有没有隐藏的螺栓或其他固定位置，确认以后再继续拆解设备。
英：When you meet a service cover or guard plate, take the cover off first, then check behind it for hidden bolts or other fixing points, and carry on dismantling the equipment only after confirming.

[codex:cx_cover_x]
中：工程蓝图通常会标出盖板后面的设备和固定位置，不过图纸记录的是船体设计时的结构，实际拆解的船只可能经过维修或者改装，所以有些位置会和图纸不一样。\n被盖板挡住的固定点不会在光标上显示，也无法直接操作。只有拆除盖板以后，才能找到并拆卸后面的固定螺栓。
英：Engineering blueprints usually mark the equipment and fixing points behind a cover. However, the drawing records the structure at the time of design, and a ship being dismantled may have been repaired or modified, so some positions may differ from the drawing.\nFixing points hidden behind a cover are not shown on the cursor and cannot be operated directly. Only after the cover is removed can you find and undo the fixing bolts behind it.

[codex:cx_port]
中：接口（测压口 / 回收口）
英：PORT

[codex:cx_port_a]
中：船体上用来连接测量工具或者回收软管的接口，一般安装在舱室、储罐或者管路上，可以通过这些接口检查内部压力，也可以把里面的气体和液体回收出来。
英：A connection on the hull for the measuring tool or the recovery hose, usually fitted on a room, tank or line. Through it you can check the internal pressure, and recover the gas and liquid inside.

[codex:cx_port_b]
中：测压口只能测量与它连通的那段管路，如果中间的阀门处于关闭状态，就算压力读数显示为零，也不能说明舱室或者储罐里面已经没有气压。
英：A pressure port can only measure the section of line it is connected to. If a valve in between is shut, a pressure reading of zero does not mean the room or tank has no pressure.

[codex:cx_port_c]
中：使用接口之前，先打开工程图，检查接口连接到了哪个舱室或者储罐，再确认中间的阀门已经打开，避免测量到错误的压力数据。
英：Before using a port, open the engineering drawing and check which room or tank it is connected to, then confirm the valves in between are open, so you do not read the wrong pressure.

[codex:cx_port_x]
中：测压口后面通常连接着一小段管路和阀门，如果阀门处于关闭状态，测量工具只能读取这段短管里面的压力，即使显示为零，阀门另一侧也可能还有大量高压气体。\n回收口则可以连接软管，通过船坞的回收泵把船内的气体或者液体抽出来，其中回收的气体会按照对应价值计入合同收益。\n回收速度主要取决于接口的口径和回收泵的工作能力，如果接口太小，就算使用更大的回收泵，也很难明显提高回收速度。
英：Behind a pressure port there is usually a short pipe and a valve. If the valve is shut, the measuring tool can only read the pressure in that short pipe, and even if it shows zero, there may still be a lot of high-pressure gas on the other side of the valve.\nA recovery port can take a hose, and the berth’s recovery pump draws the gas or liquid out of the ship. The gas recovered is counted into the contract income at its corresponding value.\nRecovery speed mainly depends on the port’s bore and the recovery pump’s capacity. If the port is too small, even a larger recovery pump will hardly raise the recovery speed.

[codex:cx_valve]
中：阀门
英：VALVE

[codex:cx_valve_a]
中：安装在船体管路上的手动阀门，可以控制管路的开启和关闭，也能用来切断两个舱室或容器之间的连接。
英：A manual valve fitted on the ship’s lines. It opens and closes the line, and can also cut the connection between two rooms or containers.

[codex:cx_valve_b]
中：关闭的阀门会把管路隔成不同的部分，如果只测量其中一段，就可能误以为整条管路都已经排空。有些阀门还可能因为故障而卡住，导致后面无法正常连接软管。
英：A closed valve splits a line into separate parts. If you measure only one section, you may wrongly think the whole line has been emptied. Some valves may also jam through a fault, so a hose cannot be connected properly afterwards.

[codex:cx_valve_c]
中：可以使用维护工具开启或关闭阀门。如果遇到卡死的阀门，就先使用扳手把它拧开，再继续处理管路。
英：You can open or close a valve with the maintenance tool. If a valve is jammed, free it with the wrench first, then carry on with the line.

[codex:cx_valve_x]
中：阀门可以把整套管路分成几个独立的部分，每一段管路里面的压力和液位都可能不同，所以其中一段已经排空，并不代表其他部分也已经安全。\n检查管路的时候，可以按照工程图找到对应接口，再沿着管路逐段检查。每经过一个阀门，都要确认它当前处于开启还是关闭状态，避免遗漏仍然存在压力或残留液体的管路。
英：Valves can divide a whole line system into several independent parts, and the pressure and liquid level in each section may differ, so one section being empty does not mean the other parts are safe.\nWhen checking lines, follow the engineering drawing to find the matching port, then check the line section by section. At every valve, confirm whether it is currently open or shut, so you do not miss a line that still holds pressure or leftover liquid.

[codex:cx_switch]
中：开关
英：SWITCH

[codex:cx_switch_a]
中：船上的开关可以用来切断电路、控制设备待发状态，也可以操作刹车和机械锁等装置。不同开关控制的设备和功能也会有所区别。
英：Switches on the ship can cut a circuit, set equipment to standby, and operate devices such as brakes and mechanical locks. Different switches control different equipment and functions.

[codex:cx_switch_b]
中：关闭一个开关，通常只能切断它所控制的那条线路。如果船上还有备用电池、独立电源或者储能设备，其他线路就可能仍然带电，直接拆解会有危险。
英：Turning off one switch usually cuts only the line it controls. If the ship also has backup batteries, independent supplies or energy storage, other lines may still be live, and dismantling them directly is dangerous.

[codex:cx_switch_c]
中：关闭开关以后，先使用测量工具检查对应线路的电压，确认电压已经降到 0 V，再继续拆解相关设备。
英：After turning a switch off, use the measuring tool to check the voltage of the line concerned, confirm it has dropped to 0 V, and only then continue dismantling the related equipment.

[codex:cx_switch_x]
中：设备关闭以后，不代表内部已经完全断电。主开关一般只能切断主电源，备用电池和独立供电线路仍然可能带电，电容组也可能保留之前储存的电能。\n部分设备还需要按照指定顺序操作开关，比如先完成冷却再解除刹车，或者先释放内部压力再拔出锁销。如果操作顺序错误，可能会导致设备损坏，甚至引发危险，所以拆解之前最好先确认每个开关的作用。
英：Equipment being switched off does not mean it is completely dead inside. The main switch can usually cut only the main supply, while backup batteries and independent supply lines may still be live, and capacitor banks may still hold the energy stored earlier.\nSome equipment also needs its switches operated in a set order, such as finishing cooling before releasing the brake, or releasing internal pressure before pulling the locking pin. A wrong order may damage the equipment or even cause danger, so it is best to confirm what each switch does before dismantling.

[codex:cx_test]
中：验电点
英：TEST POINT

[codex:cx_test_a]
中：船体电网上专门用来测量电压的位置，可以使用测量工具读取对应线路的电压，检查这部分电路是否已经断电。
英：A place on the ship’s grid made for measuring voltage. You can use the measuring tool to read the voltage of that line and check whether this part of the circuit has been de-energised.

[codex:cx_test_b]
中：验电点只能显示它所在那段线路的电压，其他支路可能连接着独立电源，所以一个位置显示 0 V，并不代表整艘船的电路都已经断电。
英：A test point shows only the voltage of the section of line it is on. Other branches may be connected to independent supplies, so 0 V at one position does not mean the circuits of the whole ship are dead.

[codex:cx_test_c]
中：先切断相关线路的电源，再释放电容等设备中储存的电能，完成这些操作以后，重新使用测量工具检查电压，确认读数为 0 V 再开始拆解。
英：First cut the power to the lines concerned, then release the energy stored in capacitors and similar equipment. After that, check the voltage with the measuring tool again, and start dismantling only when the reading is 0 V.

[codex:cx_test_x]
中：验电点显示的是测量时的电压，如果测量完成以后又操作了其他开关，线路的供电状态就可能发生变化，之前的读数也就不能继续作为判断依据。\n比较安全的操作顺序是先断开电源，再释放储存的电能，随后进行验电。一定要在前两项操作完成以后重新测量，如果中途修改了开关状态，也需要再次确认电压，避免根据之前的读数进行拆解。
英：A test point shows the voltage at the time of measurement. If you operate other switches after measuring, the supply state of the line may change, and the earlier reading can no longer be relied on.\nThe safer order is to isolate the power first, then release the stored energy, and then test. Always measure again after the first two steps are done, and if you change any switch state in between, confirm the voltage again, so you never dismantle on the strength of an earlier reading.

[codex:cx_probe]
中：探头
英：PROBE

[codex:cx_probe_a]
中：安装在船体和各种设备上的传感器，可以用来测量温度、液位、辐射剂量、张力和转速等数据，帮助你了解设备当前的运行状态。
英：Sensors fitted on the hull and various equipment. They can measure data such as temperature, level, radiation dose, tension and rotation speed, and help you see the equipment’s current state.

[codex:cx_probe_b]
中：有些设备即使已经停止运行，内部也可能残留压力、热量或者其他危险。如果没有提前查看探头的读数，就很难判断设备是否已经达到安全的拆解条件。
英：Some equipment may still hold pressure, heat or other dangers inside even after it has stopped running. If you have not read the probe beforehand, it is hard to tell whether the equipment has reached a safe condition for dismantling.

[codex:cx_probe_c]
中：开始拆解之前，先使用测量工具查看相关探头的读数，确认设备目前的状态。完成操作以后再测量一次，检查数值有没有达到安全范围。
英：Before dismantling, read the relevant probes with the measuring tool to confirm the equipment’s current state. After the work, measure again and check whether the values have reached the safe range.

[codex:cx_probe_x]
中：船上的很多特殊设备都有对应的测量探头，包括线圈的储存能量、飞轮的转速、钻杆的扭矩、储罐的压力、舱室内的氧气浓度、辐射剂量率和炉芯温度等。\n这些读数可以帮助你判断设备是否已经达到安全状态。如果数值还没有降到要求的范围，就说明可能还需要继续等待，或者检查有没有遗漏的操作。拆解之前多检查一次，通常能避免后面出现不必要的事故。
英：Many special pieces of equipment on the ship have matching measuring probes, including a coil’s stored energy, a flywheel’s rotation speed, a drill string’s torque, a tank’s pressure, the oxygen concentration in a room, radiation dose rate and a furnace core’s temperature.\nThese readings help you judge whether the equipment has reached a safe state. If a value has not yet dropped to the required range, you may need to keep waiting, or check for a step you missed. Checking once more before dismantling usually avoids needless accidents later.

[codex:cx_socket]
中：外部插座
英：EXTERNAL SOCKET

[codex:cx_socket_a]
中：船坞外部面板上的连接接口，可以为船体提供临时电力、冷却系统和接地线路，方便在拆解过程中继续使用部分设备。
英：Connection ports on the berth’s external panel. They can supply the hull with temporary power, a cooling system and an earthing line, so that some equipment can keep running during dismantling.

[codex:cx_socket_b]
中：有些设备在切断船体电源以后，仍然需要电力或者冷却才能安全关闭。如果提前断开电源，又没有接好船坞提供的外部接口，就可能导致这些设备无法正常工作。
英：Some equipment still needs power or cooling to shut down safely after the ship’s power is cut. If you cut the power too early without connecting the berth’s external ports, this equipment may fail to work properly.

[codex:cx_socket_c]
中：切断船体电源之前，先找到船坞外部面板上的对应插座，把需要的供电、供冷或者接地线路连接好，再继续拆解。
英：Before cutting the ship’s power, find the matching sockets on the berth’s external panel, connect the power, cooling or earthing lines you need, and then continue dismantling.

[codex:cx_socket_x]
中：船坞的外部供电可以让舱门、水泵和制冷机在船体断电以后继续工作，这样就不用担心拆除电池后，还有设备需要通电才能完成操作。\n外部供冷可以让冷链货舱继续保持低温，避免货物因为温度升高而损坏。接地线路则可以把帆面等大面积结构上积累的静电导走，减少拆解时发生静电放电的风险。\n这些接口都安装在船坞的外部面板上，需要完成连接以后才能发挥作用。
英：The berth’s external power lets doors, pumps and chillers keep working after the ship’s power is cut, so you need not worry that some equipment still needs power to finish its operation after the battery is removed.\nExternal cooling lets a cold-chain hold stay cold, so cargo is not spoiled by a temperature rise. The earthing line can conduct away the static charge built up on large structures such as sail panels, reducing the risk of electrostatic discharge during dismantling.\nThese ports are all fitted on the berth’s external panel and only take effect once they are connected.

[codex:cx_discharge]
中：放电点
英：DISCHARGE POINT

[codex:cx_discharge_a]
中：安装在设备附近接线盒上的放电开关，可以让电容组通过电阻缓慢放电，把内部储存的电能释放掉。
英：A discharge switch fitted on a junction box near the equipment. It lets a capacitor bank discharge slowly through a resistor and releases the energy stored inside.

[codex:cx_discharge_b]
中：电容即使已经断开电源，内部也可能储存着大量电能。如果没有完成放电就直接拆解，接触带电部件时可能产生高压电弧，造成严重伤害。
英：Even when disconnected from the power, a capacitor may still store a great deal of energy. If you dismantle it before discharging, touching a live part can produce a high-voltage arc and cause serious injury.

[codex:cx_discharge_c]
中：拆解电容组之前，先找到附近接线盒上的放电开关，启动放电程序。等电量显示归零以后，还需要使用检测工具确认没有残余电压，再开始拆解。
英：Before dismantling a capacitor bank, find the discharge switch on the nearby junction box and start the discharge. When the charge reading falls to zero, also check with the test tool that no residual voltage is left, and only then start dismantling.

[codex:cx_discharge_x]
中：电容可以在断电以后继续储存电能，储存的能量可以通过公式 E = ½CV² 计算。一组容量为 0.1 F 的电容，如果电压达到 400 V，内部就储存着 8 kJ 的能量，即使已经切断电源，也可能在接触时产生危险的电弧。\n通过电阻放电时，电容的电压会按照 e^(−t/RC) 的规律逐渐降低，大约经过五个时间常数以后，电压就会降到初始值的 1% 以下。\n放电开关一般安装在设备附近的接线盒上，完成放电以后还要检查剩余电压，确认安全才能继续拆解。
英：A capacitor can keep its stored energy after the power is cut, and the stored energy can be calculated with the formula E = ½CV². A bank of 0.1 F at 400 V stores 8 kJ of energy, and even after the supply is cut, touching it can produce a dangerous arc.\nWhen discharging through a resistor, the capacitor’s voltage falls gradually following e^(−t/RC), and after about five time constants it drops below 1% of its initial value.\nThe discharge switch is generally on a junction box near the equipment. After discharging, check the remaining voltage, and continue dismantling only once it is confirmed safe.

[codex:cx_seal]
中：密封盖
英：SEAL

[codex:cx_seal_a]
中：用来封存船上生物危险品的装置，可以通过密封袋或者密封盖把危险品封闭起来，防止污染物在搬运过程中扩散。
英：A device for sealing the ship’s biohazard items. It closes the hazardous item in with a sealing bag or sealing cap, to stop contaminants spreading while it is being moved.

[codex:cx_seal_b]
中：如果还没有完成密封就直接搬运或者切割，里面的污染物可能会进入舱室空气，并随着空气流动扩散到其他位置，导致更多设备受到污染。
英：If you move or cut it before sealing is complete, the contaminants inside may get into the room’s air and spread with the air flow to other places, contaminating more equipment.

[codex:cx_seal_c]
中：处理生物危险品之前，先使用密封袋或者密封盖把危险品封好，确认不会发生泄漏以后，再把整件危险品送到专门的危险品回收口。
英：Before handling a biohazard item, seal it with the sealing bag or cap, confirm it will not leak, and then take the whole item to the dedicated hazard recovery mouth.

[codex:cx_seal_x]
中：生物污染物可以随着空气流动扩散，如果舱室里面还有没有密封的危险品，直接打开舱门或者切开舱壁，就可能让污染物进入隔壁舱室。\n一旦设备受到污染，即使设备本身没有损坏，也需要先经过清洗和消毒才能回收，最终价值会降低 25%。\n所以在拆解受到生物污染的舱室之前，最好先把里面的危险品全部密封好，再继续处理周围的船体结构。
英：Biological contaminants can spread with the air flow. If a room still holds unsealed hazardous items, opening its door or cutting its bulkhead directly may let the contaminants into the next room.\nOnce equipment is contaminated, it has to be cleaned and disinfected before recovery even if the equipment itself is undamaged, and its final value is reduced by 25%.\nSo before dismantling a biologically contaminated room, it is best to seal all the hazardous items inside first, and only then deal with the surrounding hull structure.

[codex:cx_s_gas]
中：舱压
英：ROOM PRESSURE

[codex:cx_s_gas_a]
中：船上的密封舱室里可能还存有空气或者其他工艺气体，这些气体会让舱室内部保持一定的压力，拆解之前需要先检查。
英：Sealed rooms on the ship may still hold air or other process gases. These gases keep the room under some pressure, and it must be checked before dismantling.

[codex:cx_s_gas_b]
中：如果舱室里面还有较高的气压，直接打开舱门或者切开舱壁，就可能让里面的气体突然喷出，严重时甚至会把舱门、舱壁板和附近的设备一起冲出去。
英：If a room still has high pressure, opening its door or cutting its bulkhead directly may make the gas inside burst out suddenly, and in bad cases it throws out the door, the bulkhead plate and nearby equipment together.

[codex:cx_s_gas_c]
中：拆解密封舱室之前，先通过接口检查内部气压，再连接回收软管，把舱室压力降到 5 kPa 以下，确认泄压完成以后才能打开舱门或者切割舱壁。
英：Before dismantling a sealed room, check its internal pressure at a port, then connect the recovery hose and bring the room pressure below 5 kPa. Open the door or cut the bulkhead only after confirming the venting is complete.

[codex:cx_s_gas_x]
中：舱室里的气压与内部气体的数量、温度和舱室容积有关，可以用公式 pV = nRT 来表示。\n连接回收泵以后，舱室里的气体会逐渐被抽走，内部压力也会随之降低。舱室越大，或者抽气接口越小，完成泄压需要的时间就越长，一些大型舱室可能需要几十秒才能达到安全压力。\n如果直接打开舱门或者切开舱壁，里面的气体就会从开口流向压力较低的一侧。几个原本独立的舱室一旦连通，内部的气压也会相互影响。\n通过回收泵抽走的气体还可以按照回收价值计入合同，所以提前完成气体回收，也能获得额外收入。
英：The pressure in a room depends on the amount and temperature of the gas inside and on the room volume, and can be expressed by the formula pV = nRT.\nOnce the recovery pump is connected, the gas in the room is gradually drawn off and the internal pressure falls with it. The larger the room, or the smaller the port, the longer venting takes, and some large rooms may need tens of seconds to reach a safe pressure.\nIf you open the door or cut the bulkhead directly, the gas inside flows through the opening to the lower-pressure side. Once several originally separate rooms are connected, their pressures also affect one another.\nGas drawn off by the recovery pump is also counted into the contract at its recovery value, so recovering the gas early earns extra income.

[codex:cx_s_stale]
中：过期
英：STALE

[codex:cx_s_stale_a]
中：检测工具显示的数值只代表测量时的状态。如果后面又对船上的设备进行了操作，之前测到的数值就可能不准确了。
英：A value shown by the test tool represents only the state at the time of measurement. If you then operate other equipment on the ship, the value you measured earlier may no longer be accurate.

[codex:cx_s_stale_b]
中：打开阀门、切断电源或者修改电路连接以后，设备的实际状态可能已经发生变化。如果继续按照之前的检测结果进行拆解，就有可能误判当前的压力或者电力状态。
英：After you open a valve, cut a supply or change a circuit connection, the actual state of the equipment may have changed. If you carry on dismantling by the earlier test result, you may misjudge the current pressure or power state.

[codex:cx_s_stale_c]
中：每次操作设备或者改变连接状态以后，最好重新检测一次，确认当前状态是否安全。已经过期的检测数值会显示成灰色，看到这种情况就需要重新测量。
英：After every operation on equipment or change of connection state, it is best to test again and confirm the current state is safe. A stale test value is shown in grey, and when you see that, you need to measure again.

[codex:cx_s_stale_x]
中：如果舱室压力与上次测量时相比，变化超过 2 kPa 或者 4%，之前的压力读数就会被标记为过期。电路的连接关系发生改变以后，相关的电力检测结果也会失效。\n船上的控制面板和总览屏通常显示的是上一次记录的数据，这些数据也可能已经过期，所以不能完全依靠它们判断当前状态。\n拆解之前，最好使用自己的检测工具重新测量，确认设备当前的实际状态以后再继续操作。
英：If the room pressure has changed by more than 2 kPa or 4% since the last measurement, the earlier pressure reading is marked stale. Once the circuit connections change, the related power test results also become invalid.\nControl panels and overview screens on the ship usually show the data from the last record, which may also be stale, so you cannot rely on them entirely to judge the current state.\nBefore dismantling, it is best to measure again with your own test tool, and carry on only after confirming the actual current state of the equipment.

[codex:cx_s_power]
中：电网与多路电源
英：POWER AND ITS SOURCES

[codex:cx_s_power_a]
中：船上的发电机、电池和电容都可以为设备提供电力，大部分设备通过船体总线供电，也有一些设备使用独立电源，所以切断主电源以后，船上仍然可能有带电的设备。
英：Generators, batteries and capacitors on the ship can all supply power to equipment. Most equipment is powered through the ship’s bus, but some uses an independent supply, so after the main power is cut, there may still be live equipment on the ship.

[codex:cx_s_power_b]
中：关闭船体主电源以后，备用电池和独立电源可能还在供电，电容里面也可能储存着没有释放的电能。如果直接拆除这些设备或者切断电缆，就有可能产生电弧。
英：After the ship’s main power is switched off, backup batteries and independent supplies may still be supplying power, and capacitors may hold energy that has not been released. If you remove this equipment or cut its cables directly, an arc may result.

[codex:cx_s_power_c]
中：拆解电力设备之前，先查看工程图，确认船上有哪些供电线路和独立电源，再逐个断开电源并完成电容放电。处理完成以后，还需要使用检测工具重新验电，确认没有残余电压才能开始拆解。
英：Before dismantling power equipment, check the engineering drawing to confirm which supply lines and independent supplies the ship has, then disconnect each supply in turn and discharge the capacitors. When that is done, test the voltage again with the test tool, and start dismantling only after confirming no residual voltage is left.

[codex:cx_s_power_x]
中：工程图的电路层会标出每条电缆的连接位置，可以通过这些线路找到对应的电源和设备。\n有些设备使用自己的独立电源，包括无人机电池、舱门备用电源和仪器的不间断电源。这些电源通常只负责附近的设备，关闭船体主开关也不会切断它们的供电。\n如果独立电源连接到了船体总线，还可能通过总线向其他设备供电，所以即使已经关闭主电源，也不能直接认定整条线路都已经断电。\n电压超过 30 V 的设备，在拔除电缆或者进行切割时就可能产生电弧。拆解之前需要检查所有供电线路，断开剩余电源并完成放电，最后重新验电，确认安全以后再继续操作。
英：The circuit layer of the engineering drawing marks where each cable connects, and through these lines you can find the matching supplies and equipment.\nSome equipment uses its own independent supply, including drone batteries, door backup supplies and instrument UPS units. These supplies usually power only the equipment nearby, and switching off the ship’s main switch does not cut their supply.\nIf an independent supply is connected to the ship’s bus, it can also feed other equipment through the bus, so even after the main power is off, you cannot assume the whole line is dead.\nEquipment above 30 V can produce an arc when its cable is unplugged or cut. Before dismantling, check all supply lines, disconnect the remaining supplies and discharge, then test the voltage again, and carry on only after confirming it is safe.

[codex:cx_s_heat]
中：热管线与余热
英：HEAT

[codex:cx_s_heat_a]
中：发动机、反应堆和加热炉等设备即使已经停止运行，内部的管线和零件也可能保持很高的温度，需要经过一段时间的冷却才能安全拆除。
英：Even after equipment such as engines, reactors and furnaces has stopped running, the lines and parts inside may stay very hot, and need some time to cool before they can be removed safely.

[codex:cx_s_heat_b]
中：如果设备温度还在 60°C 以上，直接使用无人机抓取可能会造成无人机损坏。如果使用不合适的方法快速降温，也可能因为温度变化太大，导致管线或设备出现裂纹。
英：If equipment is still above 60°C, grabbing it directly with the drone may damage the drone. If you cool it quickly by an unsuitable method, the temperature change may also be so large that lines or equipment crack.

[codex:cx_s_heat_c]
中：拆解之前先测量设备温度，确认冷却系统还在正常运行，让设备慢慢降温。等温度降到安全范围以后，再开始拆除管线和其他零件。
英：Before dismantling, measure the equipment temperature, confirm the cooling system is still running normally, and let the equipment cool slowly. Once the temperature has dropped to a safe range, start removing the lines and other parts.

[codex:cx_s_heat_x]
中：真空环境里没有空气对流，设备只能通过热辐射和接触部件之间的热传导慢慢散热。一些温度达到几百度的大型设备，如果只依靠自然冷却，可能需要几个小时才能降到安全温度。\n冷却系统需要有电力供应和足够的冷却液才能正常工作，如果设备还很热就提前切断冷却系统，剩余的热量会很难散出去，冷却时间也会变得更长。所以拆解高温设备时，最好先保持冷却系统运行，等设备充分降温以后再切断电源。
英：There is no air convection in vacuum, so equipment can only lose heat slowly by thermal radiation and by conduction between parts in contact. A large piece of equipment at a few hundred degrees may take several hours to fall to a safe temperature by natural cooling alone.\nThe cooling system needs a power supply and enough coolant to work. If you cut the cooling system while the equipment is still hot, the remaining heat is hard to get out and cooling takes even longer. So when dismantling hot equipment, it is best to keep the cooling system running first, and cut the power only after the equipment has cooled fully.

[codex:cx_s_cloud]
中：推进剂云
英：PROPELLANT CLOUD

[codex:cx_s_cloud_a]
中：燃料发生泄漏以后，挥发出来的蒸气可能会聚集在舱室里面，或者在船体外面形成一团燃料气体。
英：After a fuel leak, the vapour that evaporates may gather inside a room, or form a cloud of fuel gas outside the hull.

[codex:cx_s_cloud_b]
中：如果燃料蒸气达到能够燃烧的浓度，附近的激光切割就可能将其点燃。如果舱室里面聚集了大量燃料蒸气，还可能发生爆燃，损坏附近的设备。
英：If the fuel vapour reaches a concentration that can burn, laser cutting nearby may ignite it. If a large amount of fuel vapour has gathered in a room, it may also deflagrate and damage nearby equipment.

[codex:cx_s_cloud_c]
中：发现推进剂泄漏以后，先把剩余的燃料回收干净，等泄漏形成的气体充分扩散，确认附近没有危险以后，再继续进行切割。
英：After finding a propellant leak, first recover the remaining fuel, wait until the gas from the leak has dispersed fully and you have confirmed there is no danger nearby, and then continue cutting.
