[codex:cx_s_cloud_x]
中：燃料蒸气需要达到一定的浓度才能燃烧，浓度太低或者太高都可能无法点燃。拿肼和氢气来说，肼在空气中的可燃浓度范围约为 4.7%～100%，氢气则约为 4%～75%。\n即使云团中心的燃料浓度太高，外围也可能存在浓度刚好适合燃烧的区域，只要接触到火源，就有可能发生燃烧。\n在真空环境下，泄漏的燃料蒸气会逐渐向周围扩散，浓度也会慢慢降低。如果泄漏发生在有空气的封闭舱室里面，燃料蒸气就可能聚集更长时间，需要先完成通风和气体清理，才能安全进行切割。
英：Fuel vapour has to reach a certain concentration to burn, and if it is too low or too high it may not ignite. Take hydrazine and hydrogen: hydrazine’s flammable range in air is about 4.7%–100%, and hydrogen’s is about 4%–75%.\nEven if the fuel concentration at the centre of the cloud is too high, there may be a zone at the edge where the concentration is just right for burning, and if it meets an ignition source, it can burn.\nIn vacuum, leaked fuel vapour gradually spreads outwards and its concentration slowly falls. If the leak is in a closed room with air, the fuel vapour may gather for longer, and you must finish ventilating and clearing the gas before you can cut safely.

[codex:cx_s_fire]
中：火灾
英：FIRE

[codex:cx_s_fire_a]
中：燃料需要接触空气中的氧气或者其他氧化剂才能燃烧。如果周围是真空环境，而且没有其他氧化剂，泄漏的燃料一般只会逐渐蒸发、冻结或者向周围扩散。
英：Fuel needs oxygen from the air or another oxidiser to burn. If the surroundings are vacuum and there is no other oxidiser, leaked fuel generally just gradually evaporates, freezes or drifts away.

[codex:cx_s_fire_b]
中：激光切割、电弧、带电电缆产生的火花、短路、高温切口、发动机尾焰和爆炸都可能引燃附近的燃料。\n一旦舱室发生火灾，火焰就可能逐渐蔓延到其他区域。严重时，舱内压力甚至可能上升到原来的 8 倍，导致舱门被气压冲开。
英：Laser cutting, arcs, sparks from live cables, short circuits, hot cuts, engine exhaust and explosions can all ignite nearby fuel.\nOnce a room catches fire, the flames may gradually spread to other areas. In serious cases, the pressure in the room can even rise to 8 times the original, so that the door is blown open by the pressure.

[codex:cx_s_fire_c]
中：使用激光切割工具之前，先把舱室里的空气抽干净，确认内部已经达到真空状态。\n如果舱室已经发生火灾，暂时不要打开舱门，以免外面的空气进入，让火势进一步扩大。保持舱室封闭，等内部氧气消耗到无法维持燃烧时，火焰就会逐渐熄灭。
英：Before using the laser, pump the room’s air out and confirm it is at vacuum.\nIf a room is already on fire, keep its door shut so outside air cannot feed the fire. Once the oxygen inside is used up, the flames go out.

[codex:cx_s_fire_x]
中：燃烧需要燃料、氧气和点火源三个条件，只要缺少其中一个，火焰就无法继续维持。\n在失重环境下，火焰不会像地面上一样向上燃烧，因为这里没有重力造成的自然对流。火焰可能形成一个缓慢扩散的球形区域，主要依靠周围氧气的扩散来维持燃烧。\n如果舱室完全封闭，火焰会持续消耗内部的氧气，等氧气浓度下降到一定程度以后，燃烧就会停止。不过这时候燃料可能还留在舱室里面，如果重新打开舱门，让空气进入，再遇到火花就可能再次起火。\n封闭舱室发生火灾时，燃烧产生的高温还会让内部气体迅速膨胀。如果气体温度上升到几千度，舱内压力就可能增加到原来的数倍，严重时甚至会损坏舱门和周围的船体结构。
英：Burning needs three conditions: fuel, oxygen and an ignition source. If any one is missing, the flame cannot be sustained.\nIn weightlessness, flames do not burn upwards as they do on the ground, because there is no natural convection caused by gravity. A flame may form a slowly spreading sphere, sustained mainly by oxygen diffusing in from around it.\nIf the room is completely closed, the fire keeps using up the oxygen inside, and once the oxygen concentration falls to a certain level, burning stops. By then the fuel may still be left in the room, though, and if you open the door again and let air in, a spark may start the fire again.\nWhen a closed room is on fire, the high temperature of the burning also makes the gas inside expand rapidly. If the gas temperature rises to several thousand degrees, the pressure in the room may increase to several times the original, and in serious cases it can even damage the door and the surrounding hull structure.

[codex:cx_s_liquid]
中：失重液体
英：LIQUIDS IN FREE FALL

[codex:cx_s_liquid_a]
中：船上的管线或储罐发生泄漏以后，里面的液体会进入失重环境，并在表面张力的作用下形成漂浮的液团。
英：After a line or tank on the ship leaks, the liquid inside enters weightlessness and forms floating globules under surface tension.

[codex:cx_s_liquid_b]
中：泄漏的液体如果接触到还在通电的设备，可能会造成短路。如果泄漏的是燃料，在附近使用激光切割工具还可能引起火灾。温度很低的液体也会对设备造成损伤，严重时甚至会让零件冻裂。
英：If leaked liquid touches equipment that is still powered, it may cause a short circuit. If the leaked liquid is fuel, using the laser cutting tool nearby may also start a fire. Very cold liquids can also damage equipment, and in serious cases even freeze-crack parts.

[codex:cx_s_liquid_c]
中：拆除管线或储罐之前，先把软管连接到对应的接口，将里面剩余的液体抽取干净，确认没有残留液体以后，再开始切割管线或者拆除储罐。
英：Before removing a line or tank, connect the hose to the matching port and draw off the liquid left inside. Once you have confirmed no liquid remains, start cutting the line or removing the tank.

[codex:cx_s_liquid_x]
中：在失重环境下，液体会受到表面张力的影响，逐渐聚集成球形液团。如果没有其他外力影响，这些液团就会沿着原来的运动方向继续漂浮，碰到设备表面以后，可能会溅散成更小的液滴，也可能附着在表面形成一层液膜。\n不同液体泄漏以后还会出现不同的情况。液氢和液氦会在泄漏时迅速汽化一部分，剩余液体也会继续沸腾；水在真空环境下可能在几秒内结成冰球；燃料会逐渐挥发，产生的蒸气如果聚集起来，就可能形成可燃气体区域。钻井泥浆则容易附着在接触到的设备表面，增加后续的清理工作。\n所以拆解装有液体的设备之前，最好先通过接口把液体抽取干净，避免切割过程中发生泄漏。
英：In weightlessness, liquid is affected by surface tension and gradually gathers into spherical globules. If no other force acts on them, these globules keep drifting in their original direction of motion, and after they hit an equipment surface, they may splash into smaller droplets, or stick to the surface as a liquid film.\nDifferent liquids also behave differently after leaking. Liquid hydrogen and liquid helium flash part of themselves into vapour as they leak, and the rest of the liquid keeps boiling; water in vacuum may freeze into an ice ball within seconds; fuel gradually evaporates, and if the vapour gathers, it may form a flammable gas zone. Drilling mud tends to stick to whatever equipment surface it touches, adding to the cleaning work afterwards.\nSo before dismantling equipment that holds liquid, it is best to draw the liquid off through a port first, to avoid leaks during cutting.

[codex:cx_s_hose]
中：回收软管
英：RECOVERY HOSE

[codex:cx_s_hose_a]
中：回收软管用来抽取船上的气体和液体，将软管连接到对应的接口以后，就可以通过船坞的回收泵，把管线或储罐里面的气体和液体抽取出来。
英：The recovery hose draws gas and liquid out of the ship. Once the hose is connected to the matching port, the berth’s recovery pump can draw the gas and liquid out of the line or tank.

[codex:cx_s_hose_b]
中：如果把软管连接到错误的接口，可能会让不同的回收物混在一起，影响回收处理。有些回收泵在抽取完成以后还继续运行，也可能因为没有液体进入而发生空转，造成设备损坏。
英：If you connect the hose to the wrong port, different recovered substances may mix and affect recovery processing. Some recovery pumps also keep running after the draw is finished, and may run dry because no liquid is coming in, causing damage to the equipment.

[codex:cx_s_hose_c]
中：先确认接口对应的回收物，再连接软管开始抽取。抽取过程中注意查看剩余量，等气体或液体回收到指定数量以后，就可以停止回收并取下软管。
英：First confirm what the port recovers, then connect the hose and start drawing. While drawing, watch the remaining amount, and once the gas or liquid has been recovered to the set quantity, you can stop recovery and take the hose off.

[codex:cx_s_hose_x]
中：一条回收软管每次只能连接一个接口。随着储罐里面的气压或者液位逐渐降低，回收速度也会慢慢下降，如果觉得等待时间太长，可以按住「=」键加快游戏时间。\n回收的气体和液体会按照当前市场价格计入合同收入，所以能回收的材料最好尽量保留下来。遇到危险气体或者液体时，还需要连接对应的专用回收接口，不能直接排放到太空中。
英：One recovery hose can connect to only one port at a time. As the pressure or liquid level in the tank gradually falls, the recovery speed also slowly drops, and if the wait feels too long, you can hold the ‘=’ key to speed up game time.\nRecovered gas and liquid are counted into the contract income at the current market price, so keep as much recoverable material as you can. When you meet hazardous gas or liquid, you also need to connect the matching dedicated recovery port, and it cannot be vented straight into space.

[codex:cx_s_free]
中：脱离与惯性
英：FREE PIECES AND INERTIA

[codex:cx_s_free_a]
中：当零件的最后一个固定点被拆除以后，零件就会完全脱离船体，开始在失重环境中自由漂浮。
英：Once a part’s last fixing point is removed, the part comes completely free of the hull and starts to float freely in weightlessness.

[codex:cx_s_free_b]
中：零件脱离以后，会继续保持原来的移动速度和旋转状态。如果没有及时控制，零件就可能撞上附近的船体结构或者其他设备，造成不必要的损坏。
英：After a part comes free, it keeps its original speed and spin. If you do not control it in time, the part may hit nearby hull structure or other equipment and cause needless damage.

[codex:cx_s_free_c]
中：拆除最后一个固定点之前，先用抓钩固定住零件。等零件完全脱离船体以后，再慢慢调整它的移动方向，将零件送进回收口。
英：Before removing the last fixing point, secure the part with the grapple. Once the part has fully come free of the hull, slowly adjust its direction of motion and send the part into a recovery mouth.

[codex:cx_s_free_x]
中：在失重环境中，如果没有受到其他外力，零件就会一直保持原来的运动状态。即使只是轻轻推动一下，零件也会继续向前移动，想让它停下来，就需要施加反方向的作用力。\n零件的质量和移动速度都会影响撞击时的动量。一件重 3 吨、以 0.5 m/s 移动的设备，和一件重 1 吨、以 1.5 m/s 移动的设备，具有相同大小的动量，失去控制后都可能造成严重损坏。\n如果零件不小心漂出了船坞范围，船坞边缘的牵引场会逐渐把它拉回来。不过拆解大型设备时，最好还是提前使用抓钩控制，避免零件发生碰撞。
英：In weightlessness, if no other force acts on it, a part keeps its original state of motion forever. Even a light push keeps the part moving on, and to stop it you need to apply a force in the opposite direction.\nA part’s mass and its speed both affect its momentum on impact. A 3 t piece of equipment moving at 0.5 m/s and a 1 t piece moving at 1.5 m/s have the same momentum, and either can cause serious damage if it gets out of control.\nIf a part accidentally drifts outside the berth area, the tractor field at the berth edge gradually pulls it back. Even so, when dismantling large equipment, it is best to control it with the grapple beforehand, to avoid collisions.

[codex:cx_s_grapple]
中：磁力抓钩
英：MAGNETIC GRAPPLE

[codex:cx_s_grapple_a]
中：磁力抓钩是无人机用来固定和拖动零件的工具，可以在拆解过程中控制零件的移动方向，也能把拆下来的设备送到指定位置。
英：The magnetic grapple is the tool the drone uses to hold and drag parts. During dismantling it controls a part’s direction of motion, and also carries removed equipment to the place you choose.

[codex:cx_s_grapple_b]
中：磁力抓钩有一定的承重限制，如果零件超过了额定质量，就无法正常拖动。如果零件还连接着船体，强行拖动可能会扯断固定锁扣和连接管线，造成设备损坏。
英：The magnetic grapple has a load limit. If a part exceeds the rated mass, it cannot be dragged properly. If the part is still connected to the hull, dragging it by force may tear off the locks and connecting lines and damage the equipment.

[codex:cx_s_grapple_c]
中：使用磁力抓钩之前，先检查零件的质量和剩余固定点，确认零件已经完全脱离船体，并且没有超过抓钩的承重限制以后，再开始拖动。
英：Before using the magnetic grapple, check the part’s mass and the fixing points remaining, and start dragging only after you have confirmed the part has fully come free of the hull and does not exceed the grapple’s load limit.

[codex:cx_s_grapple_x]
中：磁力抓钩默认最多可以拖动 3 吨的零件，升级以后可以提高到 5 吨。\n由于无人机的推力有限，拖动较重的设备时，加速和减速都需要更长时间，所以在接近回收口或者其他船体结构之前，最好提前减速，避免因为来不及停止而发生碰撞。\n如果零件还连接着船体，使用抓钩拖动时，光标会显示剩余的固定点数量。继续增加拉力就可能扯断锁扣或者连接管线，所以最好先把这些连接全部拆除，再使用抓钩搬运零件。
英：The magnetic grapple can drag parts of up to 3 t by default, and after an upgrade this rises to 5 t.\nBecause the drone’s thrust is limited, accelerating and braking take longer when dragging heavy equipment, so before you approach a recovery mouth or other hull structure, it is best to start slowing down early, to avoid a collision because you could not stop in time.\nIf a part is still connected to the hull, the cursor shows the number of fixing points left when you drag with the grapple. Increasing the pull further may tear off locks or connecting lines, so it is best to remove all these connections first and then move the part with the grapple.

[codex:cx_s_furnace]
中：熔炉与废料口
英：THE FURNACE

[codex:cx_s_furnace_a]
中：船坞底部设有专门回收废金属的熔炉入口，拆下来的金属废料可以直接送进去熔炼，处理完成后会按照回收的金属重量结算报酬。
英：At the bottom of the berth there is a furnace mouth made for recovering scrap metal. Scrap metal removed from the ship can be sent straight in for melting, and after processing you are paid by the weight of metal recovered.

[codex:cx_s_furnace_b]
中：不要把危险品或者还带有电压、压力的设备送进熔炉，这些东西在高温下可能发生爆炸。\n完整设备也不建议直接送进去，因为熔炉只会按照废金属的价格结算，原本还能正常使用的设备就卖不出完整回收的价格了。
英：Never send hazardous items, or equipment still carrying voltage or pressure, into the furnace: they may explode in the heat.\nDon’t send whole equipment in either. The furnace pays only the scrap-metal price, so working equipment loses its full-recovery price.

[codex:cx_s_furnace_c]
中：先确认准备回收的材料只有普通金属，没有残留的危险物品。然后在距离熔炉入口大约 6–8 m 的位置，用抓钩将废料对准下方的长条形入口，调整好方向后送进去就可以了。
英：First make sure the material to be recovered is only ordinary metal with no hazardous items left. Then, from about 6–8 m away from the furnace mouth, use the grapple to line the scrap up with the long, narrow opening below, and send it in once its direction is right.

[codex:cx_s_furnace_x]
中：废金属进入熔炉入口以后，会先经过下方的料仓压实，再由螺旋给料机送进真空感应炉进行熔炼。\n熔炉内部的坩埚以每分钟 45 转的速度旋转，产生大约 1.4 g 的离心加速度，让熔融金属保持在炉衬附近。炉体外侧还安装了水冷铜线圈，线圈通电以后会产生交变磁场，使金属内部形成涡流并逐渐升温。电流在金属内部的集肤深度可以用公式 δ = 503 × √(ρ/μf) 计算。\n当炉内温度达到 1640°C 后，熔融金属就会被倒入水冷铜锭模，冷却成型后作为钢锭回收。\n整个熔炼过程需要在真空环境下进行，所以船坞已经为熔炉准备了相应的真空系统，不需要额外调整外部压力。
英：After scrap metal enters the furnace mouth, it is first compacted in the bunker below, and then a screw feeder sends it into a vacuum induction furnace to be melted.\nThe crucible inside the furnace spins at 45 revolutions per minute, producing a centrifugal acceleration of about 1.4 g that keeps the molten metal against the lining. Water-cooled copper coils are fitted around the outside of the furnace body, and when the coils are energised they produce an alternating magnetic field, which sets up eddy currents inside the metal and gradually heats it. The skin depth of the current in the metal can be calculated with the formula δ = 503 × √(ρ/μf).\nWhen the temperature in the furnace reaches 1640°C, the molten metal is poured into water-cooled copper ingot moulds, and after cooling and setting it is recovered as steel ingots.\nThe whole melting process has to take place in vacuum, so the berth has already provided the furnace with a matching vacuum system, and you need not adjust any external pressure.

[codex:cx_s_equip_mouth]
中：设备回收口
英：EQUIPMENT BAY

[codex:cx_s_equip_mouth_a]
中：船坞里专门用来回收完整设备的回收口，拆下来的设备需要从下方送进去，才能按照设备的完整价值进行回收。
英：The recovery mouth in the berth made for whole equipment. Removed equipment must be fed in from below to be recovered at its full value.

[codex:cx_s_equip_mouth_b]
中：如果设备在拆解过程中受到撞击或者切割损坏，回收价格就会按照受损程度降低。如果设备内部还有电力或者残留液体，直接送进回收口还可能引发事故。
英：If equipment is knocked or damaged by cutting during dismantling, the recovery price drops according to the damage. If the equipment still holds power or leftover liquid inside, sending it straight into the recovery mouth may also cause an accident.

[codex:cx_s_equip_mouth_c]
中：回收设备之前，先切断电源，排空内部的液体，再拔掉连接的线路。确认设备已经安全以后，尽量保持设备完整，再用抓钩把它送进回收口。
英：Before recovering equipment, cut its power, drain the liquid inside and unplug the connected lines. Once the equipment is confirmed safe, keep it whole as far as you can, and then send it into the recovery mouth with the grapple.

[codex:cx_s_equip_mouth_x]
中：设备的回收价格按照原本价值乘以当前成色计算。如果设备在拆解过程中受到撞击、过热、短路或者污染，成色就会下降，最后能够拿到的回收金额也会减少。\n如果合同要求回收指定设备，就必须把这些设备完整送进回收口，才能满足合同要求。\n当设备距离回收口太远时，回收口的接收环会向设备所在的位置移动，方便你把设备送进去。
英：The recovery price of equipment is its original value multiplied by its current condition. If equipment is knocked, overheated, short-circuited or contaminated during dismantling, its condition drops, and the recovery payment you finally get is smaller.\nIf a contract requires recovery of named equipment, those items must be sent whole into the recovery mouth to meet the contract requirement.\nWhen equipment is too far from the recovery mouth, the mouth’s receiving ring moves towards where the equipment is, so it is easier to send it in.

[codex:cx_s_hazard_mouth]
中：危险品回收口
英：HAZARD MOUTH

[codex:cx_s_hazard_mouth_a]
中：船坞里专门用来回收危险品的地方，燃料罐、电池、火工品和放射源等物品都需要送到这里处理。
英：The place in the berth made for recovering hazardous items. Fuel tanks, batteries, pyrotechnics and radioactive sources all need to be taken here for handling.

[codex:cx_s_hazard_mouth_b]
中：危险品不能直接送进熔炉，如果把燃料罐或者其他危险物品送进去，可能会引发爆炸，也可能造成熔炉污染。
英：Hazardous items must not be sent straight into the furnace. If a fuel tank or another hazardous item is sent in, it may explode, and may also contaminate the furnace.

[codex:cx_s_hazard_mouth_c]
中：拆解过程中，如果光标指向的物品显示为「危险品」，就需要把它送进危险品回收口，不要送到普通的设备回收口或者熔炉里。
英：During dismantling, if the item the cursor points at is shown as ‘hazard’, it must go into the hazard mouth, not the ordinary equipment recovery mouth or the furnace.

[codex:cx_s_hazard_mouth_x]
中：危险品会按照危险处理津贴计算回收收入，不会按照普通金属的价格结算，所以需要把它们单独送到危险品回收口。\n危险品回收口的入口比较小，运送的时候需要慢慢调整位置和方向，确认对准以后再把物品送进去。\n如果你拖着危险品靠近熔炉，系统也会显示提醒，避免因为送错回收位置而发生事故。
英：Hazardous items are paid as hazard-handling allowance, not at the price of ordinary metal, so they need to be taken separately to the hazard mouth.\nThe opening of the hazard mouth is fairly small, so when carrying an item you need to adjust its position and direction slowly, and send it in once it is lined up.\nIf you drag a hazardous item near the furnace, the system also shows a warning, to avoid an accident from sending it to the wrong recovery point.

[codex:cx_s_field]
中：牵引场
英：TRACTOR FIELD

[codex:cx_s_field_a]
中：船坞开放区域边缘安装了牵引力场，可以把漂出泊位的零件和设备逐渐拉回来，防止它们继续向外漂移。
英：Tractor fields are fitted along the edges of the berth’s open areas. They gradually pull back parts and equipment that have drifted out of the bay, and stop them drifting further out.

[codex:cx_s_field_b]
中：牵引场只能改变零件的移动方向，无法直接让零件停下来。如果零件漂出去的时候速度太快，即使被牵引场拉了回来，也可能继续撞上船体或者附近的设备，造成损坏。
英：The tractor field can only change a part’s direction of motion, and cannot stop the part directly. If a part drifts out too fast, even once it is pulled back by the field, it may still hit the hull or nearby equipment and cause damage.

[codex:cx_s_field_c]
中：拆除零件的最后一个固定点之前，最好先用抓钩固定住零件，避免零件失去控制。不要完全依赖牵引场，等零件漂出去以后再处理。
英：Before removing a part’s last fixing point, it is best to secure the part with the grapple first, so it does not get out of control. Do not rely entirely on the tractor field and deal with the part only after it has drifted out.

[codex:cx_s_field_x]
中：船坞采用半开放式结构，底部设有作业平台，周围安装了塔架和悬臂，上方和右侧则保持开放，方便大型船体和拆解下来的设备进出。\n牵引场主要在船坞内部边框以外的区域发挥作用，零件漂出去的距离越远，受到的牵引力就越大，最终会被逐渐拉回泊位附近。\n如果后续扩建船坞，牵引场的作用范围也会跟着扩大，边界会移动到新建船坞的最外侧，确保扩建后的开放区域仍然受到牵引场保护。
英：The berth has a half-open structure, with a work platform at the bottom, towers and booms around it, and the top and right sides left open so that large hulls and removed equipment can go in and out.\nThe tractor field mainly acts in the area outside the berth’s inner frame. The further out a part drifts, the stronger the pull on it, and in the end it is gradually pulled back to the bay.\nIf the berth is extended later, the field’s range grows with it, and the boundary moves to the outermost edge of the newly built berth, so the open area after the extension is still covered by the field.

[codex:cx_s_berth_ext]
中：船坞扩建
英：BERTH EXTENSION

[codex:cx_s_berth_ext_a]
中：购买船坞扩建项目以后，施工队会直接在现有泊位旁边进行建设，按照实际的在轨施工流程安装新的桁架、平台和其他设施。
英：After you buy a berth extension, the construction crew builds right next to the existing bay, installing new trusses, platforms and other facilities in the way real in-orbit construction works.

[codex:cx_s_berth_ext_b]
中：扩建施工期间，正在施工的区域会暂时封闭。如果有零件或者设备进入施工区域，周围的牵引场就会把它们推开，避免影响正在进行的安装工作。
英：During the extension work, the area under construction is temporarily closed. If parts or equipment enter the work area, the tractor field around it pushes them away, so they do not interfere with the installation under way.

[codex:cx_s_berth_ext_c]
中：你可以留在现场观看整个施工过程，也可以按任意键跳过，直接等到船坞扩建完成。
英：You can stay on site to watch the whole construction, or press any key to skip it and wait until the berth extension is finished.

[codex:cx_s_berth_ext_x]
中：船坞扩建会按照实际的在轨施工流程进行。施工开始前，系统会先隔离施工区域，然后由拖船把装有折叠桁架的货箱运送到指定位置。\n桁架通过铰接结构从储存筒内逐节展开，每节长度为 0.6 m。施工设备会在横向支撑杆展开并锁定以后，再继续安装下一节，直到整个桁架达到设计长度。\n安装模块的时候，会先用机械钩爪固定接口，再使用 M20、10.9 级高强度螺栓进行连接，每颗螺栓的预紧力为 125 kN。完成固定以后，施工设备还会使用电子束焊接技术处理连接处的焊缝。由于电子束焊接需要真空环境，而船坞本身就位于太空中，所以可以直接进行焊接。\n所有结构安装完成以后，还需要进行压力测试和承重测试。管路会使用氦气加压到正常工作压力的 1.5 倍，检查有没有泄漏，新安装的平台则需要承受设计载荷的 1.25 倍，测试时的最大挠度不能超过跨度的 1/800。\n通过这些测试以后，施工队才会进行最后的设备调试和验收，确认没有问题后，新的船坞区域就可以正式投入使用。\n如果扩建后的船坞需要停靠更高的船只，施工队还会增加塔架的高度，同时降低底部平台的位置，为大型船体留出足够的空间。
英：A berth extension follows real in-orbit construction. The work area is isolated first, then a tug brings in the crates of folded truss.\nThe truss unfolds from its canister one hinged 0.6 m section at a time; each next section goes on only after the cross braces have opened and locked, until the truss reaches its design length.\nModules are held by mechanical claws, then joined with M20 grade 10.9 high-strength bolts at 125 kN preload each, and the seams are electron-beam welded. That welding needs vacuum, which space already provides.\nThe lines are then pressurised with helium to 1.5 times working pressure and leak-checked, and the new platform must carry 1.25 times its design load with no more than 1/800 of the span in deflection.\nAfter final commissioning and acceptance, the new berth area goes into use.\nFor a taller ship, the crew also raises the tower and lowers the bottom platform to make room for the hull.

[codex:cx_s_blueprint]
中：工程图
英：THE DRAWING

[codex:cx_s_blueprint_a]
中：工程图记录了船只原本的设计结构，包括舱室分布、管路走向、电路连接和各个设备的固定位置，拆解的时候可以用来查看船体内部的布置。
英：The engineering drawing records the ship’s original design structure, including the room layout, line routes, circuit connections and the fixing position of each piece of equipment, and can be used to look at the layout inside the hull when dismantling.

[codex:cx_s_blueprint_b]
中：工程图记录的是船只最初的设计情况，但船只在使用过程中可能经历过改装、维修或者事故，所以图纸上的结构不一定和现在的船体完全一致。
英：The engineering drawing records the ship’s original design, but the ship may have been refitted, repaired or in an accident during its service, so the structure on the drawing may not match the current hull exactly.

[codex:cx_s_blueprint_c]
中：拆解之前可以先查看工程图，确认设备的位置和管线的连接方式。不过实际操作的时候，还是要通过现场检查来确认设备当前的状态，不能完全按照图纸判断。
英：Before dismantling you can look at the engineering drawing first to confirm the positions of equipment and how lines are connected. In actual work, though, you still need to check the equipment’s current state on site, and cannot judge entirely by the drawing.

[codex:cx_s_blueprint_x]
中：按下 Tab 键就可以打开工程图，查看船体内部的结构，包括盖板后面安装了哪些设备、阀门连接着哪条管线，以及各个设备从哪里获得电力。\n不过工程图通常不会记录船只后来发生的变化。如果船只曾经进行过改装或者维修，内部就可能出现额外安装的电源、已经堵塞的接口，或者重新修补过的管路。\n这些变化都需要在拆解现场进行检查，尤其是涉及电路和压力管线的时候，最好先测量实际状态，再决定怎么拆除。
英：Press the Tab key to open the engineering drawing and see the structure inside the hull, including what equipment sits behind the covers, which line a valve is connected to, and where each piece of equipment gets its power.\nHowever, the engineering drawing usually does not record later changes to the ship. If the ship was refitted or repaired, there may be an extra power supply, a blocked port or a repaired line inside.\nThese changes all have to be checked on site during dismantling. Especially where circuits and pressure lines are involved, it is best to measure the actual state first, and then decide how to remove it.

[codex:cx_s_scope]
中：分段与抢救合同
英：PART-SHIP CONTRACTS

[codex:cx_s_scope_a]
中：这类合同只要求你拆除船体的指定区域，或者回收几件指定的设备，不需要把整艘船全部拆完。
英：This kind of contract asks you to dismantle only a specified area of the hull, or to recover a few named pieces of equipment, and you do not need to dismantle the whole ship.

[codex:cx_s_scope_b]
中：合同只允许你处理指定范围内的零件，如果拆除了范围以外的结构，或者在操作过程中造成损坏，都会被视为违反合同，需要从报酬中扣除相应的费用。
英：The contract allows you to handle only the parts inside the specified scope. If you remove structures outside the scope, or cause damage during the work, it counts as a breach of contract and the matching cost is deducted from your payment.

[codex:cx_s_scope_c]
中：开始拆解之前，先查看界面上标记的合同范围，确认哪些区域和设备可以处理。范围以外的部分尽量不要碰，避免因为误拆或者损坏而被扣钱。
英：Before dismantling, check the contract scope marked on the screen to confirm which areas and equipment you may handle. Try not to touch anything outside the scope, so you are not charged for wrongly removing or damaging it.

[codex:cx_s_scope_x]
中：分段拆解合同只会收购指定区段内的零件，船体里面剩余的气体和液体不属于收购范围，所以即使把它们回收下来，也不会计入合同收入。\n抢救合同则按照指定设备支付固定报酬，只要把合同要求的设备全部送进回收口，就可以直接完成任务，剩下的船体会由拖船负责运走，不需要继续拆解。\n拆解时可以通过光标确认零件是否属于合同范围，如果光标指向的零件显示「范围外」，就说明合同没有授权拆除这个零件，最好保留原样。
英：A section dismantling contract buys only the parts inside the specified section. The gas and liquid left in the hull are not within the purchase scope, so even if you recover them, they are not counted into the contract income.\nA salvage contract pays a fixed fee for the named equipment. As soon as all the equipment the contract asks for has been sent into the recovery mouth, the task is complete, and the rest of the hull is towed away by a tug, so you need not dismantle any further.\nWhen dismantling, you can use the cursor to check whether a part is within the contract scope. If the part under the cursor shows ‘out of scope’, the contract does not authorise removing this part, and it is best left as it is.

[codex:cx_s_hauler]
中：搬运无人机
英：HAULING DRONE

[codex:cx_s_hauler_a]
中：船坞悬臂上安装的辅助搬运设备，可以自动把你标记好的零件运送到对应的回收口，省去手动搬运的过程。
英：A helper handling machine fitted on the berth boom. It automatically carries the parts you have marked to the matching recovery mouth, saving you the manual carrying.

[codex:cx_s_hauler_b]
中：搬运无人机只会按照你的标记执行任务，不会主动检查零件是否安全。即使零件内部还有残留液体，或者设备尚未断电，无人机也会直接进行搬运，可能在运输过程中发生事故。
英：The hauling drone only carries out your marks and does not check on its own whether a part is safe. Even if the part still holds leftover liquid, or the equipment has not been de-energised, the drone moves it anyway, and an accident may happen in transit.

[codex:cx_s_hauler_c]
中：标记零件之前，先检查并处理好内部的电力和液体等危险，确认零件已经可以安全回收以后，再点击鼠标右键进行标记，让无人机负责搬运。
英：Before marking a part, check and deal with dangers inside such as power and liquid, and mark the part with a right click only after confirming it is safe to recover, to let the drone carry it.

[codex:cx_s_hauler_x]
中：搬运无人机会按照你标记零件的先后顺序安排运输任务，并自动规划搬运路线，选择对应的回收口。\n有了无人机以后，你就不用每次都亲自把零件拖到回收口了，不过无人机不会替你判断零件是否已经处理安全，也不会检查零件应该如何分类回收。\n所以在标记之前，你仍然需要确认零件已经完成断电、排液等必要处理，并检查回收目标是否正确，之后再交给无人机搬运。
英：The hauling drone arranges its transport jobs in the order you marked the parts, plans its own carrying routes automatically, and chooses the matching recovery mouth.\nWith the drone, you no longer have to drag every part to a recovery mouth yourself. However, the drone does not judge for you whether a part has been made safe, and does not check how a part should be sorted for recovery.\nSo before marking, you still need to confirm that the part has had the necessary handling such as power-off and draining, and check that the recovery target is right, and only then hand it to the drone to carry.

[codex:cx_s_impact]
中：撞击
英：IMPACT

[codex:cx_s_impact_a]
中：在失重环境中，漂浮的零件、无人机或者整段船体，都有可能在移动过程中撞上附近的设备或其他船体结构。
英：In weightlessness, floating parts, the drone or a whole section of hull may hit nearby equipment or other hull structure as they move.

[codex:cx_s_impact_b]
中：发生撞击以后，游戏会根据碰撞时的速度变化和零件的易损程度，降低双方的完好度。精密设备比较容易受到损坏，即使碰撞速度不快，也可能造成很大的损失。
英：After an impact, the game lowers the condition of both sides according to the change in speed at the collision and how easily each part is damaged. Precision equipment is easily damaged, and even a slow collision can cause great loss.

[codex:cx_s_impact_c]
中：使用抓钩拖运零件的时候，尽量控制移动速度，接近目标位置之前就提前减速。尤其是搬运大型设备时，要避免零件脱离控制后继续漂移。
英：When towing parts with the grapple, keep your speed under control and start slowing down before you reach the target position. Especially when carrying large equipment, do not let a part get out of control and keep drifting.

[codex:cx_s_impact_x]
中：撞击造成的损坏主要取决于碰撞前后的速度变化（Δv），以及零件本身的易损程度。\n同样是以 1 m/s 的速度发生碰撞，普通钢结构通常不会受到太大影响，但光学玻璃、芯片等精密设备就可能出现严重损坏，甚至直接失去回收价值。\n拖运重型设备的时候，还要注意它们的惯性。设备的质量越大，想要让它停下来就越困难。如果发现自己来不及减速，直接松开抓钩只会让情况更加危险，因为设备还会保持原来的动量继续移动，直到受到其他外力影响。\n所以搬运大型零件时，最好提前减速，并且一直保持抓钩连接，直到设备安全进入回收区域。
英：The damage an impact causes mainly depends on the change in speed (Δv) before and after the collision, and on how easily the part is damaged.\nIn a collision at the same 1 m/s, ordinary steel structure is usually not much affected, but precision equipment such as optical glass and chips may be seriously damaged, and may even lose all recovery value.\nWhen towing heavy equipment, also watch its inertia. The greater the mass of the equipment, the harder it is to stop. If you find you cannot slow down in time, simply releasing the grapple only makes things more dangerous, because the equipment keeps its original momentum and carries on moving until some other force acts on it.\nSo when carrying large parts, it is best to slow down early and keep the grapple attached until the equipment is safely inside the recovery area.

[codex:cx_swb]
中：配电盘
英：SWITCHBOARD

[codex:cx_swb_a]
中：配电盘负责把船上的电力分配到不同设备和线路，内部装有母排、仪表和一排断路器，可以分别控制各条线路的供电。
英：A switchboard distributes the ship’s power to different equipment and lines. Inside it are busbars, meters and a row of circuit breakers, which control the supply to each line separately.

[codex:cx_swb_b]
中：配电盘连接着船上的多条供电线路，只要还有一路电源没有切断，内部就可能仍然带电。即使已经关闭了所有断路器，也不能直接拆除配电盘。
英：A switchboard is connected to many of the ship’s supply lines, and as long as one supply is not cut, the inside may still be live. Even after all the circuit breakers are switched off, the switchboard cannot be removed directly.

[codex:cx_swb_c]
中：拆除配电盘之前，先切断船上所有的电源，再通过配电盘上的验电点检查电压。确认读数已经降到 0 V 以后，才能开始拆解。
英：Before removing a switchboard, cut all the ship’s supplies first, then check the voltage at the switchboard’s test point. Start dismantling only after the reading has dropped to 0 V.

[codex:cx_swb_x]
中：配电盘的上半部分是三相母排室，负责连接和分配电力。下半部分分成多个区域，每个区域都安装了仪表板和断路器，供电电缆则通过底部的电缆密封板接入。\n需要注意的是，断路器通常只能切断通往设备的负载侧线路。如果母排上游还有任何一路电源没有关闭，配电盘内部就仍然可能带电，所以不能只看断路器是否关闭，还要检查所有电源有没有彻底断开。\n带电拆解配电盘非常危险，如果工具导致母排之间发生短路，就可能产生温度高达上万摄氏度的电弧，严重损坏周围设备。\n拆解之前一定要完成断电和验电，确认电压为 0 V 以后再动手。
英：The upper half of a switchboard is the three-phase busbar chamber, which connects and distributes power. The lower half is divided into several sections, each fitted with a meter panel and circuit breakers, and the supply cables come in through the cable gland plate at the bottom.\nNote that a circuit breaker can usually cut only the load-side line to the equipment. If any supply upstream of the busbar has not been switched off, the inside of the switchboard may still be live, so do not go by whether the breakers are off alone, but also check that all supplies are completely disconnected.\nDismantling a switchboard while live is very dangerous. If a tool shorts the busbars together, it may produce an arc at temperatures of over ten thousand degrees Celsius, seriously damaging the surrounding equipment.\nAlways complete the power-off and the voltage test before dismantling, and start work only after confirming the voltage is 0 V.

[codex:cx_battery]
中：电池组
英：BATTERY PACK

[codex:cx_battery_a]
中：船上的电池组由多个串联的电芯模块组成，内部还安装了汇流排、冷却板和电池管理板，主要负责储存电能，并在需要的时候为船上设备供电。
英：The ship’s battery pack is made of several cell modules in series, and inside it also has busbars, cooling plates and a battery management board. It mainly stores electrical energy and supplies the ship’s equipment when needed.

[codex:cx_battery_b]
中：电池组即使已经关闭了外部电源，内部仍然储存着大量电能。切断电源只能让电池停止向外供电，无法消除电芯内部的能量，如果直接切割或者拆开电池组，就可能引发短路甚至起火。
英：Even with the external supply switched off, a battery pack still stores a great deal of energy inside. Cutting the connection only stops the battery supplying power outward and cannot remove the energy inside the cells, and cutting or opening the pack directly may cause a short circuit or even a fire.

[codex:cx_battery_c]
中：拆除电池组之前，先关闭对应的隔离开关，再拔掉连接到船体的输出电缆。确认电池组已经与船体电路断开以后，将整组电池完整拆下来，送进危险品回收口处理。
英：Before removing a battery pack, switch off its isolator first, then unplug the output cable connected to the hull. Once the pack is confirmed disconnected from the ship’s circuits, take the whole pack off intact and send it to the hazard mouth for handling.

[codex:cx_battery_x]
中：一组容量为 50 kWh 的锂电池，在充满电以后可以储存约 180 MJ 的能量，即使已经切断了外部电源，这些能量也仍然保存在电芯内部。\n如果拆解时刺穿电芯，或者让内部电路发生短路，就可能引发热失控。在严重的情况下，电芯温度可能在几秒内升高到 600°C 以上，同时释放大量可燃气体，甚至引燃附近的其他电芯。\n电池组的主接触器一般安装在模块的一端，断开以后可以停止向外部设备供电，但电池内部的正负极之间仍然存在电压。\n所以拆解电池组的时候，一定要先断开隔离开关和输出电缆，之后再将整个电池组送去回收，不要直接切割内部结构。
英：A lithium battery pack with a capacity of 50 kWh can store about 180 MJ of energy when fully charged, and even after the external supply is cut, this energy is still kept inside the cells.\nIf a cell is punctured during dismantling, or the internal circuit is shorted, thermal runaway may result. In serious cases the cell temperature can rise above 600°C within seconds, releasing a lot of flammable gas and even igniting other cells nearby.\nThe pack’s main contactor is usually fitted at one end of the module. Once opened, it stops supplying power to external equipment, but there is still voltage between the pack’s own positive and negative terminals.\nSo when dismantling a battery pack, always disconnect the isolator and the output cable first, and then send the whole pack for recovery, and do not cut the internal structure directly.

[codex:cx_cap]
中：电容组
英：CAPACITOR BANK

[codex:cx_cap_a]
中：电容组由多个电容罐、汇流排和放电电阻组成，主要用来储存电能，在发射系统等设备需要大量电力的时候，可以在短时间内释放储存的能量。
英：A capacitor bank is made of several capacitor cans, busbars and a discharge resistor. It mainly stores electrical energy, and can release the stored energy in a short time when equipment such as launch systems needs a lot of power.

[codex:cx_cap_b]
中：即使已经切断了船体电源，电容组内部也可能保留着很高的电压。如果没有提前放电就直接拆解，工具接触到带电部位时可能产生电弧，造成严重损坏。
英：Even after the ship’s power is cut, a capacitor bank may keep a very high voltage inside. If you dismantle it without discharging first, a tool touching a live part may produce an arc and cause serious damage.

[codex:cx_cap_c]
中：拆除电容组之前，先找到对应的放电接口，使用放电装置把剩余电压降到 0 V，再通过验电点确认没有残余电压。完成放电和验电以后，才能开始拆解。
英：Before removing a capacitor bank, find its discharge port, use the discharge device to bring the remaining voltage down to 0 V, and then confirm at the test point that no residual voltage is left. Start dismantling only after discharging and testing are done.

[codex:cx_cap_x]
中：电容组储存的能量可以通过 E = ½CV² 计算，其中 C 是电容，V 是电压。一组电容为 0.1 F、电压为 400 V 的设备，就可以储存 8 kJ 的能量，而大型发射系统使用的电容组，储存的能量甚至可以达到兆焦级别。\n电容组和普通用电设备有所不同，关闭电源以后，已经储存的电能不会立即消失，必须通过放电才能降低内部电压。\n放电电阻可以让电容组逐渐释放电能，并将这些能量转化成热量。放电速度取决于电阻和电容形成的 RC 时间常数，通常经过大约五个时间常数以后，剩余电压就会降到初始值的 1% 以下。\n所以即使船上的电源已经全部关闭，也要单独检查电容组的电压，确认完成放电以后再进行拆解。
英：The energy a capacitor bank stores can be calculated with E = ½CV², where C is the capacitance and V is the voltage. A unit of 0.1 F at 400 V can store 8 kJ of energy, and the capacitor banks used by large launch systems can store energy even on the megajoule scale.\nA capacitor bank differs from ordinary electrical equipment: after the power is switched off, the energy already stored does not vanish at once, and it has to be discharged to bring the internal voltage down.\nA discharge resistor lets the capacitor bank release its energy gradually and turns that energy into heat. The discharge speed depends on the RC time constant formed by the resistor and the capacitor, and usually after about five time constants the remaining voltage drops below 1% of its initial value.\nSo even when all the ship’s power is off, check the capacitor bank’s voltage separately, and dismantle it only after confirming the discharge is complete.

[codex:cx_gen]
中：发电机组
英：GENERATING SET

[codex:cx_gen_a]
中：发电机组通过发动机带动发电机运转，为船上的设备提供电力。整套机组还安装了散热器、励磁机和控制屏，用来维持发电机的正常运行。
英：A generator set uses an engine to drive a generator and supplies power to the ship’s equipment. The whole set also has a radiator, an exciter and a control panel, which keep the generator running properly.

[codex:cx_gen_b]
中：发电机停止运行以后，出线端仍然可能带电。如果船上的其他电源还连接着供电总线，电流就可能通过总线反向进入发电机的线路，所以不能只看发动机有没有停止运行。
英：After a generator stops running, its output terminals may still be live. If other supplies on the ship are still connected to the supply bus, current may flow back through the bus into the generator’s lines, so do not go only by whether the engine has stopped.

[codex:cx_gen_c]
中：拆除发电机组之前，先确认发动机已经完全停止运行，再断开连接供电总线的输出线路。完成断电以后，还需要检查出线端的电压，确认没有残余电压后再进行拆解。
英：Before removing a generator set, first confirm the engine has stopped completely, then disconnect the output line to the supply bus. After the power is cut, also check the voltage at the output terminals, and dismantle only after confirming no residual voltage.

[codex:cx_gen_x]
中：发电机内部的转子装有凸极磁极，定子槽内则安装了用于发电的绕组。发动机带动转子旋转以后，发电机就可以产生电力。\n即使已经关闭了励磁系统，转子内部仍然可能保留一定的剩余磁性。如果转子继续旋转，发电机就可能产生几十伏的电压，所以停机以后还要等待转子完全停止，并检查线路是否带电。\n一套发电机组通常重达 1–3 吨，底座上还安装了减振器，用来减少运行时产生的振动。固定螺栓一般集中在机组底座上，拆解时需要先找到这些连接位置。\n由于整套设备的重量比较大，拆除底座螺栓之前，最好先用抓钩固定住机组，避免最后一个固定点松开以后，发电机组失去控制并撞上附近的设备。
英：Inside the generator, the rotor carries salient magnetic poles, and the stator slots hold the windings used to generate power. Once the engine drives the rotor round, the generator can produce electricity.\nEven with the excitation system switched off, the rotor may keep some residual magnetism. If the rotor keeps turning, the generator may produce tens of volts, so after shutdown you also need to wait until the rotor has stopped completely and check whether the lines are live.\nA generator set usually weighs 1–3 t, and the base also carries anti-vibration mounts to reduce vibration while running. The fixing bolts are generally concentrated on the base of the set, and you need to find these connection points first when dismantling.\nBecause the whole unit is heavy, before removing the base bolts it is best to secure the set with the grapple first, so that once the last fixing point is loose, the generator set does not get out of control and hit nearby equipment.

[codex:cx_panel]
中：控制箱
英：CONTROL ENCLOSURE

[codex:cx_panel_a]
中：安装在墙上的电气控制箱，外面有箱门和指示灯观察窗，里面装着端子排，电缆通过格兰头接入箱体。
英：An electrical control box mounted on a wall. It has a door and an indicator viewing window on the outside, terminal blocks inside, and cables enter the box through glands.

[codex:cx_panel_b]
