[codex:cx_panel_c]
中：先对照工程图，找到所有接入控制箱的电源线路，把每一路电源都断开并确认没有电压以后，再开始拆除控制箱。
英：First check the engineering drawing to find all the supply lines coming into the control box, disconnect every supply and confirm there is no voltage, and then start removing the control box.

[codex:cx_panel_x]
中：控制箱里面一般装着继电器、PLC 和端子排，这些设备本身的回收价值不算高，但连接的线路比较多。最好在拆除控制箱之前就把所有进线处理好，这样拆下来以后也不用再花时间逐一查找线路。
英：A control box generally holds relays, a PLC and terminal blocks. The recovery value of this equipment is not high, but it has many connected lines. It is best to deal with all the incoming lines before removing the control box, so that you need not spend time looking for the lines one by one after it is removed.

[codex:cx_junction]
中：接线盒
英：JUNCTION BOX

[codex:cx_junction_a]
中：接线盒一般装在电缆连接的位置，盒盖四角有固定螺钉，内部有接地螺柱，电缆通过格兰头接入和引出。
英：A junction box is usually fitted where cables connect. The lid has fixing screws at the four corners, there is an earth stud inside, and cables enter and leave through glands.

[codex:cx_junction_b]
中：多条电缆会在这里连接和分支，接线盒上通常还设有验电点或放电点。拆除之前要确认所有相关线路都已经断电。
英：Several cables connect and branch here, and the junction box usually also has a test point or discharge point. Before removal, confirm that all the related lines are de-energised.

[codex:cx_junction_c]
中：先找到接线盒对应的验电点和放电点，按照操作要求完成验电和放电，确认安全以后，再处理与它连接的设备。
英：First find the test point and discharge point for the junction box, complete the voltage test and discharge as required, and after confirming it is safe, deal with the equipment connected to it.

[codex:cx_junction_x]
中：接线盒主要用来连接和分配电缆线路，一台设备的电源可能要经过好几个接线盒才能接到供电端。如果不清楚设备的电源从哪里接入，可以先找到对应的接线盒，再沿着电缆逐段往回查找，这样通常更容易找到供电线路。
英：A junction box is mainly used to connect and distribute cable lines, and the supply of one piece of equipment may pass through several junction boxes before it reaches the supply end. If you do not know where a piece of equipment’s power comes in, first find the matching junction box and trace back along the cable section by section, which usually makes it easier to find the supply line.

[codex:cx_transformer]
中：变压器
英：TRANSFORMER

[codex:cx_transformer_a]
中：这是一台干式变压器，主要由三柱铁芯、高低压绕组和外部的夹紧框架组成，铁芯和绕组都固定在框架里面。
英：This is a dry-type transformer. It mainly consists of a three-limb core, high- and low-voltage windings and an outer clamping frame, and the core and windings are fixed inside the frame.

[codex:cx_transformer_b]
中：变压器的高低压两侧都可能带电，即使高压侧已经断开，如果低压侧还有电源接入，变压器仍然可能把低压升成高压，所以不能只断开其中一侧。
英：Both the high-voltage and low-voltage sides of a transformer may be live. Even if the high-voltage side is disconnected, if a supply is still connected on the low-voltage side, the transformer may step the low voltage up to high voltage, so you cannot disconnect only one side.

[codex:cx_transformer_c]
中：拆除之前，要把高压侧和低压侧的电源全部断开，分别验电并确认没有电压，完成必要的隔离和放电以后，再开始拆除。
英：Before removal, disconnect all the supplies on both the high-voltage and low-voltage sides, test each for voltage and confirm there is none, and start removal after the necessary isolation and discharge are done.

[codex:cx_transformer_x]
中：变压器通过高低压绕组的匝数比例来改变电压。拿匝数比为 10:1 的变压器来说，如果从低压侧接入 400 V，高压侧就可能产生约 4 kV 的电压。\n干式变压器通常比较重，其中铁芯占了很大一部分重量。拆除的时候可以根据夹紧框架的结构，把铁芯和其他部件分开处理，方便后续搬运。
英：A transformer changes voltage through the turns ratio of its high- and low-voltage windings. Take a transformer with a turns ratio of 10:1: if 400 V is fed in from the low-voltage side, the high-voltage side may produce about 4 kV.\nA dry-type transformer is usually heavy, and the core makes up a large part of the weight. When removing it, you can use the structure of the clamping frame to separate the core from the other parts, which makes later carrying easier.

[codex:cx_inverter]
中：逆变器
英：INVERTER

[codex:cx_inverter_a]
中：逆变器内部主要装着 IGBT 功率模块、散热器、直流母线电容和输出电抗器，这些部件共同负责把直流电转换成电机需要的交流电。
英：An inverter mainly contains IGBT power modules, a heat sink, DC-link capacitors and an output choke. Together these parts convert DC into the AC that a motor needs.

[codex:cx_inverter_b]
中：逆变器断开电源以后，内部的直流母线电容可能还存有高压电，需要经过几分钟才能逐渐放电。即使设备已经停止运行，也不能直接拆除。
英：After an inverter is disconnected from the power, the DC-link capacitors inside may still hold high voltage, and take a few minutes to discharge gradually. Even if the equipment has stopped running, it must not be removed directly.

[codex:cx_inverter_c]
中：先断开逆变器的所有输入电源，等待电容完成放电，必要时通过指定的放电点进行放电。确认直流母线没有残余电压以后，再开始拆除设备。
英：First disconnect all the inverter’s input supplies and wait for the capacitors to discharge, and if needed discharge them through the designated discharge point. Start removing the equipment only after confirming the DC bus has no residual voltage.

[codex:cx_inverter_x]
中：逆变器通过 IGBT 模块的快速开关，把直流电转换成交流电，用来控制电机运行。内部的直流母线电容负责储存电能，让供电电压保持稳定。\n这类设备的直流母线电压经常达到 600–800 V，即使已经断开电源，电容里也可能残留危险电压。设备通常装有泄放电阻，但一般需要等待几分钟才能完成放电，具体时间要看设备型号，拆除前仍然需要实际测量确认。
英：An inverter converts DC into AC by fast switching of its IGBT modules, and uses it to control motor running. The DC-link capacitors inside store the electrical energy and keep the supply voltage steady.\nThe DC bus voltage of this kind of equipment often reaches 600–800 V, and even after the supply is disconnected, a dangerous voltage may remain in the capacitors. The equipment usually has bleed resistors, but it generally takes a few minutes to finish discharging, and the exact time depends on the model, so you still need to measure and confirm before removal.

[codex:cx_fuelcell]
中：燃料电池
英：FUEL CELL STACK

[codex:cx_fuelcell_a]
中：燃料电池内部有上百片双极板，整组电池由两端的端板和拉杆固定在一起，旁边还配有鼓风机和加湿器，用来维持电池运行时需要的空气供应和湿度。
英：Inside a fuel cell there are hundreds of bipolar plates. The whole stack is held together by end plates and tie rods at the two ends, and beside it are a blower and a humidifier that keep up the air supply and humidity the cell needs while running.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_b]
中：只要内部还有氢气和氧气，燃料电池就可能继续发电。即使已经断开了主电源线路，电池本身也可能带电，里面残留的氢气还有泄漏和起火的危险。
英：As long as hydrogen and oxygen remain inside, a fuel cell can keep generating power. Even after the main supply line is disconnected, the cell itself may be live, and the hydrogen left inside can leak and catch fire.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_c]
中：拆除之前，先断开燃料电池自己的电源开关，关闭燃料供应阀门，再按照设备要求排出内部残留的氢气。确认燃料供应已经隔离、内部没有危险的氢气残留，并完成验电以后，才能开始拆除。
英：Before removal, switch off the fuel cell at its own power switch, shut the fuel supply valve, then vent the hydrogen left inside as the equipment requires. Start removal only after confirming the fuel supply is isolated, no dangerous hydrogen remains inside, and the voltage test is done.

[codex:cx_fuelcell_x]
中：燃料电池通过氢气和氧气发生电化学反应来产生电能，每片电池工作时的电压大约是 0.7 V，几百片串联起来以后，总电压就可能达到几百伏。\n即使关闭了燃料阀门，电池内部仍然可能残留氢气。如果氢气泄漏到舱室里面，浓度达到空气体积的 4% 到 75% 时就可能燃烧，而且只需要很小的点火能量就能引燃，所以拆除之前一定要确认氢气已经安全排出。
英：A fuel cell produces electricity through an electrochemical reaction between hydrogen and oxygen. Each cell works at about 0.7 V, and with several hundred in series the total voltage can reach several hundred volts.\nEven with the fuel valve closed, hydrogen may remain inside the cell. If hydrogen leaks into a room and its concentration reaches 4% to 75% of the air by volume, it can burn, and a very small ignition energy is enough to light it, so always confirm the hydrogen has been safely vented before removal.

[codex:cx_charger]
中：充电桩
英：CHARGING STATION

[codex:cx_charger_a]
中：充电桩主要用来给无人机或车辆充电，通常配有充电枪头和连接线缆，可以将外部电源接入需要充电的设备。
英：A charging unit is mainly used to charge drones or vehicles. It usually comes with a charging connector and cable, and can feed an external supply into the equipment to be charged.

[codex:cx_charger_b]
中：充电桩连接着充电设备时，充电线缆的两端都可能带电。即使已经断开充电桩的电源，被充电设备内部的电池也可能还有电压。
英：While a charging unit is connected to equipment being charged, both ends of the charging cable may be live. Even after the charging unit’s supply is disconnected, the battery inside the equipment being charged may still have voltage.

[codex:cx_charger_c]
中：先关闭充电开关，断开充电桩的供电并确认已经停止充电，再按照要求拔下充电线缆。确认连接已经断开以后，才能继续处理被充电的设备。
英：First switch off the charging switch, disconnect the charging unit’s supply and confirm charging has stopped, then unplug the charging cable as required. Carry on with the equipment that was being charged only after confirming the connection is cut.

[codex:cx_charger_x]
中：充电桩从供电总线获取电能，再通过充电线缆把电能输送给连接的设备。如果设备内部装有电池，即使充电桩已经断电，电池本身仍然储存着电能，所以拔掉充电线缆以后，设备的电池一侧也可能继续带电，拆除时需要单独检查。
英：A charging unit takes power from the supply bus and sends it to the connected equipment through the charging cable. If the equipment has a battery inside, that battery still stores energy even after the charging unit loses power, so after the charging cable is unplugged, the battery side of the equipment may remain live, and it needs to be checked separately during removal.

[codex:cx_dump_res]
中：泄能电阻
英：DUMP RESISTOR

[codex:cx_dump_res_a]
中：泄能电阻由一排排反复折叠的不锈钢电阻带组成，连接在超导磁体的泄能回路上，主要用来消耗磁体储存的能量。
英：A dump resistor is made of rows of stainless steel resistor ribbon folded back and forth. It is connected to the dump circuit of a superconducting magnet and mainly consumes the energy the magnet has stored.

[codex:cx_dump_res_b]
中：磁体泄能时，大量电能会通过电阻转化成热量，满负荷运行时，电阻带甚至会被加热到发红。如果提前拆除泄能电阻，磁体就会失去正常的泄能通道，可能造成严重事故。
英：When a magnet dumps its energy, a great deal of electrical energy is turned into heat in the resistor, and at full load the resistor ribbon can even be heated until it glows red. If the dump resistor is removed too early, the magnet loses its normal dump path, which may cause a serious accident.

[codex:cx_dump_res_c]
中：先确认磁体已经完成泄能，等回路电流降到安全范围，并按照要求完成隔离、验电和放电以后，才能拆除泄能电阻及其连接的母排。
英：First confirm the magnet has finished dumping its energy and the circuit current has dropped to a safe range, and complete the isolation, voltage test and discharge as required, and only then remove the dump resistor and the busbars connected to it.

[codex:cx_dump_res_x]
中：磁体泄能时，线圈中的电流会经过泄能电阻逐渐减小，计算公式为 I = I₀·e^(−t/τ)，其中 τ = L/R。电阻越大，电流下降得越快，但泄能时产生的电压也会更高，可以用 U = I·R 计算。在大型超导磁体中，几十吉焦的储能可能在几十秒内转化成热量，电阻带的温度也可能升高到几百度。
英：When a magnet dumps its energy, the current in the coil gradually falls through the dump resistor, calculated as I = I₀·e^(−t/τ), where τ = L/R. The larger the resistance, the faster the current falls, but the voltage produced during the dump is also higher, and can be calculated as U = I·R. In a large superconducting magnet, tens of gigajoules of stored energy may be turned into heat within tens of seconds, and the temperature of the resistor ribbon may also rise to several hundred degrees.

[codex:cx_slip_ring]
中：滑环
英：SLIP RING

[codex:cx_slip_ring_a]
中：滑环由安装在旋转部分的一组铜环和固定部分的电刷组成，主要用来给不断旋转的居住环供电，让居住环在转动的时候也能正常使用电力。
英：A slip ring is made of a set of copper rings on the rotating part and brushes on the fixed part. It mainly supplies power to a habitat ring that keeps rotating, so the habitat ring can use electricity normally while it turns.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_b]
中：如果居住环还在通电，就直接启动刹车，电机在减速时产生的回馈电流可能会经过滑环。电流过大时，滑环内部可能出现电弧，严重时还会烧坏铜环和电刷。
英：If you start the brake while the habitat ring is still powered, the regenerated current the motor produces while slowing down may pass through the slip ring. If the current is too large, an arc may form inside the slip ring, and in serious cases it burns out the copper rings and brushes.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_c]
中：先断开居住环上的用电负载，确认相关电路已经安全隔离以后，再启动制动系统，让居住环逐渐停止旋转。
英：First disconnect the electrical loads on the habitat ring, and after confirming the related circuits are safely isolated, start the braking system and let the habitat ring gradually stop rotating.

[codex:cx_slip_ring_x]
中：滑环依靠电刷与铜环之间的接触来传输电流，即使居住环一直旋转，也能保持电力连接。不过电刷能够承受的电流密度有限，如果回馈电流过大，接触位置就可能产生电弧，在铜环表面留下烧蚀和凹坑。滑环还是这座旋转居住环唯一的供电通路，一旦损坏，整个居住环就可能失去电力供应。
英：A slip ring carries current through contact between the brushes and the copper rings, so the electrical connection holds even while the habitat ring keeps rotating. However, the current density the brushes can bear is limited, and if the regenerated current is too large, an arc may form at the contact point and leave burn marks and pits on the copper ring surface. The slip ring is also the only supply path for this rotating habitat ring, and if it is damaged, the whole habitat ring may lose its power supply.

[codex:cx_tray]
中：电缆桥架
英：CABLE TRAY

[codex:cx_tray_a]
中：安装在顶板下方的梯式电缆桥架，里面铺设着成捆的电缆，附近通常还安装着照明灯具和消防喷头。
英：A ladder-type cable tray mounted under the deckhead, with bundles of cable laid in it, and lamps and fire sprinklers usually fitted nearby.

[codex:cx_tray_b]
中：桥架里面可能有多条不同的供电线路，只要其中还有一根电缆带电，就不能直接切割整捆电缆，否则可能造成短路或触电事故。
英：A tray may hold several different supply lines, and as long as one cable in it is still live, you cannot cut the whole bundle directly, or it may cause a short circuit or an electric shock.

[codex:cx_tray_c]
中：先找到桥架内所有电缆对应的电路，逐一断开电源并确认没有电压以后，再拆除电缆和桥架。
英：First find the circuits that all the cables in the tray belong to, disconnect each supply and confirm there is no voltage, and then remove the cables and the tray.

[codex:cx_tray_x]
中：电缆桥架本身只是用来固定和支撑电缆的金属结构，真正需要注意的是里面的线路。动力电缆、信号电缆和控制电缆可能铺设在同一座桥架里，所以拆除之前要按照工程图逐一确认每条线路的连接情况，完成必要的断电和安全检查以后，再进行拆除。
英：The cable tray itself is only a metal structure that holds and supports the cables, and what really needs care is the lines inside. Power cables, signal cables and control cables may be laid in the same tray, so before removal, check the connections of every line against the engineering drawing, and remove them after the necessary power-off and safety checks.

[codex:cx_module]
中：可更换单元（ORU）
英：ORBITAL REPLACEMENT UNIT

[codex:cx_module_a]
中：可更换单元是一种可以整体拆卸和更换的密封设备箱，外面装有两个方便抓握的把手，通过自锁螺钉固定在安装位置上，背面还有可以直接插拔的连接接头。
英：A replaceable unit is a sealed equipment box that can be removed and replaced as a whole. It has two easy-to-grip handles on the outside, is held in place by captive screws, and has plug-in connectors on the back that can be pulled out directly.

[codex:cx_module_b]
中：设备背面的接头直接连接着安装背板，如果还没有解除固定就强行拔出，可能会损坏背板上的接头，影响后续设备的安装和使用。
英：The connectors on the back of the equipment connect straight into the mounting backplane. If you pull it out by force before releasing it, you may damage the connectors on the backplane, affecting later installation and use of equipment.

[codex:cx_module_c]
中：先断开设备电源，确认安全以后，再松开固定设备的自锁螺钉，握住把手将整个单元拔出来，尽量保持设备和接头的完整。
英：First disconnect the equipment’s power, and once it is safe, loosen the captive screws that hold the equipment, grip the handles and pull the whole unit out, keeping the equipment and its connectors intact as far as possible.

[codex:cx_module_x]
中：ORU采用的是航天器上常见的整体更换设计，宇航员即使戴着厚重的手套，也能通过把手和自锁螺钉完成拆装，部分设备还可以直接由机械臂操作。由于不需要拆开内部零件，整个单元就能取下来，所以这类设备通常比较容易拆卸，只要没有损坏，完整回收的价值也比较高。
英：The ORU uses the whole-unit replacement design common on spacecraft. Even an astronaut in thick gloves can fit and remove it with the handles and captive screws, and some units can also be handled directly by a mechanical arm. Because the internal parts need not be taken apart, the whole unit can be lifted out, so this kind of equipment is usually fairly easy to remove, and if it is undamaged, its value when recovered whole is also fairly high.

[codex:cx_pump]
中：离心泵组
英：CENTRIFUGAL PUMP SET

[codex:cx_pump_a]
中：离心泵组一般由泵壳、轴承架、联轴器和电机组成，这些部件都安装在同一个底座上。泵壳通常采用蜗壳结构，用来输送管道里的液体。
英：A centrifugal pump set generally consists of a pump casing, a bearing frame, a coupling and a motor, all mounted on the same baseplate. The pump casing is usually a volute, used to move the liquid in the line.

[codex:cx_pump_b]
中：泵组虽然已经停止运行，但进出口管道里可能还存有液体，电机也可能没有断电。直接切断管道会造成液体泄漏，带电拆除电线还可能产生电弧。
英：Although the pump set has stopped running, the inlet and outlet lines may still hold liquid, and the motor may not be de-energised. Cutting a line directly causes a liquid leak, and removing the wires while live may also produce an arc.

[codex:cx_pump_c]
中：先切断电机电源，再通过排放口把泵组和连接管道里的液体排空，确认没有残余压力以后，断开电缆和进出口管道，最后把整套泵组从底座上拆下来。
英：First cut the motor’s power, then drain the liquid in the pump set and the connected lines through the drain, confirm there is no residual pressure, disconnect the cable and the inlet and outlet lines, and finally take the whole pump set off its baseplate.

[codex:cx_pump_x]
中：离心泵通过电机带动叶轮旋转，让液体向泵壳外侧流动，再利用蜗壳把液体的部分流动速度转化成压力。运行时需要有液体提供冷却和润滑，如果泵内没有液体还继续运转，机械密封可能在几十秒内就因为过热而损坏。完整泵组的回收价值通常比直接拆成废金属高得多。
英：A centrifugal pump has a motor turn an impeller, which throws the liquid towards the outside of the pump casing, and the volute then turns part of the liquid’s speed into pressure. While running it needs liquid for cooling and lubrication, and if the pump keeps running with no liquid inside, the mechanical seal may be damaged by overheating within tens of seconds. A whole pump set is usually worth much more when recovered than when broken down into scrap metal.

[codex:cx_hx]
中：管壳式换热器
英：SHELL-AND-TUBE HEAT EXCHANGER

[codex:cx_hx_a]
中：管壳式换热器里面装着一组换热管，一种液体从管子内部流过，另一种液体则从管子外面的壳体空间流过。两种液体通过管壁传递热量，正常情况下不会直接混合。
英：A shell-and-tube heat exchanger holds a bundle of heat exchange tubes. One liquid flows through the inside of the tubes, and another flows through the shell space outside the tubes. The two liquids pass heat through the tube walls, and normally do not mix directly.

[codex:cx_hx_b]
中：换热器内部有两套独立的液体管路，分别叫作管程和壳程。就算其中一侧已经排空，另一侧也可能还存有液体和压力，如果直接拆除管道，就可能造成液体泄漏。
英：A heat exchanger has two independent sets of liquid lines inside, called the tube side and the shell side. Even if one side has been emptied, the other side may still hold liquid and pressure, and removing the pipes directly may cause a liquid leak.

[codex:cx_hx_c]
中：先分别检查管程和壳程，确认两侧都已经隔离并完成泄压，再把内部残留的液体全部排空。确认安全以后，逐一拆除连接的管道，最后再拆下整台换热器。
英：First check the tube side and the shell side separately, confirm both sides are isolated and vented, and then drain all the liquid left inside. Once it is safe, remove the connected pipes one by one, and finally take off the whole heat exchanger.

[codex:cx_hx_x]
中：换热器内部装有折流板，可以改变壳程液体的流动方向，让液体反复经过换热管束，提高热量传递的效率。由于管程和壳程的压力、温度以及内部介质可能各不相同，拆除时需要把四个管口分别检查和处理，不能因为其中一侧已经排空，就认为整台换热器都已经安全。
英：A heat exchanger has baffles inside which change the flow direction of the shell-side liquid and make it cross the tube bundle repeatedly, raising the efficiency of heat transfer. Because the pressure, temperature and medium inside the tube side and the shell side may all differ, the four nozzles need to be checked and handled separately when removing it, and you cannot assume the whole heat exchanger is safe just because one side has been emptied.

[codex:cx_radiator]
中：散热板
英：RADIATOR PANEL

[codex:cx_radiator_a]
中：散热板的两端装有集管，中间排列着大量冷却液流道管。冷却液经过这些管道时，会把热量传递到散热板表面，再通过热辐射把热量排放到太空中。
英：A radiator panel has headers at both ends and a large number of coolant flow tubes arranged in between. As coolant passes through these tubes, it passes heat to the panel surface, and the heat is then radiated into space.

[codex:cx_radiator_b]
中：散热板的管道里可能还存有冷却液，刚停止运行时，板面温度也可能很高。如果还没有完成冷却就直接收拢或者拆除，散热板可能因为受热不均而发生变形。
英：The tubes of a radiator panel may still hold coolant, and just after it stops running, the panel surface may also be very hot. If you fold or remove it before it has cooled, the radiator panel may deform from uneven heating.

[codex:cx_radiator_c]
中：先让冷却回路继续运行一段时间，等散热板的温度降下来以后，再把管道里的冷却液排空。确认温度和管道状态都正常以后，就可以拆除散热板了。
英：First let the cooling loop keep running for a while, and once the radiator panel’s temperature has dropped, drain the coolant out of the tubes. After confirming the temperature and the state of the tubes are both normal, you can remove the radiator panel.

[codex:cx_radiator_x]
中：太空中没有空气，散热板只能依靠热辐射把热量排出去，散热功率可以用 P = εσAT⁴ 计算。在理想条件下，温度为 300 K 的散热板，每平方米大约能辐射 400 W 的热量，所以航天器通常需要安装面积很大的散热板。\n散热板本身比较薄，重量也比较轻，一般通过铰链固定在支撑吊杆上。拆卸和搬运的时候要控制好力度，避免把板面或者连接部位弄坏。
英：There is no air in space, so a radiator panel can only shed heat by thermal radiation, and the radiated power can be calculated as P = εσAT⁴. Under ideal conditions, a panel at 300 K radiates about 400 W of heat per square metre, which is why spacecraft usually need radiator panels of large area.\nThe panel itself is fairly thin and light, and is generally held on its supporting boom by hinges. When removing and moving it, control your force, so you do not damage the panel surface or the connection points.

[codex:cx_chiller]
中：制冷机组
英：CHILLER

[codex:cx_chiller_a]
中：制冷机组主要由压缩机、冷凝器、膨胀阀和蒸发器组成，这些设备通过管道连接起来，形成一套完整的制冷系统，负责给货舱或其他设备降温。
英：A chiller set consists mainly of a compressor, a condenser, an expansion valve and an evaporator. They are joined by pipes into a complete refrigeration system that cools cargo holds and other equipment.

[codex:cx_chiller_b]
中：有些货舱和设备需要一直保持低温，如果提前关闭制冷机组，或者在拆除过程中损坏了制冷管道，原本冷却的区域就会逐渐升温，里面的货物和设备可能因此受损。
英：Some cargo holds and equipment must be kept cold all the time. If you switch off the chiller set too early, or damage the refrigeration lines during removal, the area that was cooled gradually warms up, and the cargo and equipment inside may be damaged.

[codex:cx_chiller_c]
中：拆除之前，先确认制冷机组还连接着哪些货舱或设备。如果这些地方仍然需要保持低温，就先接通船坞的临时供冷系统，或者把需要冷藏的货物处理好，确认不再需要原来的制冷机组以后，再停止运行并准备拆除。
英：Before removal, check which cargo holds or equipment the chiller set still serves. If they must stay cold, connect the berth’s temporary cooling first or deal with the chilled cargo; stop and remove the set only once it is no longer needed.

[codex:cx_chiller_x]
中：制冷机组通过制冷剂循环来转移热量。制冷剂在蒸发器里吸收热量并蒸发成气体，经过压缩机压缩以后，再进入冷凝器释放热量，重新变成液体，随后经过膨胀阀降压，继续下一轮循环。拆除时还要通过储液罐的回收接口，把管道里的制冷剂回收到专用容器中，不能直接排放到外部环境。
英：A chiller set moves heat by circulating a refrigerant. The refrigerant absorbs heat in the evaporator and evaporates into gas, is compressed by the compressor, then enters the condenser, gives off heat and becomes liquid again, and then drops in pressure through the expansion valve and goes on to the next cycle. When removing it, you also need to use the recovery port of the receiver to recover the refrigerant in the lines into a dedicated container, and it cannot be vented directly into the outside environment.

[codex:cx_freezer]
中：超低温冷柜
英：ULTRA-LOW FREEZER

[codex:cx_freezer_a]
中：这种冷柜主要用来保存需要低温储存的样品和药品，内部温度通常保持在 −80 °C 左右。冷柜配有保温门、温度控制器和压缩机舱，可以长时间维持内部的低温环境。
英：This kind of freezer is mainly used to store samples and medicines that need low-temperature storage, and its internal temperature is usually kept at about −80 °C. The freezer has an insulated door, a temperature controller and a compressor bay, and can keep the low temperature inside for a long time.

[codex:cx_freezer_b]
中：冷柜断电以后，内部温度会慢慢升高。有些样品对储存温度要求很严格，只要温度超过规定范围，就可能失去使用价值。
英：After a freezer loses power, the temperature inside slowly rises. Some samples have strict storage temperature requirements, and as soon as the temperature goes beyond the set range, they may lose their value.

[codex:cx_freezer_c]
中：如果冷柜里面还有需要低温保存的样品，就保持供电，把整台冷柜送到设备回收口。如果准备断电拆除，需要先检查内部有没有必须继续冷藏的物品，确认可以断电以后再进行拆除。
英：If the freezer still holds samples that must be kept cold, keep it powered and send the whole freezer to the equipment recovery mouth. If you plan to cut the power and remove it, first check whether anything inside must stay refrigerated, and remove it only after confirming the power can be cut.

[codex:cx_freezer_x]
中：保温性能较好的冷柜在断电以后，内部温度通常每小时会上升几度，具体速度取决于冷柜的保温性能和外部温度。如果打开柜门，温度上升的速度还会明显加快。很多低温样品都有严格的温度限制，只要储存过程中超过规定温度，即使后来重新冷冻，也可能无法继续使用，所以完整保存的冷链样品通常有更高的回收价值。
英：A freezer with good insulation usually warms by a few degrees per hour after losing power, and the exact speed depends on its insulation performance and the outside temperature. If the door is opened, the temperature rises noticeably faster. Many low-temperature samples have strict temperature limits, and once they exceed the set temperature in storage, they may be unusable even if frozen again later, so cold-chain samples kept intact usually have a higher recovery value.

[codex:cx_env]
中：空气处理机
英：AIR HANDLING UNIT

[codex:cx_env_a]
中：空气处理机由进风阀、过滤器、冷却盘管、离心风机和送风箱组成，这些部件按照空气流动的方向依次安装在机组内部。
英：An air handling unit consists of an intake damper, a filter, a cooling coil, a centrifugal fan and a discharge plenum, installed inside the unit one after another in the direction of the air flow.

[codex:cx_env_b]
中：冷却盘管里面可能还留有冷却液，接水盘里也可能有积水。风机通过供电总线连接电源，拆除之前需要确认电源已经断开。
英：The cooling coil may still hold coolant, and the drain pan may hold standing water. The fan is connected to its power through the supply bus, so you need to confirm the power is disconnected before removal.

[codex:cx_env_c]
中：先断开风机电源，再把冷却盘管里的液体排空，确认处理完成以后，就可以拆除连接的风管和底座固定螺栓。
英：First disconnect the fan’s power, then drain the liquid out of the cooling coil, and once that is done, you can remove the connected ducts and the base fixing bolts.

[codex:cx_env_x]
中：空气处理机负责让舱室里的空气不断循环，同时进行降温和除湿，通常也是舱室里体积比较大的设备之一。内部各个部件按照空气流动的方向排列，方便空气依次经过过滤器和冷却盘管。需要注意的是，空气处理机不负责控制舱室的整体气压，如果要给舱室泄压，还得通过专门的舱室接口操作。
英：An air handling unit keeps the air in a room circulating while also cooling and drying it, and is usually one of the larger pieces of equipment in a room. Its internal parts are arranged along the direction of the air flow, so that the air passes through the filter and the cooling coil in turn. Note that the air handling unit does not control the overall pressure of the room, and to vent a room, you still have to use the room’s own dedicated port.

[codex:cx_duct_fan]
中：风道轴流风机
英：AXIAL DUCT FAN

[codex:cx_duct_fan_a]
中：安装在通风管道里的轴流风机，主要由电机、叶轮和导流叶片组成，用来推动空气沿着管道流动。
英：An axial fan fitted in a ventilation duct, consisting mainly of a motor, an impeller and guide vanes, used to drive air along the duct.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_b]
中：如果没有切断电源就拆除接线盒，可能会产生电弧。风机断电以后，叶轮也可能还在继续转动，直接拆除容易发生意外。
英：If you remove the terminal box without cutting the power, an arc may be produced. After the fan loses power, the impeller may also still be turning, and removing it directly easily leads to an accident.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_c]
中：先切断风机电源，确认线路没有电压，再等待叶轮完全停止转动，最后拆除连接法兰，把风机从管道上卸下来。
英：First cut the fan’s power and confirm the line has no voltage, then wait until the impeller has stopped turning completely, and finally remove the connecting flanges and take the fan off the duct.

[codex:cx_duct_fan_x]
中：风机断电以后，叶轮不会立即停止，还会因为惯性继续转动一段时间。如果周围是真空环境，没有空气阻力，叶轮减速的速度就会更慢，所以拆除之前一定要确认叶轮已经完全停止转动。
英：After a fan loses power, the impeller does not stop at once, and keeps turning for a while by inertia. In vacuum there is no air resistance, so the impeller slows down even more slowly, and before removal you must confirm the impeller has stopped turning completely.

[codex:cx_filter]
中：过滤器
英：CARTRIDGE FILTER

[codex:cx_filter_a]
中：过滤器里面装着折叠式滤芯，外壳上有进出口、压差表和排放阀，主要用来过滤管道里的杂质和污染物。
英：A filter holds a pleated element, and the housing has an inlet, an outlet, a differential pressure gauge and a drain valve. It is mainly used to filter impurities and contaminants out of the line.

[codex:cx_filter_b]
中：滤芯使用一段时间以后，会积累大量过滤下来的污染物，其中可能含有有毒物质，甚至带有放射性，拆除的时候要特别注意。
英：After an element has been in use for a while, it collects a lot of the contaminants it has filtered out, which may contain toxic substances and may even be radioactive, so take special care when removing it.

[codex:cx_filter_c]
中：先关闭过滤器连接的管路，确认内部已经卸压，再通过排放阀排空残留物。遇到受到污染的滤芯，要做好密封处理，然后送到危险品回收口。
英：First close the line the filter is connected to, confirm the pressure inside has been relieved, and then drain the residue through the drain valve. A contaminated element must be sealed, and then sent to the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_filter_x]
中：压差表可以显示滤芯进出口之间的压力差，滤芯里面积累的杂质越多，流体通过时受到的阻力就越大，压差通常也会随之增加。隔离舱、核设施和生物舱里的过滤器还可能残留危险物质，所以拆卸下来的滤芯也要按照危险品处理。
英：The differential pressure gauge shows the pressure difference between the element’s inlet and outlet. The more impurities collect in the element, the greater the resistance to the fluid passing through, and the differential pressure usually rises with it. Filters in isolation rooms, nuclear facilities and biological bays may also hold hazardous substances, so removed elements must also be handled as hazardous items.

[codex:cx_trap]
中：疏水器
英：CONDENSATE TRAP

[codex:cx_trap_a]
中：这是一种倒置浮桶式疏水器，安装在蒸汽管路上，主要用来排出管道里积存的冷凝水，同时尽量减少蒸汽流失。
英：This is an inverted-bucket steam trap fitted on a steam line. It is mainly used to discharge condensate that collects in the line, while losing as little steam as possible.

[codex:cx_trap_b]
中：疏水器连接着蒸汽管路，内部可能还有高温蒸汽和残余压力。就算已经停止供汽，也不能直接拆除。
英：A steam trap is connected to a steam line, and may still hold high-temperature steam and residual pressure inside. Even if the steam supply has stopped, it cannot be removed directly.

[codex:cx_trap_c]
中：拆除之前先检查疏水器和管路的温度，再把内部压力释放干净，确认温度和压力都已经降到安全范围以后，才能开始拆除。
英：Before removal, first check the temperature of the steam trap and the line, then release the pressure inside completely, and start removal only after confirming that the temperature and pressure have both dropped to a safe range.

[codex:cx_trap_x]
中：普通倒置浮桶式疏水器利用浮力控制阀门，浮桶里充满蒸汽时会上浮并关闭阀门，冷凝水进入以后，浮桶会逐渐下沉，让阀门重新打开。\n不过在失重环境下，普通浮桶无法依靠浮力正常工作，所以船上的疏水器都经过了专门改造，主要利用管路之间的压力差和流体流动来排出冷凝水。
英：An ordinary inverted-bucket steam trap uses buoyancy to control a valve. When the bucket is full of steam, it floats up and closes the valve, and once condensate enters, the bucket gradually sinks and opens the valve again.\nIn weightlessness, however, an ordinary bucket cannot work by buoyancy, so the steam traps on the ship have all been specially modified, and mainly use the pressure difference between lines and the flow of the fluid to discharge condensate.

[codex:cx_manifold]
中：阀组（总管）
英：VALVE MANIFOLD

[codex:cx_manifold_a]
中：一根总管连接着多条支管，每条支管上都有独立的隔离阀，可以分别控制各条管路的流通。
英：One header pipe connects to several branch pipes, and each branch has its own isolating valve, which can control the flow in each line separately.

[codex:cx_manifold_b]
中：每条支管里输送的介质和内部压力可能都不一样，如果直接拆除总管，里面残留的液体或气体就可能从多个接口同时泄漏出来。
英：The medium and internal pressure in each branch may differ, and if you remove the header directly, the liquid or gas left inside may leak out of several ports at once.

[codex:cx_manifold_c]
中：先把每条支管的隔离阀逐一关闭，再排空管道里剩余的介质，确认所有管路都没有压力以后，才能开始拆除总管。
英：First close the isolating valve of each branch one by one, then drain the medium left in the lines, and start removing the header only after confirming that no line has any pressure.

[codex:cx_manifold_x]
中：阀组可以让一台泵同时连接多个储罐，通过各条支管上的阀门来控制输送方向。如果其他储罐的阀门没有关闭，抽取其中一个储罐时，就可能连带抽走其他储罐里的介质。如果里面装着不同的物料，还可能在总管中发生混合，所以操作之前一定要确认各条支管的阀门状态。
英：A valve manifold lets one pump connect to several tanks at once, and the valves on the branches control the direction of transfer. If the valves of the other tanks have not been closed, drawing from one tank may also draw the contents of the others. If they hold different materials, they may also mix in the header, so always confirm the valve state of every branch before operating.

[codex:cx_aux]
中：调节阀
英：CONTROL VALVE

[codex:cx_aux_a]
中：调节阀由球形阀体、阀盖、膜片执行器和定位器组成，可以根据控制信号自动调整阀门的开度，控制管道内的流量。
英：A control valve consists of a globe valve body, a bonnet, a diaphragm actuator and a positioner. It adjusts its opening automatically according to a control signal, controlling the flow in the line.

[codex:cx_aux_b]
中：执行器失去气源或电源后，阀门会自动移动到预设的故障位置，有些会完全打开，有些会完全关闭，具体要看阀门的设置。
英：When the actuator loses its air or power supply, the valve automatically moves to its preset fail position. Some open fully and some close fully, depending on how the valve is set.

[codex:cx_aux_c]
中：不要直接用调节阀隔离管道，拆卸之前要先关闭手动隔离阀，确认管道已经完成隔离和泄压后才能动手。
英：Do not use a control valve directly to isolate a line. Before removal, close the manual isolating valves first, and start work only after confirming the line has been isolated and depressurised.

[codex:cx_aux_x]
中：调节阀通过控制信号连续调整阀门开度，阀芯本身存在间隙，关闭以后也可能发生泄漏，所以不能用来代替专门的隔离阀。故障开（FO）型调节阀在触发预设的故障动作时，会自动移动到全开位置。
英：A control valve adjusts its opening continuously according to the control signal, and its trim has clearances, so it may leak even when closed, and cannot take the place of a proper isolating valve. A fail-open (FO) control valve moves automatically to the fully open position when its preset failure action is triggered.

[codex:cx_relief]
中：安全阀
英：PRESSURE RELIEF VALVE

[codex:cx_relief_a]
中：当储气罐里面的压力超过规定值时，安全阀就会自动打开，把多余的气体排出去，等压力降下来以后再关闭。
英：When the pressure in a gas tank goes above the set value, the relief valve opens automatically and vents the excess gas, and closes again once the pressure has dropped.

[codex:cx_relief_b]
中：如果安全阀结冰或者被杂物堵住，罐内的气体就没办法正常排出。一旦压力继续升高，整个储气罐都有可能发生爆炸。
英：If the relief valve freezes or is blocked by debris, the gas in the tank cannot be vented properly. If the pressure keeps rising, the whole gas tank may explode.

[codex:cx_relief_c]
中：先检查储气罐的温度和压力，如果发现安全阀已经结冰，就需要按照操作规程进行解冻处理。如果压力仍然过高，可以通过专门的接口进行抽气泄压。
英：First check the gas tank’s temperature and pressure. If you find the relief valve frozen, it must be thawed following the operating procedure. If the pressure is still too high, you can pump it down and relieve it through a dedicated port.

[codex:cx_relief_x]
中：安全阀是防止储气罐超压的重要保护装置，开启压力通常按照容器的设计压力设定，一般在设计压力的 1.0～1.1 倍范围内。安全阀打开以后会持续排气，直到压力降低到规定的回座压力才会重新关闭。低温储气罐在排气时，排出的冷气可能让阀门出口附近结冰，严重时还会形成冰塞，影响正常泄压。
英：The relief valve is an important protective device that stops a gas tank from over-pressurising. Its opening pressure is usually set by the vessel’s design pressure, generally within 1.0–1.1 times the design pressure. Once the relief valve opens, it keeps venting until the pressure falls to the specified reseat pressure, and only then closes again. When a cryogenic gas tank vents, the cold gas it releases may freeze the area near the valve outlet, and in serious cases it forms an ice plug that stops normal pressure relief.

[codex:cx_pipe]
中：管路
英：PIPE RUN

[codex:cx_pipe_a]
中：这是一段连接着法兰的管道，外面包着保温材料，并通过吊架固定在船体结构上。
英：This is a length of pipe with flanges connected to it, wrapped in insulation material and held to the hull structure by hangers.

[codex:cx_pipe_b]
中：如果管道里面还有液体，直接切开就可能发生泄漏。如果里面装的是易燃物质，切割时还可能喷出可燃液体或气体，引发火灾。
英：If the pipe still holds liquid, cutting it directly may cause a leak. If it holds something flammable, cutting may also spray out flammable liquid or gas and start a fire.

[codex:cx_pipe_c]
中：先关闭管道上下游的阀门，再通过低处的排放口把内部液体排空。确认没有残留压力以后，就可以拆卸法兰，把整段管道取下来。
英：First close the valves upstream and downstream of the pipe, then drain the liquid inside through the low-point drain. Once you have confirmed there is no residual pressure, you can unbolt the flanges and take off the whole length of pipe.

[codex:cx_pipe_x]
中：相比直接切割管道，拆卸法兰可以保留完整的管道结构，只需要松开连接处的螺栓，就能把整段管道拆下来回收。不过在失重环境下，管道里面残留的液体不会因为打开排放口就自动流出去，需要使用抽液设备才能排空。
英：Compared with cutting the pipe, unbolting the flanges keeps the pipe structure whole, and you only need to loosen the bolts at the joints to take the whole length off for recovery. In weightlessness, however, the liquid left in the pipe does not flow out by itself just because the drain is opened, and it needs a liquid-pumping device to be emptied.

[codex:cx_hpu]
中：液压站
英：HYDRAULIC POWER UNIT

[codex:cx_hpu_a]
中：液压站主要由油箱、电机泵、蓄能器、电磁阀组和压力表组成，负责给船上的液压设备提供动力。
英：A hydraulic power unit consists mainly of an oil reservoir, a motor-pump, accumulators, a solenoid valve block and a pressure gauge, and powers the ship’s hydraulic equipment.

[codex:cx_hpu_b]
中：蓄能器里面储存着高压液压油，即使已经切断电源，内部也可能保留很高的压力。如果这时候直接切断管道，高压液压油就可能从切口喷射出来，十分危险。
英：The accumulators store high-pressure hydraulic oil, and even after the power is cut, they may keep a very high pressure inside. If you cut a line at this point, the high-pressure hydraulic oil may spray out of the cut, which is very dangerous.

[codex:cx_hpu_c]
中：先打开泄压阀，把蓄能器里面的压力释放掉，确认压力表已经降到 0，而且设备没有残留压力以后，再开始拆卸连接管道和液压机构。
英：First open the relief valve and release the pressure in the accumulators. After confirming the pressure gauge has dropped to 0 and the equipment holds no residual pressure, start removing the connected lines and the hydraulic mechanisms.

[codex:cx_hpu_x]
中：液压系统的工作压力经常超过 20 MPa，一个容量为 20 L 的蓄能器，在 20 MPa 的压力下可能储存约 200 kJ 的能量，具体数值取决于蓄能器的工作条件。即使关闭了电机，只要内部还有压力，液压缸、夹钳和机械臂就可能突然动作，所以拆卸之前一定要把残留压力释放干净，并确认机械部件已经固定好。
英：The working pressure of a hydraulic system often exceeds 20 MPa, and a 20 L accumulator at 20 MPa may store about 200 kJ of energy, with the exact value depending on the accumulator’s working conditions. Even with the motor switched off, as long as pressure remains inside, hydraulic cylinders, clamps and mechanical arms may suddenly move, so always release the residual pressure completely before removal, and confirm the mechanical parts have been secured.

[codex:cx_actuator]
中：直线作动器
英：LINEAR ACTUATOR

[codex:cx_actuator_a]
中：直线作动器由电机、传动皮带、滚珠丝杠和推杆组成，可以把电机的旋转运动转换成推杆的直线运动，用来推动或者调整设备的位置。
英：A linear actuator consists of a motor, a drive belt, a ball screw and a push rod. It converts the motor’s rotary motion into straight-line motion of the push rod, used to push or adjust the position of equipment.

[codex:cx_actuator_b]
中：如果作动器还在通电，拆断控制线路时就可能导致推杆突然移动。如果它正在支撑其他设备，而这些设备又没有固定好，就可能在推杆移动后直接掉下来。
英：If an actuator is still powered, cutting its control line may make the push rod suddenly move. If it is supporting other equipment that has not been secured, that equipment may drop straight down once the rod moves.

[codex:cx_actuator_c]
中：拆卸之前，先用锁定装置固定推杆，并把它支撑的设备固定好。确认这些部件不会突然移动以后，再切断电源，开始拆卸作动器。
英：Before removal, first fix the push rod with the locking device, and secure the equipment it supports. After confirming these parts will not suddenly move, cut the power and start removing the actuator.

[codex:cx_actuator_x]
中：滚珠丝杠的传动效率很高，除了能够把电机的旋转运动转换成直线推力，外部负载也可能反过来推动丝杠旋转。如果作动器没有安装制动装置，即使切断了电源，推杆仍然可能在负载作用下发生移动。一些用于调整精密镜片位置的作动器还会配备专门的锁定机构，防止断电以后出现位置偏移。
英：A ball screw has very high drive efficiency. As well as converting the motor’s rotary motion into straight-line thrust, an external load can also drive the screw round in reverse. If the actuator has no brake, even after the power is cut, the push rod may still move under the load. Some actuators used to adjust the position of precision mirrors are also fitted with dedicated locking mechanisms, to stop the position shifting after power-off.

[codex:cx_gastank]
中：气瓶
英：GAS CYLINDER

[codex:cx_gastank_a]
中：这是一种用来储存高压气体的气瓶，顶部装有瓶阀和控制手轮，瓶身上的颜色标记可以帮助工作人员辨认里面储存的气体种类。
英：This is a cylinder for storing high-pressure gas. It has a cylinder valve and a control handwheel on top, and the colour marks on the body help crew identify the kind of gas stored inside.

[codex:cx_gastank_b]
中：气瓶里面储存着大量高压气体，如果直接切割瓶身，就可能导致气瓶爆裂。即使没有发生爆裂，泄漏的气体也可能迅速扩散到舱室里面。如果瓶阀被撞断，高压气体会从瓶口猛烈喷出，甚至可能推动整个气瓶高速飞出去。
英：A cylinder holds a lot of high-pressure gas, and cutting the body directly may burst it. Even if it does not burst, the leaked gas may spread quickly into the room. If the cylinder valve is knocked off, the high-pressure gas shoots violently out of the neck, and may even push the whole cylinder off at high speed.

[codex:cx_gastank_c]
中：如果气瓶的密封状态完好，可以直接把整瓶送到设备回收口。如果需要排放里面的气体，就通过气瓶的 -V 接口进行泄压，确认压力已经释放以后再继续处理，千万不要直接切割瓶身。
英：If the cylinder’s seal is intact, you can send the whole cylinder straight to the equipment recovery mouth. If you need to vent the gas inside, relieve it through the cylinder’s -V port, and carry on only after confirming the pressure has been released. Never cut the body directly.

[codex:cx_gastank_x]
中：一只容量为 50 L、内部压力达到 200 bar 的气瓶，大约储存着 1.5 MJ 的压缩能量，具体数值会受到气体种类和温度的影响。如果瓶阀突然断裂，高压气体会产生很大的反冲力，足以让没有固定好的气瓶高速移动。氧气瓶还需要特别注意，因为高浓度氧气会让周围的可燃材料更容易起火，而且燃烧速度也会明显加快。
英：A cylinder of 50 L capacity with an internal pressure of 200 bar stores about 1.5 MJ of compression energy, and the exact value is affected by the kind of gas and its temperature. If the cylinder valve suddenly breaks off, the high-pressure gas produces a large recoil, enough to send an unsecured cylinder moving at high speed. Oxygen cylinders need special care, because high-concentration oxygen makes the surrounding combustible materials catch fire more easily, and also makes them burn noticeably faster.

[codex:cx_fueltank]
中：推进剂 / 工艺储罐
英：PROPELLANT TANK

[codex:cx_fueltank_a]
中：这是一种用来储存推进剂或其他工艺液体的压力容器，通常横放在鞍座上，内部安装了防止液体晃动的隔板，顶部还有管道接口和液位计。
英：This is a pressure vessel for storing propellant or other process liquids. It usually lies on its side on saddles, has baffles inside to stop the liquid sloshing, and has pipe connections and a level gauge on top.

[codex:cx_fueltank_b]
中：如果储罐里面还有液体或者残留压力，直接切割就可能引发爆炸。如果储存的是易燃燃料，泄漏后还可能形成可燃气云，一旦遇到火花就有发生火灾或爆炸的危险。
英：If the tank still holds liquid or residual pressure, cutting it directly may cause an explosion. If it stores flammable fuel, the leak may also form a flammable gas cloud, and if it meets a spark, there is a danger of fire or explosion.

[codex:cx_fueltank_c]
中：先通过回收接口把储罐里面的液体抽走，再释放罐顶残留的气体压力。确认储罐已经排空并完成泄压以后，就可以把整个储罐拆下来，送到危险品回收口处理。
英：First draw the liquid out of the tank through the recovery port, then release the residual gas pressure at the top of the tank. After confirming the tank has been emptied and depressurised, you can take the whole tank off and send it to the hazard mouth for handling.

[codex:cx_fueltank_x]
中：储罐顶部通常会留出一部分气体空间，利用气体压力维持罐内压力，让液体能够正常进入输送泵。抽取液体时，需要通过增压系统维持压力，或者在允许的情况下补充气体，避免罐内压力降得太低。在真空环境下，如果只抽取液体却没有及时补充气体，罐内外的压力差就可能让储罐发生变形，严重时甚至会被压瘪。
英：A tank usually leaves some gas space at the top, which keeps the pressure in the tank up so that the liquid can enter the transfer pump normally. When drawing off liquid, you need to keep the pressure up with the pressurisation system, or add gas where allowed, so that the pressure in the tank does not fall too low. In vacuum, if you draw off only liquid and do not add gas in time, the pressure difference between inside and outside the tank may deform the tank, and in serious cases even crush it.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank]
中：球形压力罐
英：SPHERICAL TANK

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_a]
中：这是一种用来储存高压气体或推进剂的球形储罐，罐体中间有一圈焊缝，外侧装有固定用的安装耳，顶部还有加注阀。罐内的推进剂管理叶片可以帮助液体流向出口。
英：This is a spherical tank for storing high-pressure gas or propellant. There is a girth weld around the middle of the tank, mounting tabs on the outside for fixing it, and a fill valve on top. The propellant management vanes inside the tank help the liquid flow towards the outlet.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_b]
中：球形压力罐通常需要承受很高的内部压力，如果还没有泄压就直接切割，罐内的高压气体可能会突然喷出，严重时甚至会导致整个罐体爆裂。
英：A spherical pressure tank usually has to bear very high internal pressure, and if you cut it before it is depressurised, the high-pressure gas inside may suddenly burst out, and in serious cases the whole tank may rupture.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_c]
中：先检查压力罐的内部压力，再通过专门的接口进行泄压。等压力降到安全范围以后，就可以拆卸外侧的安装耳，将整个压力罐完整取下来。
英：First check the internal pressure of the pressure tank, then relieve it through the dedicated port. Once the pressure has dropped to a safe range, you can unbolt the mounting tabs on the outside and take the whole pressure tank off intact.

[codex:cx_sphere_tank_x]
中：在材料和壁厚相同的情况下，理想薄壁球罐的承压能力大约是圆筒形罐体的两倍，所以高压气体和推进剂经常会储存在球形压力罐里。由于失重环境下的液体不会自然沉到罐底，罐内还装有推进剂管理叶片，可以利用毛细作用让液体集中到出口附近，方便后续输送。
英：With the same material and wall thickness, an ideal thin-walled spherical tank can bear about twice the pressure of a cylindrical tank, so high-pressure gas and propellant are often stored in spherical pressure tanks. Because liquid in weightlessness does not settle at the bottom of the tank by itself, the tank also has propellant management vanes, which use capillary action to gather the liquid near the outlet and make later transfer easier.

[codex:cx_cryotank]
中：低温储罐
英：CRYOGENIC TANK

[codex:cx_cryotank_a]
中：低温储罐主要用来储存液氢、液氮和液化天然气等需要低温保存的液体。储罐内部有一层真空夹套，可以减少外界热量进入，让里面的液体保持在很低的温度。
英：A cryogenic tank is mainly used to store liquids that must be kept cold, such as liquid hydrogen, liquid nitrogen and liquefied natural gas. Inside the tank there is a vacuum jacket, which reduces heat coming in from outside and keeps the liquid at a very low temperature.

[codex:cx_cryotank_b]
中：即使有保温层，外界的热量还是会慢慢进入储罐，导致里面的液体不断蒸发，罐内压力也会随之升高。如果安全阀因为结冰而无法正常泄压，压力继续升高以后，就有可能发生爆罐。
英：Even with insulation, heat from outside still slowly enters the tank, so the liquid inside keeps evaporating and the pressure in the tank rises with it. If the relief valve is frozen and cannot relieve the pressure properly, the pressure keeps rising and the tank may burst.

[codex:cx_cryotank_c]
中：先保持制冷系统正常运行，再通过专门的接口抽出罐内的液体和气体，让内部压力逐渐降低。确认储罐已经排空并完成泄压以后，再关闭制冷系统，切断相关电源。
英：First keep the refrigeration system running normally, then draw the liquid and gas out of the tank through the dedicated port, so that the internal pressure gradually falls. After confirming the tank has been emptied and depressurised, switch off the refrigeration system and cut the related power.

[codex:cx_cryotank_x]
中：在正常大气压下，液氢的沸点约为 −253°C，液氮的沸点约为 −196°C。只要有热量进入储罐，就会有一部分液体蒸发成气体，而这些气体在常温常压下的体积可以达到原来液体体积的几百倍。如果气体没有及时排出，罐内压力就会不断升高。真空夹套一旦破损，外界进入的热量可能增加十倍，液体的蒸发速度也会明显加快。
英：At normal atmospheric pressure, liquid hydrogen boils at about −253°C and liquid nitrogen at about −196°C. Whenever heat gets into the tank, some of the liquid evaporates into gas, and at normal temperature and pressure this gas can take up several hundred times the volume of the original liquid. If the gas is not vented in time, the pressure in the tank keeps rising. Once the vacuum jacket is damaged, the heat coming in from outside may increase tenfold, and the liquid evaporates noticeably faster.

[codex:cx_water]
中：水箱
英：WATER TANK

[codex:cx_water_a]
中：这是船上用来储存水的容器，水箱上装有人孔、通气管和液位镜，内部还有防晃板，可以减少船体移动时水的晃动。
英：This is a container on the ship for storing water. The water tank has a manway, a vent pipe and a level sight glass, and anti-slosh baffles inside that reduce water sloshing when the hull moves.

[codex:cx_water_b]
中：如果水箱里面还有水，直接切开就会造成泄漏。在失重环境下，流出来的水会漂浮在空中，一旦接触附近的带电设备，就可能引发短路。
英：If the water tank still holds water, cutting it open directly causes a leak. In weightlessness, the water that comes out floats in the air, and if it touches live equipment nearby, it may cause a short circuit.

[codex:cx_water_c]
中：先通过出水阀或者专门的接口，把水箱里面的水全部抽出来，确认水箱已经排空以后，再拆卸外部连接件，将整个水箱取下来。

[codex:cx_water_x]
中：水如果直接暴露在真空环境中，就会因为压力突然降低而迅速沸腾，同时蒸发还会带走大量热量，让剩余的水逐渐结冰，形成漂浮的冰块。这些冰块还会继续缓慢升华，所以直接切开水箱会损失不少水。水在船上属于有回收价值的资源，哪怕只有几立方米，也能按照合同价格卖出一笔钱。
英：If water is exposed directly to vacuum, it boils rapidly because the pressure suddenly drops, and the evaporation also carries away a lot of heat, so the remaining water gradually freezes into floating blocks of ice. These blocks of ice also keep slowly subliming, so cutting the tank open directly loses quite a lot of water. Water on a ship is a resource with recovery value, and even a few cubic metres can be sold at the contract price for a sum of money.

[codex:cx_extinguisher]

英：FIRE SUPPRESSION BOTTLE

[codex:cx_extinguisher_b]

英：The extinguisher bottle is itself a high-pressure vessel. If the solenoid actuator is still connected to power, it may be triggered by accident during removal, and the agent in the bottle suddenly sprays out. If the bottle still holds pressure, cutting the body directly may also make it burst.

[codex:cx_extinguisher_c]
中：发生火灾时，可以使用灭火瓶进行灭火。如果暂时不需要使用，就先断开电磁启动器的电源，再把整个灭火瓶拆卸下来，送到危险品回收口处理。
英：In a fire, you can use the extinguisher bottle to put it out. If it is not needed for now, first disconnect the solenoid actuator’s power, then take the whole extinguisher bottle off and send it to the hazard mouth for handling.

[codex:cx_extinguisher_x]
中：船上的灭火系统通常使用二氧化碳、哈龙替代灭火剂或者泡沫。二氧化碳释放到密闭舱室以后，可以降低空气中的氧气浓度，让火焰无法继续燃烧，不过舱室里的气体也会因此混入大量二氧化碳，不能再按照普通空气回收，而且人员继续留在里面还可能发生窒息。有些旧船上的灭火瓶可能已经用完了，拆卸前可以先查看压力表，确认瓶内是否还有压力。
英：The ship’s fire-fighting systems usually use carbon dioxide, halon substitutes or foam. After carbon dioxide is released into a closed room, it lowers the oxygen concentration in the air so that flames cannot keep burning, but the gas in the room then also contains a lot of carbon dioxide, and can no longer be recovered as ordinary air, and anyone who stays inside may suffocate. The extinguisher bottles on some old ships may already be used up, so before removal you can check the pressure gauge first to confirm whether the bottle still holds pressure.

[codex:cx_o2cab]

[codex:cx_o2cab_a]

[codex:cx_o2cab_b]

英：Oxygen itself does not burn, but it helps other substances burn, and the higher the oxygen concentration, the more violent the burning usually is. Materials stained with grease, fabric and even tiny metal swarf can all be ignited more easily in a high-concentration oxygen environment, so when removing an oxygen cabinet, take special care about hot equipment nearby and tools that may produce sparks.

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber]
中：CO₂ 洗涤器
英：CO₂ SCRUBBER

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber_a]

英：This equipment removes carbon dioxide from the air in the cabin. It holds two treatment canisters that use an amine absorbent or zeolite, plus valves that switch the air flow and a blower that keeps the air circulating.

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber_b]

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber_c]
中：先切断洗涤器的电源，关闭连接两个吸收罐的切换阀门，确认管道已经隔离、内部压力也恢复安全以后，再开始拆卸设备。
英：First cut the scrubber’s power and close the switching valves that connect the two absorber canisters. Start removing the equipment only after confirming the lines are isolated and the internal pressure is back to a safe level.

[codex:cx_co2_scrubber_x]
中：洗涤器里面的两个吸收罐会交替工作，一个负责吸收舱内空气中的二氧化碳，另一个则通过连接真空的管道，把之前吸收的二氧化碳排出去，让吸收材料恢复使用能力。如果空气中的二氧化碳浓度超过 1%，人就可能出现头痛等不适症状，浓度超过 5% 时还会有严重的生命危险，所以这套设备对密闭舱室的空气循环非常重要。
英：The two absorber canisters in the scrubber work in turns. One absorbs the carbon dioxide in the cabin air, while the other releases the carbon dioxide it absorbed earlier through the line connected to vacuum, so the absorbent can be used again. If the carbon dioxide concentration in the air goes above 1%, people may suffer discomfort such as headaches, and above 5% there is serious danger to life, so this equipment is very important for the air circulation of a closed room.

[codex:cx_psa]
中：变压吸附分离器
英：PRESSURE-SWING ADSORPTION

[codex:cx_psa_a]
中：这套设备由两根吸附塔和上下两组切换阀门组成，可以利用内部的分子筛，从混合气体中分离出需要的气体。
英：This equipment consists of two adsorber columns and an upper and a lower set of switching valves. It uses the molecular sieve inside to separate the gas you need from a mixed gas.

[codex:cx_psa_b]
中：吸附塔内部可能还存有高压气体，而且分离出来的气体可能是纯氧、纯氮或者氢气。如果直接拆卸，残留的气体可能会从接口喷出。氢气容易燃烧，纯氧会加剧燃烧，纯氮泄漏则可能造成缺氧窒息。
英：The adsorber columns may still hold high-pressure gas, and the separated gas may be pure oxygen, pure nitrogen or hydrogen. If you remove them directly, the residual gas may shoot out of the ports. Hydrogen burns easily, pure oxygen makes burning fiercer, and a leak of pure nitrogen may cause suffocation from lack of oxygen.

[codex:cx_psa_c]
中：先关闭进气阀门，停止向设备输送气体，再通过泄压接口排出吸附塔里面的残余压力。确认两根吸附塔都已经完成泄压以后，再拆卸连接部件。
英：First close the feed valve to stop gas going into the equipment, then release the residual pressure in the adsorber columns through the relief port. Remove the connected parts only after confirming both columns have been depressurised.

[codex:cx_psa_x]
中：吸附塔内部装有分子筛，在较高压力下，分子筛会吸附混合气体中的某些分子，等压力降低以后，又会释放之前吸附的气体。两根吸附塔交替进行加压和减压，就能持续分离出需要的气体。需要注意的是，纯氮一旦大量泄漏到密闭空间，就可能挤走原本的氧气，而且人体通常不会提前察觉缺氧，严重时会直接失去意识。
英：The adsorber columns contain a molecular sieve. At higher pressure, the sieve adsorbs certain molecules from the mixed gas, and when the pressure drops, it releases the gas it adsorbed earlier. With the two columns taking turns to pressurise and depressurise, the equipment can keep separating out the gas you need. Note that once a large amount of pure nitrogen leaks into a closed space, it can push out the original oxygen, and people usually do not notice the lack of oxygen beforehand, and in serious cases lose consciousness at once.

[codex:cx_electrolyser]
中：电解制氧机
英：WATER ELECTROLYSER

[codex:cx_electrolyser_a]
中：这套设备由电解槽、氢氧气液分离器、循环泵和整流器组成，通过电解水来生产氧气，同时还会产生氢气。
英：This equipment consists of an electrolyser stack, hydrogen and oxygen gas-liquid separators, a circulation pump and a rectifier. It produces oxygen by electrolysing water, and also produces hydrogen.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_b]
中：设备里面同时有氢气、氧气和高压电，如果气体发生泄漏，氢气和氧气就可能混合在一起。一旦遇到电火花或者高温，就有可能发生爆炸。
英：The equipment holds hydrogen, oxygen and high-voltage power at the same time. If gas leaks, the hydrogen and oxygen may mix together, and if the mixture meets an electric spark or high temperature, it may explode.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_c]
中：先切断设备电源，确认电解槽已经停止运行，再通过各自的排气接口，分别排空氢气和氧气。等两侧都完成泄压以后，再开始拆卸设备。
英：First cut the equipment’s power and confirm the electrolyser stack has stopped running, then vent the hydrogen and the oxygen separately through their own vent ports. Start removing the equipment only after both sides have been depressurised.

[codex:cx_electrolyser_x]
中：电解制氧机通过电流把水分解成氢气和氧气，每消耗 1 kg 水，大约可以产生 1.24 m³ 氢气和 0.62 m³ 氧气，耗电量约为 5 kWh。氢气和氧气的体积比例达到 2:1 时，正好符合完全反应所需的比例，这种混合气体通常被称为爆鸣气，遇到火源就可能发生非常剧烈的爆炸。
英：An electrolyser oxygen generator uses electric current to split water into hydrogen and oxygen. For every 1 kg of water consumed, it produces about 1.24 m³ of hydrogen and 0.62 m³ of oxygen, using about 5 kWh of electricity. When the volume ratio of hydrogen to oxygen reaches 2:1, it exactly matches the ratio needed for complete reaction, and this mixed gas is usually called detonating gas, which can explode very violently if it meets a source of ignition.

[codex:cx_humidifier]
中：加湿器
英：STEAM HUMIDIFIER

[codex:cx_humidifier_a]
中：这是一台电极式加湿器，里面装着用来加热水的电极筒，外面连接着进水阀、排水阀和蒸汽软管，旁边还有一个负责控制设备运行的控制盒。
英：This is an electrode humidifier. It holds an electrode cylinder for heating water, the outside is connected to a fill valve, a drain valve and a steam hose, and beside it is a control box that controls how the equipment runs.

[codex:cx_humidifier_b]
中：加湿器运行时，电极会直接通电加热筒内的水。如果没有切断电源就进行拆卸，可能发生触电事故，而且筒内的热水和蒸汽也容易造成烫伤。
英：While the humidifier runs, its electrodes pass current directly through the water in the cylinder to heat it. If you remove it without cutting the power, an electric shock may occur, and the hot water and steam in the cylinder can also easily scald.

[codex:cx_humidifier_c]
中：先切断加湿器的电源，确认设备已经停止运行，再把筒内的热水排出去。等设备冷却并确认内部没有残留压力以后，就可以开始拆卸了。

[codex:cx_humidifier_x]

英：An electrode humidifier makes heat by passing current directly through the water, and after heating the water to boiling, it uses the steam produced to raise the humidity of the air. The conductivity of the water affects the heating power, and the stronger the conductivity, the larger the current usually is under the same voltage and other conditions. Even after the power is cut, the water in the cylinder does not cool at once, so before removal you also need to check the water temperature, to avoid being scalded by the hot water left inside.

[codex:cx_separator_3ph]
中：三相分离器
英：THREE-PHASE SEPARATOR

[codex:cx_separator_3ph_a]
中：三相分离器是一种卧式容器，内部装有堰板，可以把混合在一起的油、水和气体分离开来，再通过各自的出口排出去。
英：A three-phase separator is a horizontal vessel with a weir inside. It separates mixed oil, water and gas, and discharges each through its own outlet.

[codex:cx_separator_3ph_b]
中：即使设备已经停止运行，容器内部也可能残留一定的压力，而且里面还可能存有油、水和气体。如果直接拆开，残留的压力和介质就有可能从开口处喷出来。
英：Even after the equipment has stopped running, some pressure may remain inside the vessel, and it may also still hold oil, water and gas. If you open it directly, the residual pressure and contents may shoot out of the opening.

[codex:cx_separator_3ph_c]
中：拆卸之前，先通过顶部的泄压接口把容器内部的压力降下来，再分别打开油、水和气体对应的排放接口，确认三路都已经排空以后，才能开始拆卸。
英：Before removal, first bring down the pressure inside the vessel through the relief port at the top, then open the drain ports for oil, water and gas separately, and start removal only after confirming all three have been emptied.

[codex:cx_separator_3ph_x]
中：地面上的三相分离器主要利用重力进行分离，油、水和气体的密度不同，经过一段时间后就会逐渐分开。船上的分离设备则通常会利用旋流或者离心力来完成分离，这样可以减少对重力沉降的依赖，也能让设备做得更加紧凑。
英：On the ground, a three-phase separator separates mainly by gravity: oil, water and gas have different densities, and gradually separate after some time. Separating equipment on a ship usually uses swirl or centrifugal force instead, which reduces the reliance on gravity settling and also lets the equipment be made more compact.

[codex:cx_coriolis]
中：科氏流量计
英：CORIOLIS FLOW METER

[codex:cx_coriolis_a]
中：科氏流量计安装在管路上，内部有两根弯曲的测量管。设备运行时，激振器会让测量管持续振动，通过检测振动的变化，就能计算出流过管路的流体质量。
英：A Coriolis flow meter is fitted on a line and has two bent measuring tubes inside. While the equipment runs, an exciter keeps the measuring tubes vibrating, and by detecting changes in the vibration, it can calculate the mass of fluid flowing through the line.

[codex:cx_coriolis_b]
中：流量计本身就是管路的一部分，如果直接拆卸，就相当于把整条管路从中间断开。如果管道里面还有压力或者残留液体，拆开法兰时就可能发生喷溅或泄漏。
英：The flow meter is itself part of the line, and removing it directly amounts to breaking the whole line in the middle. If the pipe still holds pressure or leftover liquid, splashing or leaking may happen when the flanges are opened.

[codex:cx_coriolis_c]
中：拆卸之前，先关闭并隔离流量计所在的管路，确认内部已经完成泄压和排空，再松开连接法兰，将流量计拆卸下来。
英：Before removal, first close and isolate the line the flow meter is on, confirm it has been depressurised and drained, then loosen the connecting flanges and take the flow meter off.

[codex:cx_coriolis_x]
中：流体经过正在振动的测量管时，会受到科里奥利力的影响，使测量管两端的振动产生相位差。流量计通过检测这个相位差，就能计算出实际的质量流量，部分高精度型号的测量精度可以达到 0.1% 左右，不过价格通常也比较高。
英：When fluid passes through the vibrating measuring tubes, it is acted on by the Coriolis force, which makes the vibration at the two ends of the tubes differ in phase. By detecting this phase difference, the flow meter can calculate the actual mass flow, and some high-precision models reach a measuring accuracy of around 0.1%, though they are usually quite expensive.

[codex:cx_level_radar]
中：雷达液位计
英：RADAR LEVEL GAUGE

[codex:cx_level_radar_a]
中：雷达液位计安装在储罐顶部，由伸进罐内的喇叭形天线和外部的电子测量装置组成，可以用来测量储罐里面还剩下多少液体。
英：A radar level gauge is fitted on top of a tank. It consists of a horn antenna reaching into the tank and electronic measuring equipment outside, and can measure how much liquid is left in the tank.

[codex:cx_level_radar_b]
中：液位计直接安装在储罐的接口上，如果罐内还有压力，拆卸液位计就相当于打开了一个没有封闭的罐口，里面残留的气体或者液体可能会突然喷出来。
英：The level gauge is mounted directly on a tank connection. If the tank still holds pressure, removing the gauge amounts to opening an unsealed tank mouth, and the gas or liquid left inside may suddenly shoot out.

[codex:cx_level_radar_c]
中：拆卸之前，先通过液位计查看储罐里面还剩下多少液体，等罐内液体排空、压力完全释放，并确认设备已经处于安全状态以后，再拆卸液位计。
英：Before removal, first use the level gauge to see how much liquid is left in the tank, and remove the gauge only after the liquid in the tank has been emptied, the pressure fully released, and the equipment confirmed to be in a safe state.

[codex:cx_level_radar_x]
中：雷达液位计通过天线向液面发射电磁波，再根据电磁波反射回来所花费的时间计算液位高度。在失重环境下，液体很难保持平整的液面，所以船上的液位计通常需要经过特殊设计和数据处理，才能估算出罐内的平均液位。
英：A radar level gauge sends electromagnetic waves to the liquid surface through the antenna, and calculates the level height from the time the waves take to reflect back. In weightlessness, liquid can hardly keep a flat surface, so a gauge on a ship usually needs a special design and data processing to estimate the average level in the tank.

[codex:cx_gauge_box]
中：载荷传感器（应变片）
英：LOAD CELL

[codex:cx_gauge_box_a]
中：载荷传感器里面装有金属箔应变片，这些应变片固定在能够轻微变形的弹性体上，可以通过测量变形程度，判断设备结构目前承受了多大的力。
英：A load sensor holds metal foil strain gauges, fixed on an elastic body that can deform slightly. By measuring how much it deforms, it can tell how much force the equipment structure is currently bearing.

[codex:cx_gauge_box_b]
中：如果传感器显示的载荷数值很高，就说明这部分结构还在承受较大的拉力或者压力。这时候直接切割或者拆卸，原本受到约束的部件可能会突然弹开，甚至造成附近的零件移位。
英：If the load value shown by the sensor is high, this part of the structure is still bearing a large tension or compression. If you cut or remove it at this point, parts that were held under restraint may suddenly spring off, and even shift nearby parts.

[codex:cx_gauge_box_c]
中：拆卸之前，先查看载荷传感器的读数，确认这部分结构还有没有承受较大的力。如果读数仍然很高，就需要先卸除载荷，等数值降低到安全范围，并确认结构已经稳定以后，再开始拆卸。
英：Before removal, first read the load sensor to see whether this part of the structure is still bearing a large force. If the reading is still high, you need to take the load off first, and start removal only after the value has dropped to a safe range and the structure is confirmed stable.

[codex:cx_gauge_box_x]
中：金属箔应变片受到拉伸或者压缩时，电阻会发生细微变化，惠斯通电桥可以把这些变化转换成电压信号，再由仪表计算出对应的载荷。承重结构、拉杆和铰链上安装的载荷传感器，可以帮助你判断哪些部件仍然处于受力状态，避免在拆卸时突然释放积累的弹性力。
英：When a metal foil strain gauge is stretched or compressed, its resistance changes slightly. A Wheatstone bridge converts these changes into a voltage signal, and the instrument then calculates the matching load. Load sensors fitted on load-bearing members, tie rods and hinges can help you judge which parts are still under force, so that stored elastic force is not suddenly released during removal.

[codex:cx_insp]
中：现场仪表与传感器
英：FIELD INSTRUMENTS

[codex:cx_insp_a]
中：现场仪表和传感器安装在设备的不同位置，包括带有显示窗口的仪表、传感器节点和探测头。通过这些设备，可以查看管路压力、温度以及电路的运行状态。
英：Field instruments and sensors are fitted at different positions on the equipment, including instruments with display windows, sensor nodes and detector heads. Through them you can check line pressure, temperature and the running state of circuits.

[codex:cx_insp_b]
中：仪表和传感器本身通常没有太大的危险，但它们经常直接连接着带压管路或者带电设备。如果没有提前处理，就直接拆卸，可能会造成气体泄漏，或者接触到仍然带电的线路。
英：Instruments and sensors are usually not very dangerous themselves, but they are often connected directly to a pressurised line or live equipment. If you remove one without dealing with that first, gas may leak, or you may touch a line that is still live.

[codex:cx_insp_c]
中：拆卸之前，先查看仪表上的读数，确认设备目前的运行状态，再根据仪表连接的管路或者电路进行处理。如果管路还有压力，就需要先泄压；如果连接着带电线路，就需要先切断电源并确认断电，之后才能开始拆卸。
英：Before removal, first read the instrument to confirm the equipment’s current running state, then deal with the line or circuit the instrument is connected to. If the line still has pressure, relieve it first; if it is connected to a live circuit, cut the power and confirm it is off, and only then start removal.

[codex:cx_insp_x]
中：现场仪表可以帮助你了解飞船各个设备目前的状态。控制面板上记录的可能是之前保存的数据，而现场仪表显示的是测量位置当前的数值。只要仪表还在正常工作，你就可以直接查看最新读数，判断设备的实际情况。
英：Field instruments help you see the current state of the ship’s various equipment. What a control panel records may be earlier saved data, while a field instrument shows the current value at its measuring position. As long as the instrument is still working normally, you can read the latest value directly and judge the real condition of the equipment.

[codex:cx_screw_comp]
中：螺杆压缩机
英：TWIN-SCREW COMPRESSOR

[codex:cx_screw_comp_a]
中：里面有一对相互啮合的阴阳转子，还有电机、油分离器和油冷却器。
英：It contains a pair of meshing male and female rotors, a motor, an oil separator and an oil cooler.

[codex:cx_screw_comp_b]
中：排气端和油分离器里面可能还留有高压气体，润滑油的温度也可能很高，直接拆卸会有危险。
英：The discharge end and the oil separator may still hold high-pressure gas, and the lubricating oil may also be very hot, so removing it directly is dangerous.

[codex:cx_screw_comp_c]
中：先关闭压缩机，等内部压力完全释放以后，再把润滑油排干净，确认没有残余压力后才能开始拆卸。
英：First switch the compressor off, and once the internal pressure has been fully released, drain the lubricating oil, and start removal only after confirming there is no residual pressure.

[codex:cx_screw_comp_x]
中：电机带动两根螺旋转子转动，通过转子之间不断缩小的空间，把吸入的气体逐渐压缩，再从排气端送出去。氦压缩机主要负责给低温系统提供高压氦气，由于氦气价格很高，拆卸之前还得把里面的氦气回收好。
英：The motor turns two helical rotors, and through the shrinking space between the rotors, the gas drawn in is gradually compressed and then sent out of the discharge end. A helium compressor mainly supplies high-pressure helium to a cryogenic system, and because helium is expensive, the helium inside must also be recovered before removal.

[codex:cx_axial_comp]
中：轴流压缩机
英：AXIAL COMPRESSOR

[codex:cx_axial_comp_a]
中：压缩机内部装着一排排交替排列的动叶片和静叶片，外面的机匣从前往后逐渐收窄。
英：Inside the compressor there are rows of rotor blades and stator vanes arranged alternately, and the casing outside narrows gradually from front to back.

[codex:cx_axial_comp_b]
中：设备停机以后，内部转子可能还在继续转动，进气口也可能仍然连接着工艺气管道，拆卸前需要确认。
英：After the equipment stops, the rotor inside may still be turning, and the inlet may also still be connected to the process gas line, so check before removal.

[codex:cx_axial_comp_c]
中：等转子完全停止转动，再确认进口管道已经隔离，完成以后才能开始拆卸。
英：Wait until the rotor has stopped turning completely, then confirm the inlet line has been isolated, and only after that start removal.

[codex:cx_axial_comp_x]
中：气体每经过一级压缩，压力一般会提高到原来的 1.2～1.4 倍。叶片尖端的运动速度可以达到每秒几百米，如果叶片在高速转动时断裂，碎片就可能高速飞出，非常危险。
英：Each stage of compression generally raises the gas pressure to 1.2–1.4 times what it was. The blade tips can move at several hundred metres per second, and if a blade breaks while spinning at high speed, the fragments may fly out at high speed, which is very dangerous.

[codex:cx_turboexpander]
中：透平膨胀机
英：TURBOEXPANDER

[codex:cx_turboexpander_a]
中：膨胀透平和制动压缩机安装在同一根转轴上，转轴由气体轴承支撑，设备运行时两边会一起转动。
英：The expansion turbine and the brake compressor are mounted on the same shaft, which is supported by gas bearings, and the two sides turn together while the equipment runs.

[codex:cx_turboexpander_b]
中：这台设备运行时的转速可以达到每分钟几万转，内部还有温度很低的工艺气体。停机以后，设备内部可能仍然存在低温和残余压力，直接拆卸会有危险。
英：While running, this equipment can reach several tens of thousands of revolutions per minute, and it holds process gas at a very low temperature. After it stops, low temperature and residual pressure may remain inside, and removing it directly is dangerous.

[codex:cx_turboexpander_c]
中：先停止设备运行，等内部温度恢复到安全范围，再把剩余压力释放干净，确认转子已经完全停止转动以后，才能开始拆卸。
英：First stop the equipment and wait until its internal temperature has returned to a safe range, then release the remaining pressure completely, and start removal only after confirming the rotor has stopped turning completely.

[codex:cx_turboexpander_x]
中：高压气体经过透平膨胀时，会带动叶轮转动并向外输出机械功，同时降低自身的温度，所以这种设备经常用在低温制冷和气体液化系统中。部分小型透平膨胀机的转速甚至可以超过每分钟 10 万转。
英：When high-pressure gas expands through the turbine, it turns the impeller and puts out mechanical work, while its own temperature drops. This kind of equipment is therefore often used in cryogenic refrigeration and gas liquefaction systems. The rotation speed of some small turboexpanders can even exceed 100,000 revolutions per minute.

[codex:cx_turbo_pump]
中：分子泵
英：TURBOMOLECULAR PUMP

[codex:cx_turbo_pump_a]
中：分子泵内部装着一层层交替排列的转子叶片和定子叶片，运行时转子会高速旋转，让进入泵内的气体分子逐渐向出口方向移动。
英：A molecular pump holds layers of rotor blades and stator blades arranged alternately. While running, the rotor spins at high speed and moves the gas molecules that enter the pump gradually towards the outlet.

[codex:cx_turbo_pump_b]
中：分子泵运行时的转速可以达到每分钟几万转，如果外部空气突然大量进入，可能会导致转子受损，严重时甚至会损坏整个泵体。
英：A molecular pump can run at several tens of thousands of revolutions per minute, and if a large amount of outside air suddenly enters, the rotor may be damaged, and in serious cases the whole pump body may be wrecked.

[codex:cx_turbo_pump_c]
中：先关闭分子泵，等待内部转子完全停止转动，再按照操作要求缓慢放气，确认泵内压力恢复正常以后，才能拆卸连接法兰。
英：First switch the molecular pump off and wait until the rotor inside has stopped turning completely, then vent the gas slowly as the operating requirements say, and unbolt the connecting flanges only after confirming the pressure in the pump is back to normal.

[codex:cx_turbo_pump_x]
中：分子泵通过高速旋转的叶片推动气体分子，叶片尖端的运动速度可以接近气体分子的热运动速度。这种泵需要在较低的气体压力下运行，还得配合前级泵一起使用。关闭设备以后，转子通常还需要几分钟才能完全停止转动。
英：A molecular pump pushes gas molecules with blades spinning at high speed, and the speed of the blade tips can approach the thermal speed of the gas molecules. This kind of pump has to run at low gas pressure, and must be used together with a backing pump. After the equipment is switched off, the rotor usually still needs a few minutes to stop turning completely.

[codex:cx_scroll_pump]
中：涡旋泵
英：SCROLL PUMP

[codex:cx_scroll_pump_a]
中：泵的内部装着两个互相嵌套的螺旋盘，其中一个固定不动，另一个围绕固定螺旋盘做公转运动，通过不断缩小气体所在的空间来输送气体。
英：Inside the pump there are two interleaved spiral scrolls. One is fixed, and the other orbits around the fixed scroll, moving gas by continually shrinking the space the gas occupies.

[codex:cx_scroll_pump_b]
中：这台泵输送的可能是价格昂贵的氦-3和氦-4混合气体，如果直接拆开管道，里面的气体就可能泄漏，造成不必要的损失。
英：What this pump moves may be an expensive mixture of helium-3 and helium-4, and if you open the line directly, the gas inside may leak out and cause needless loss.

[codex:cx_scroll_pump_c]
中：拆卸之前，先把循环管道里的混合气体回收到储气容器中，确认回收完成以后，再停止设备运行并进行拆卸。
英：Before removal, first recover the mixed gas in the circulation line into a gas storage vessel, and stop the equipment and remove it only after confirming recovery is complete.

[codex:cx_scroll_pump_x]
中：涡旋泵通过两个螺旋盘之间的相对运动来输送气体，运行时不需要使用润滑油接触气体，所以能够避免油污污染。稀释制冷机需要不断循环氦-3和氦-4混合气体，这种无油设计正好适合这种用途。
英：A scroll pump moves gas through the relative motion of two spiral scrolls, and while running it does not need lubricating oil in contact with the gas, so it avoids oil contamination. A dilution refrigerator needs a helium-3 and helium-4 mixture circulating continuously, and this oil-free design suits that use exactly.

[codex:cx_dry_cooler]
中：干式冷却器
英：DRY COOLER

[codex:cx_dry_cooler_a]
中：这台设备装有 V 形排列的翅片盘管和一排散热风机，运行时风机会让空气经过盘管，把内部冷却液的热量带走。
英：This equipment has finned coils arranged in a V and a row of cooling fans. While running, the fans draw air across the coils and carry away the heat of the coolant inside.

[codex:cx_dry_cooler_b]
中：盘管和连接管道里面可能还存有大量冷却液，如果没有提前排空，拆卸时就可能发生泄漏。
英：The coils and the connected pipes may still hold a lot of coolant, and if it has not been drained beforehand, it may leak during removal.

[codex:cx_dry_cooler_c]
中：先关闭设备并隔离冷却液回路，再把盘管和管道里面剩余的冷却液排放干净，确认没有残余压力以后才能开始拆卸。
英：First switch the equipment off and isolate the coolant circuit, then drain the coolant left in the coils and pipes, and start removal only after confirming there is no residual pressure.

[codex:cx_dry_cooler_x]
中：干式冷却器利用空气带走冷却液的热量，整个过程不需要通过蒸发水来散热。不过在没有空气的真空环境里，风机就起不到散热作用，只能依靠表面向外辐射热量，所以飞船上的干式冷却器一般安装在有空气的舱室里。
英：A dry cooler uses air to carry away the heat of the coolant, and the whole process does not need water to be evaporated to shed heat. In the vacuum, where there is no air, however, the fans have no cooling effect, and heat can only be radiated away from the surface, so the dry coolers on a ship are generally fitted in rooms that hold air.

[codex:cx_cdu]
中：冷却液分配单元
英：COOLANT DISTRIBUTION UNIT

[codex:cx_cdu_a]
中：这套设备由板式换热器、两台循环泵、过滤器、膨胀罐和控制器组成，主要负责给机柜提供冷却液，并把设备运行时产生的热量传递到主冷却回路。
英：This equipment consists of a plate heat exchanger, two circulation pumps, a filter, an expansion vessel and a controller. It mainly supplies coolant to the cabinets and passes the heat produced while the equipment runs on to the main cooling loop.

[codex:cx_cdu_b]
中：这套设备连接着整排机柜的冷却管道，内部可能还有大量冷却液和残余压力，如果直接拆卸，就可能造成冷却液泄漏。

[codex:cx_cdu_c]
中：先停止循环泵运行并切断设备电源，再隔离对应的冷却回路，把管道和设备内部的冷却液排放干净，确认压力已经释放以后才能开始拆卸。
英：First stop the circulation pumps and cut the equipment’s power, then isolate the matching cooling circuit and drain the coolant out of the pipes and the equipment, and start removal only after confirming the pressure has been released.

[codex:cx_cdu_x]
中：冷却液分配单元通过板式换热器，把机房主冷却回路和机柜内部的二次冷却回路分隔开，两边的冷却液不会直接混合，但热量仍然可以通过换热器传递。一台冷却液分配单元通常可以为数百千瓦的计算设备提供冷却服务。
英：A coolant distribution unit uses a plate heat exchanger to separate the machine room’s main cooling loop from the secondary cooling loop inside the cabinets. The coolant on the two sides does not mix directly, but heat can still pass through the heat exchanger. One coolant distribution unit can usually provide cooling for computing equipment of several hundred kilowatts.

[codex:cx_eng_core]
中：液体火箭发动机
英：LIQUID ROCKET ENGINE

[codex:cx_eng_core_a]
中：发动机主要由涡轮泵、燃气发生器、喷注器和带有再生冷却通道的燃烧室组成，这些部件连接在一起，负责输送推进剂并产生推力。
英：An engine consists mainly of a turbopump, a gas generator, an injector and a combustion chamber with regenerative cooling channels. These parts are joined together and are responsible for delivering propellant and producing thrust.

[codex:cx_eng_core_b]
中：发动机停机以后，输送管道和燃烧室的冷却通道里可能还残留着推进剂。有些推进剂带有毒性，还有一些接触氧化剂以后就会自行燃烧，拆卸时需要特别注意。
英：After an engine stops, propellant may still remain in the feed lines and in the cooling channels of the combustion chamber. Some propellants are toxic, and some ignite by themselves on contact with an oxidiser, so take special care during removal.

[codex:cx_eng_core_c]
中：先关闭推进剂主阀，通过排放口排空管道和冷却通道里残留的推进剂，确认已经完成排空和安全隔离以后，再开始拆卸发动机的推力架。
英：First close the main propellant valves, drain the propellant left in the lines and cooling channels through the drains, and after confirming draining and safe isolation are complete, start removing the engine’s thrust frame.

[codex:cx_eng_core_x]
中：发动机工作时，燃料会先经过燃烧室壁内部的冷却通道，带走一部分热量以后再进入喷注器，所以即使发动机已经停机，冷却通道里也可能残留推进剂。肼类推进剂具有毒性，部分肼类燃料与特定氧化剂接触后还会自行燃烧。火箭发动机通常也是船上重量较大、价值较高的设备之一，拆卸时经常需要沿着法兰连接的位置分段处理。
英：While the engine works, the fuel first passes through the cooling channels inside the combustion chamber wall, carries away part of the heat, and then enters the injector, so even after the engine stops, propellant may remain in the cooling channels. Hydrazine propellants are toxic, and some hydrazine fuels ignite by themselves on contact with certain oxidisers. A rocket engine is also usually one of the heavier and more valuable pieces of equipment on a ship, and when removing it, you often have to handle it in sections along the flange connections.

[codex:cx_engine_s]
中：挤压式小发动机
英：PRESSURE-FED ENGINE

[codex:cx_engine_s_a]
中：这种小型发动机依靠贮箱内部的气体压力，把推进剂送进燃烧室，主要由阀门、喷注头、燃烧室和喷管组成。
英：This small engine relies on the gas pressure inside the tank to send propellant into the combustion chamber, and consists mainly of valves, an injector head, a combustion chamber and a nozzle.

[codex:cx_engine_s_b]
中：发动机停机以后，贮箱内部可能还保留着很高的压力，如果直接打开阀门，里面的推进剂就可能突然喷出来。
英：After the engine stops, the tank may still hold very high pressure, and if you open a valve directly, the propellant inside may suddenly shoot out.

[codex:cx_engine_s_c]
中：先关闭推进剂供应阀门，按照规定排放贮箱内部的增压气体，确认压力已经降到安全范围，并且管道内没有残留的危险压力以后，再开始拆卸发动机。
英：First close the propellant supply valves, vent the pressurant gas in the tank as the rules require, and start removing the engine only after confirming the pressure has dropped to a safe range and no dangerous residual pressure remains in the lines.

[codex:cx_engine_s_x]
中：这种发动机不需要使用涡轮泵，直接利用贮箱里的气体压力输送推进剂，所以整体结构比较简单，运行起来也比较可靠。不过贮箱需要承受较高的内部压力，对结构强度的要求也更高。小型飞船、着陆器和作业艇经常会使用这类发动机。
英：This kind of engine needs no turbopump and moves propellant directly with the gas pressure in the tank, so its overall structure is fairly simple and it is fairly reliable to run. However, the tank has to bear high internal pressure, which also demands more structural strength. Small ships, landers and work tugs often use this kind of engine.

[codex:cx_nozzle]
中：喷管
英：NOZZLE

[codex:cx_nozzle_a]
中：钟形喷管主要由加强环、冷却管和喉部集管组成，整体呈现向外扩张的形状，用来让发动机产生的高温燃气加速排出。
英：A bell nozzle consists mainly of stiffening rings, coolant tubes and a throat manifold. Its overall shape flares outwards, and it lets the high-temperature gas produced by the engine be expelled at high speed.

[codex:cx_nozzle_b]
中：喷管的体积很大，但外壁比较薄，搬运过程中受到碰撞就可能发生变形。内部的冷却管里也可能残留着冷却液，拆卸前需要检查。
英：A nozzle is very large but its outer wall is fairly thin, so it may deform if it is knocked while being moved. The coolant tubes inside may also hold leftover coolant, which must be checked before removal.

[codex:cx_nozzle_c]
中：先把冷却管里残留的液体排放干净，再沿着法兰连接的位置拆卸喷管。搬运时要尽量放慢速度，避免喷管与周围的设备发生碰撞。
英：First drain the liquid left in the coolant tubes, then unbolt the nozzle at the flange connection. Move it as slowly as you can, so the nozzle does not collide with the equipment around it.

[codex:cx_nozzle_x]
中：喷管通过让高温燃气不断膨胀，把燃气的一部分热能转化成高速运动的动能。对于适合真空环境的喷管来说，出口面积与喉部面积的比值越大，通常就能获得更高的比冲。部分真空喷管的扩张比可以超过 100，喷管壁也会做得很薄，所以拆卸和搬运时都需要格外小心。
英：A nozzle lets the hot gas keep expanding and turns part of the gas’s heat energy into high-speed kinetic energy. For a nozzle suited to vacuum, the larger the ratio of exit area to throat area, the higher the specific impulse usually obtained. The expansion ratio of some vacuum nozzles can exceed 100, and the nozzle wall is also made very thin, so take extra care when removing and moving it.

[codex:cx_rcs_quad]
中：姿控推力器组
英：RCS THRUSTER QUAD

[codex:cx_rcs_quad_a]
中：一个安装座上装着四个小型推力器，每个推力器都配有电磁阀，组件内部还装有用于维持工作温度的加热片。
英：Four small thrusters are fitted on one mounting block, each with a solenoid valve, and the assembly also holds heater pads that keep the working temperature.

[codex:cx_rcs_quad_b]
中：如果电磁阀还连接着电源，拆卸时意外触发就可能让推力器突然喷射。管道里面也可能残留推进剂，需要确认安全以后才能拆卸。
英：If the solenoid valves are still connected to power, accidental triggering during removal may make a thruster suddenly fire. The lines may also hold leftover propellant, so removal must wait until it is confirmed safe.

[codex:cx_rcs_quad_c]
中：先切断推力器的电源，再关闭推进剂贮箱的阀门，通过排放口排空管道里残留的推进剂，确认管路已经没有压力以后，再开始拆卸推力器组。
英：First cut the thrusters’ power, then close the valves of the propellant tank, drain the propellant left in the lines through the drain, and start removing the thruster cluster after confirming the lines have no pressure.

[codex:cx_rcs_quad_x]
中：使用单组元肼推进剂的推力器，会让肼经过内部的催化床，通过分解反应产生高温气体，再从喷口排出产生推力，单个推力器通常能提供几十牛到几百牛的推力。加热片负责让催化床保持在合适的工作温度，方便推力器在需要时正常启动。
英：A thruster using monopropellant hydrazine passes the hydrazine through an internal catalyst bed, produces hot gas by a decomposition reaction, and sends it out of the nozzle to make thrust. One thruster can usually provide tens to hundreds of newtons of thrust. The heater pad keeps the catalyst bed at the right working temperature, so the thruster starts normally when needed.

[codex:cx_turbopump]
中：涡轮泵
英：TURBOPUMP

[codex:cx_turbopump_a]
中：燃料泵和氧化剂泵安装在同一根转轴上，中间装有一组涡轮，工作时由涡轮带动两侧的泵一起转动。
英：The fuel pump and the oxidiser pump are mounted on the same shaft, with a turbine between them, and while working, the turbine drives the pumps on both sides round together.

[codex:cx_turbopump_b]
中：燃料和氧化剂都经过这台设备，停机以后，两侧的泵壳里可能还残留着推进剂。如果拆卸时发生泄漏，两种推进剂接触后就可能引起燃烧或爆炸。
英：Both fuel and oxidiser pass through this equipment, and after it stops, propellant may remain in the casings of the pumps on both sides. If a leak happens during removal, the two propellants may burn or explode when they meet.

[codex:cx_turbopump_c]
中：拆卸之前，要分别排空燃料泵和氧化剂泵里的残留推进剂，确认两侧都已经排空并完成隔离以后，才能开始拆卸。
英：Before removal, drain the residual propellant in the fuel pump and the oxidiser pump separately, and start removal only after confirming both sides have been drained and isolated.

[codex:cx_turbopump_x]
中：涡轮泵的体积虽然不大，功率却很高，一台只有几十公斤重的设备就能输出几兆瓦的功率，转速也能达到每分钟几万转。燃料和氧化剂之间依靠轴封隔离，一旦轴封损坏，两种推进剂就可能混合，严重时会直接引起爆炸。
英：A turbopump is not large, but its power is very high: a unit weighing only a few tens of kilograms can put out several megawatts, at several tens of thousands of revolutions per minute. The fuel and oxidiser are kept apart by shaft seals, and once a shaft seal is damaged, the two propellants may mix, and in serious cases this causes an explosion directly.

[codex:cx_apu]
中：辅助动力装置
英：AUXILIARY POWER UNIT

[codex:cx_apu_a]

[codex:cx_ntr_core]

[codex:cx_ntr_core_a]
中：由六角形燃料元件、铍反射层和控制鼓组成，反应堆通过核裂变产生高温，将氢气加热后从喷管喷出，为火箭提供推力。
英：It consists of hexagonal fuel elements, a beryllium reflector and control drums. The reactor produces high temperature by nuclear fission, heats hydrogen, and expels it from the nozzle to give the rocket thrust.

[codex:cx_ntr_core_b]
中：反应堆关闭以后，堆芯仍然有很强的辐射，而且还会持续产生热量。在游戏中，如果使用激光切割到堆芯，就会直接引发核爆。

[codex:cx_ntr_core_c]

英：When removing it, stay inside the radiation shielded area, wait until the core has cooled fully, then lock the control drums, and after confirming it is safe, lift the whole core out.

[codex:cx_ntr_core_x]
中：核热火箭利用核裂变产生的热量，将氢气加热到 2500 K 以上，再通过喷管高速排出。它的比冲可以达到约 900 秒，差不多是普通化学火箭的两倍。反应堆关闭以后，堆芯还会产生衰变热，初期大约相当于满功率运行时的 1%，随后会随着时间逐渐降低。
英：A nuclear thermal rocket uses the heat from nuclear fission to heat hydrogen to above 2500 K, and then expels it at high speed through a nozzle. Its specific impulse can reach about 900 seconds, roughly twice that of an ordinary chemical rocket. After the reactor is shut down, the core still produces decay heat, which at first is about 1% of the heat at full-power running, and then gradually falls with time.

[codex:cx_nav_rack]

[codex:cx_nav_rack_c]

英：A UPS normally keeps its internal battery charged. When the main supply is disconnected, it switches to the battery at once and can usually keep running for anywhere from a few minutes to a few hours. Data equipment in a rack is generally valuable, so keep it intact: removing the whole rack sells for more.

英：Avionics units are usually built to aviation standards such as ARINC, so the whole unit can be replaced in one piece. The front panel has two handles for easy fitting and removal, and the rear connectors slot straight into the rack, so each wire does not need to be connected separately.

[codex:cx_console]

英：A console is mainly used to view equipment status and send control commands. The screen shows the data the sensors last reported. If a sensor has stopped working, or there is a communication fault, the data on the screen may never update.
