英：A ring laser gyro measures rotation using the frequency difference between two laser beams travelling in opposite directions. This principle is called the Sagnac effect. Because there are no rotating mechanical parts inside, its measuring accuracy is usually high.

英：A star tracker photographs the sky, then compares the positions of the stars in the picture with a star catalogue to calculate the ship’s attitude, with an accuracy of down to the arcsecond level. 英：A hook block uses several sheaves to reduce the pulling force needed to lift a load. The more rope segments carry the load, the less force is usually needed. [codex:cx_reel]

[codex:cx_reel_c]

[codex:cx_reel_x]

英：A steel cable under tension stores some elastic energy, which can be calculated with the formula F²L/(2EA). Take a long steel cable carrying a force of several tens of kilonewtons: if it is suddenly released, the energy let out is enough to damage equipment. So always check the tension gauge before removal and confirm the cable is no longer under load.

英：When a mechanical arm is folded, its joints may still be under hydraulic pressure or preload. Even if the power is already off, these forces do not disappear at once. When removing it, release the stored energy first, then release the joint locks, and only then remove the connecting structure.

[codex:cx_brake]

[codex:cx_brake_a]

英：A disc brake is made up of a caliper, pistons, brake pads and a hydraulic line. The caliper is mounted on the edge of the brake disc and slows the rotation by clamping the disc.

[codex:cx_brake_c]

英：First turn on the brake cooling system, then apply the brake. Remove the brake only after the equipment has stopped rotating completely.

[codex:cx_brake_x]

英：A brake turns kinetic energy from rotation into heat through friction between the brake pads and the brake disc. Take a flywheel spinning at 20,000 rpm: to bring it to a complete stop, the brake pads have to absorb a large amount of heat. If cooling is not good enough, once the pad temperature exceeds its limit the brake may fail, and the flywheel cannot be stopped properly.

中：机床值钱的地方主要在加工精度，所以拆卸的时候要尽量保护导轨和主轴。如果导轨磨损严重，或者在拆卸时受到撞击发生变形，机床就很难继续使用，最后可能只能按照废铁的价格回收。
英：A machine tool is valuable mainly for its machining accuracy, so protect the ways and the spindle as much as possible during removal. If the ways are badly worn, or are deformed by a knock during removal, the machine tool can hardly be used any more, and may end up recovered only at the price of scrap iron.

英：A standard shipping container is held and takes handling loads mainly through the corner castings at its four corners, and containers can be joined to one another with twist-locks. [codex:cx_stator]
中：直线电机定子
英：LINEAR MOTOR STATOR

[codex:cx_stator_a]
中：直线电机的固定部分，里面有叠片铁芯和安装在凹槽里的线圈，两端还能看到露出来的线圈绕组。
英：The fixed part of a linear motor. Inside are a laminated iron core and coils fitted in slots, and the end windings sticking out can be seen at both ends.

[codex:cx_stator_b]
中：定子的电源来自电容组，就算切断了主电源，电容组里也可能存着几百伏的高压电，直接拆卸会有触电危险。
英：The stator is powered from a capacitor bank. Even if the main supply is cut, the capacitor bank may still hold a high voltage of several hundred volts, and removing it directly risks an electric shock.

[codex:cx_stator_c]
中：先把电容组里的电放干净，确认没有残余电压以后，再断开线路并拆卸线圈。
英：First discharge the capacitor bank completely. After confirming there is no residual voltage, disconnect the wiring and remove the coils.

[codex:cx_stator_x]
中：直线电机的工作原理和普通旋转电机差不多，只是定子沿着轨道铺开，通电以后会产生沿轨道移动的磁场，推动动子向前运动。电磁弹射系统就利用这个原理，能在短短几秒内把载荷加速到每秒几百米。
英：A linear motor works in much the same way as an ordinary rotary motor, except that the stator is laid out along a track. When powered, it produces a magnetic field that travels along the track and pushes the mover forward. Electromagnetic launch systems use this principle to accelerate a payload to several hundred metres per second in just a few seconds.

[codex:cx_launch_cradle]

[codex:cx_launch_cradle_x]

英：During launch, the cradle has to take a load from an acceleration of several tens of g, so its overall structure is thick and heavy and needs enough strength to withstand the shock of launch.

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor]
中：飞轮储能器
英：FLYWHEEL STORE

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor_a]
中：飞轮储能器的外壳内部是真空环境，里面装着复合材料飞轮、磁悬浮轴承，还有一套可以发电和驱动飞轮旋转的电机。
英：The housing of a flywheel energy store is a vacuum inside. It holds a composite-material flywheel, magnetic bearings and a motor that can both generate power and drive the flywheel.

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor_b]
中：飞轮可能还在高速旋转，这时候直接松开固定装置或者切割外壳，里面的飞轮可能会飞出去，造成严重损坏。
英：The flywheel may still be spinning at high speed. If the fixings are released or the housing is cut at this point, the flywheel inside may fly out and cause serious damage.

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor_c]
中：先启动刹车冷却系统，再使用刹车降低飞轮转速，等转速降到 300 rpm 以下以后，才能开始拆卸。
英：First start the brake cooling system, then use the brake to slow the flywheel. Start removal only after its speed has dropped below 300 rpm.

[codex:cx_flywheel_rotor_x]
中：飞轮通过高速旋转来储存能量，计算公式是 E = ½Iω²，储存的能量和转速的平方成正比。拿同一个飞轮来说，转速达到 20,000 rpm 时，储存的能量就是 2,000 rpm 时的 100 倍。由于内部采用了真空环境和磁悬浮轴承，旋转阻力很小，就算不再供电，也能继续旋转好几天。
英：A flywheel stores energy by spinning at high speed. The formula is E = ½Iω², so the stored energy is proportional to the square of the speed. For the same flywheel, at 20,000 rpm the stored energy is 100 times that at 2,000 rpm. Because of the vacuum inside and the magnetic bearings, rotational drag is very small, and even without power the flywheel can keep spinning for several days.

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel]
中：反作用飞轮
英：REACTION WHEEL

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel_a]
中：反作用飞轮里面装有飞轮、轴承和电机线圈，飞船可以通过调整飞轮的转速来改变自身的姿态。
英：A reaction wheel contains a flywheel, bearings and motor coils. The ship can change its attitude by adjusting the speed of the wheel.

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel_b]
中：即使已经关闭电源，里面的飞轮也可能还在高速旋转，直接拆卸可能造成设备损坏。
英：Even after the power is switched off, the wheel inside may still be spinning at high speed, and removing it directly may damage the equipment.

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel_c]

英：Before removal, check the speed of the wheel first. Remove the whole unit only after confirming the wheel has stopped spinning completely.

[codex:cx_reaction_wheel_x]
中：反作用飞轮利用角动量守恒来控制飞船姿态。当电机带动飞轮向一个方向加速旋转时，飞船就会受到反方向的转动力矩，从而调整姿态。飞轮的正常工作转速通常可以达到每分钟几千转。
英：A reaction wheel uses conservation of angular momentum to control the ship’s attitude. When the motor spins the wheel up in one direction, the ship receives a torque in the opposite direction and so changes its attitude. The normal operating speed of the wheel can usually reach several thousand revolutions per minute.

[codex:cx_despin_brake]
中：消旋制动器
英：DESPIN BRAKE

[codex:cx_despin_brake_a]
中：安装在旋转居住环轮毂上的制动装置，主要由制动盘、卡钳和散热片组成，用来降低居住环的旋转速度。
英：A braking unit mounted on the hub of a rotating habitat ring, made up mainly of a brake disc, calipers and cooling fins. It is used to reduce the rotation speed of the ring.

[codex:cx_despin_brake_b]
中：如果居住环上的设备还在用电，直接启动刹车可能产生过大的回馈电流，烧坏供电滑环。如果没有开启冷却系统，刹车产生的高温还可能损坏制动器。
英：If equipment on the habitat ring is still drawing power, applying the brake directly may produce an excessive regenerated current and burn out the power slip ring. If the cooling system is not turned on, the high temperature produced by braking may also damage the brake.

[codex:cx_despin_brake_c]
中：先切断居住环上所有设备的电源，再开启制动器的冷却系统，确认准备完成以后，同时对两个居住环进行制动。

[codex:cx_despin_brake_x]
中：制动时，电机可以把居住环旋转产生的动能转化成电能，储存在电容器里，这个过程叫再生制动。等电容器充满以后，需要先释放里面的电能，才能继续使用这种制动方式。两个居住环需要一起减速，如果只刹住其中一个，另一个居住环的角动量可能让船体发生扭转。
英：During braking, the motor can convert the kinetic energy of the ring’s rotation into electrical energy and store it in a capacitor. This process is called regenerative braking. Once the capacitor is full, its energy must be released before this braking method can continue. The two habitat rings need to slow down together: if only one is braked, the angular momentum of the other ring may twist the hull.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal]
中：旋转气密封
英：ROTARY SEAL

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_a]
中：安装在旋转轮毂和固定主轴之间的密封装置，用来减少两者之间的气体泄漏。
英：A sealing device fitted between the rotating hub and the fixed spine, used to reduce gas leakage between the two.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_b]
中：如果轮毂还在旋转，就直接打开过渡气闸，可能会把里面的密封结构撕坏。
英：If the transfer airlock is opened while the hub is still turning, the sealing structure inside may be torn apart.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_c]
中：先让轮毂完全停止旋转，再锁定轴承，确认固定好以后才能开始拆卸。
英：First let the hub stop turning completely, then lock the bearing. Start removal only after confirming it is secured.

[codex:cx_rotary_seal_x]
中：迷宫密封内部有很多圈密封齿，气体经过这些位置时，流动阻力会逐渐增加，从而减少泄漏。中间还有缓冲气槽，可以隔开两侧的气体。由于密封结构之间没有直接接触，所以轮毂旋转的时候也能保持密封，不过如果出现了较大的开口，气体还是会直接泄漏。
英：A labyrinth seal has many rings of sealing teeth inside. As gas passes through them, the flow resistance builds up step by step, which reduces leakage. In the middle there is also a buffer-gas groove that separates the gas on the two sides. Because the sealing parts do not touch each other, the seal holds even while the hub turns, but if a large opening appears, the gas will still leak straight out.

[codex:cx_spoke_lift]
中：轮辐电梯
英：SPOKE ELEVATOR

[codex:cx_spoke_lift_a]
中：安装在旋转环轮辐上的电梯，轿厢通过导轨和齿条上下移动，配有舱门、驱动电机和防止轿厢失控的安全钳。
英：A lift fitted on the spoke of a rotating ring. The car moves up and down on guide rails and a rack, and has doors, a drive motor and safety gear that stops the car running away.

[codex:cx_spoke_lift_b]

[codex:cx_spoke_lift_c]

英：First stop the rotating ring from turning, and start removing the lift only after confirming the car has come to a steady stop.

[codex:cx_spoke_lift_x]
中：旋转环通过转动产生离心力，让里面的人感觉到类似重力的效果。拿半径 100 米的旋转环来说，每分钟转动 3 圈，就能产生大约 1 g 的人工重力。电梯从中央轮毂移动到外环的过程中，乘客感受到的人工重力也会从接近零逐渐增加到 1 g。
英：A rotating ring produces centrifugal force by turning, which gives the people inside an effect like gravity. For a ring with a radius of 100 m, turning 3 times per minute produces about 1 g of artificial gravity. As the lift moves from the central hub out to the outer ring, the artificial gravity felt by passengers also rises gradually from nearly zero to 1 g.

[codex:cx_tether_damper]
中：系绳阻尼器
英：TETHER DAMPER

[codex:cx_tether_damper_a]

[codex:cx_tether_damper_x]

英：A tether that is too long tends to vibrate, in much the same way as a taut string. The damper can use its hydraulic structure to absorb the energy of the vibration and convert it into heat, reducing the swaying of the tether.

[codex:cx_hyd_clamp]
中：液压夹钳
英：HYDRAULIC CLAMP

[codex:cx_hyd_clamp_a]
中：液压夹钳由固定钳口、液压缸控制的活动钳口和底部安装板组成，主要用来夹紧和固定设备。
英：A hydraulic clamp is made up of a fixed jaw, a moving jaw controlled by a hydraulic cylinder and a base mounting plate. It is mainly used to clamp and hold equipment in place.

[codex:cx_hyd_clamp_b]

[codex:cx_hyd_clamp_x]

英：A hydraulic clamp keeps its clamping state with a hydraulic lock, so even if the hydraulic pump stops, the internal pressure can be maintained for a long time. When several clamps hold one piece of equipment together, the order of release also matters during removal, because each time a clamp is released, the remaining clamps have to carry more load.

[codex:cx_iso_table]
中：隔振台
英：VIBRATION ISOLATION TABLE

[codex:cx_iso_table_a]
中：一种用来放置精密设备的工作台，上面是蜂窝结构的台面，下面装着气动隔振支腿，还有用来调整台面高度的水平阀。
英：A workbench for holding precision equipment. It has a honeycomb tabletop on top, pneumatic isolation legs underneath, and levelling valves for adjusting the height of the tabletop.

[codex:cx_iso_table_b]
中：隔振台没有锁定的时候，如果在附近进行切割或者发生碰撞，台面可能会产生晃动，导致上面的精密设备受到损坏。
英：When an isolation table is not locked, cutting or a collision nearby may make the tabletop shake, which can damage the precision equipment on it.

[codex:cx_iso_table_c]

英：First lock the isolation table, and start cutting the nearby structure only after confirming the tabletop is secured.

[codex:cx_iso_table_x]
中：隔振台通过气动支腿减少外部振动的影响，可以把几赫兹以上的振动降低到原来的 1% 以下。锁定以后，台面会和支架固定在一起，不能继续自由晃动，可以避免拆卸时因为台面移动而损坏设备。
英：An isolation table uses pneumatic legs to reduce the effect of outside vibration, and can cut vibration above a few hertz to less than 1% of its original level. Once locked, the tabletop is fixed to the frame and can no longer sway freely, which avoids damaging the equipment through tabletop movement during removal.

[codex:cx_escape_pod]
中：救生舱
英：ESCAPE POD

[codex:cx_escape_pod_a]

英：An escape pod mounted on a launch cradle. It has a heat shield and a side hatch outside, crew seats inside, and a retro-rocket pack and launch clamps at the bottom.

[codex:cx_escape_pod_b]

英：The launch clamps contain pyrotechnic charges. If the arming circuit is still powered, the pod may be ejected by accident, so take special care during removal.

[codex:cx_escape_pod_c]

英：First switch off the arming switch, and confirm the launch circuit is dead. Then remove the launch clamps and the pyrotechnic charges inside.

[codex:cx_escape_pod_x]
中：爆炸螺栓内部装有少量炸药，只需要几安培的电流持续几毫秒，就能完成起爆。发射电容使用独立的供电回路，即使主电源已经断开，也可能保留足够的电能来触发爆炸螺栓。
英：An explosive bolt contains a small amount of explosive, and a current of only a few amps lasting a few milliseconds is enough to set it off. The launch capacitor uses a separate supply circuit, so even if the main power is already disconnected, it may keep enough energy to fire the explosive bolts.

[codex:cx_safe_deposit]

英：The vault door uses a solenoid to control its locking bolts, and as long as any one supply is still connected, the mechanical release lever cannot be pulled. After all supplies are disconnected, the bolts still stay in their original position, and the release lever has to be pulled by hand to retract them.

[codex:cx_pwr_core]
中：压水堆
英：PRESSURISED-WATER REACTOR

[codex:cx_pwr_core_a]
中：压水堆的主体包括压力容器、顶部的螺栓环、控制棒驱动管、堆芯和下腔室。
英：The main body of a pressurised-water reactor includes the pressure vessel, the stud ring on top, the control rod drive housings, the core and the lower plenum.

[codex:cx_pwr_core_b]
中：即使反应堆已经停止运行，内部仍然有很强的辐射，而且会持续产生热量。一次冷却回路里的水也处于高温高压状态，拆卸时要特别小心。
英：Even after the reactor has stopped running, there is still strong radiation inside, and it keeps producing heat. The water in the primary cooling loop is also at high temperature and high pressure, so take special care during removal.

[codex:cx_pwr_core_c]
中：先保持冷却系统正常运行，等反应堆的温度降下来以后，再做好辐射屏蔽措施，将整个设备拆下来，通过危险品出口运走。
英：First keep the cooling system running normally and wait for the reactor temperature to drop. Then put radiation shielding measures in place, remove the whole unit, and send it out through the hazard mouth.

[codex:cx_pwr_core_x]
中：压水堆的一次冷却回路通常保持在 15.5 MPa 左右，水温可以达到 315℃，由于内部压力很高，水在这个温度下仍然不会沸腾。反应堆停止运行一小时后，衰变热大约还有额定热功率的 1.5%，一天后也有约 0.5%。拿一座热功率为 100 MW 的反应堆来说，即使停堆一天，内部仍然会持续产生大约 0.5 MW 的热量，所以冷却系统还得继续运行。
英：The primary cooling loop of a pressurised-water reactor is usually kept at about 15.5 MPa, and the water temperature can reach 315 °C. Because the internal pressure is so high, the water still does not boil at this temperature. One hour after the reactor stops, the decay heat is still about 1.5% of rated thermal power, and about 0.5% after one day. Take a reactor with a thermal power of 100 MW: even a day after shutdown it still keeps producing about 0.5 MW of heat, so the cooling system has to keep running.

[codex:cx_crdm]
中：控制棒驱动机构
英：CONTROL ROD DRIVE

[codex:cx_crdm_a]
中：用来控制反应堆控制棒位置的设备，主要由耐压外壳、提升线圈、保持线圈、棒位指示装置和驱动杆组成。

英：This mechanism is also part of the reactor’s primary loop pressure boundary. If the primary loop has not been depressurised, removing it directly may make high-temperature coolant spray out.

[codex:cx_crdm_c]
中：先给反应堆一回路泄压，等冷却剂温度降下来以后，再拆卸控制棒驱动机构。

[codex:cx_crdm_x]
中：磁力提升式驱动机构通过电磁线圈控制控制棒的位置，一旦失去电源，机构就会释放控制棒，让控制棒依靠重力落入堆芯，使反应堆自动停堆。这种设计叫作失电安全。
英：A magnetic-jack drive mechanism controls the position of the control rods with electromagnetic coils. Once power is lost, the mechanism releases the rods, and they drop into the core under gravity, so the reactor shuts down automatically. This design is called fail-safe.

[codex:cx_rcp]

[codex:cx_rcp_b]

英：The reactor coolant pump keeps the core cooling circulation going. If the power is cut before the reactor has cooled enough, the coolant flow gradually falls and the core temperature may rise again.

[codex:cx_rcp_c]

英：First let the reactor finish shutting down and cooling, and cut the power to the reactor coolant pump only after confirming the core temperature has dropped to a safe range.

[codex:cx_rcp_x]

英：The flywheel on the reactor coolant pump keeps the pump turning after a power loss, so the coolant flow falls off slowly, which usually gives the core several tens of seconds of extra cooling time.

[codex:cx_pressurizer]
中：稳压器
英：PRESSURISER

[codex:cx_pressurizer_a]
中：用来控制反应堆一回路压力的大型容器，内部上方留有蒸汽空间，还装有喷淋头、电加热器和安全阀。
英：A large vessel for controlling the pressure of the reactor primary loop. Inside, there is a steam space in the upper part, and it also holds a spray head, electric heaters and relief valves.

[codex:cx_pressurizer_b]

英：The pressuriser holds high-pressure hot water and steam. If it is removed directly before the primary loop has been depressurised, the hot water and steam inside may spray out.

[codex:cx_pressurizer_c]

英：First let the primary loop finish depressurising and cooling. Remove the pressuriser only after you have confirmed that the internal pressure and temperature are both within safe limits.

[codex:cx_pressurizer_x]
中：稳压器通过加热器和喷淋系统来调整一回路的压力。需要升压时，加热器会把一部分水变成蒸汽；需要降压时，就通过喷淋冷水让部分蒸汽冷凝。在 15.5 MPa 的压力下，水的饱和温度大约是 345°C。
英：The pressuriser adjusts the pressure of the primary loop with heaters and a spray system. To raise the pressure, the heaters turn some of the water into steam. To lower it, cold water is sprayed in and condenses some of the steam. At a pressure of 15.5 MPa, the saturation temperature of water is about 345°C.

[codex:cx_steam_turbine]
中：汽轮机
英：STEAM TURBINE

[codex:cx_steam_turbine_a]

英：A steam turbine consists of a high-pressure cylinder and a low-pressure cylinder. The rotors inside are joined on the same shaft, and the blades grow longer from front to back.

[codex:cx_steam_turbine_b]
中：汽轮机通常有几十吨重，而且停机以后，转子可能还在缓慢转动，直接拆卸会有危险。
英：A steam turbine usually weighs several tens of tonnes, and after shutdown the rotor may still be turning slowly, so removing it directly is dangerous.

[codex:cx_steam_turbine_c]

英：First confirm that the rotor has stopped completely, then isolate the steam lines, and after that take the turbine apart in sections along its flange joints.

[codex:cx_steam_turbine_x]
中：蒸汽经过汽轮机时，会在每一级叶片之间逐渐膨胀，压力降低的同时体积也会增大，所以后面需要使用更长的叶片。大型汽轮机通常重达几十吨。
英：As steam passes through the turbine, it expands gradually between the stages of blades. Its pressure falls and its volume grows, so the later stages need longer blades. A large turbine usually weighs several tens of tonnes.

[codex:cx_condenser]
中：凝汽器
英：CONDENSER

[codex:cx_condenser_a]
中：负责把汽轮机排出的蒸汽冷却成水的设备，主要由壳体、管束、水室和热井组成，冷凝后的水会收集在热井里。

[codex:cx_condenser_b]

[codex:cx_condenser_c]

英：First drain all the cooling water and condensate, and confirm that the internal pressure has returned to normal, then begin removing the condenser.

[codex:cx_condenser_x]
中：凝汽器通常在接近真空的低压环境下工作，蒸汽接触冷却管束后会逐渐凝结成水，体积能够缩小上千倍，从而帮助凝汽器维持内部的低压状态。
英：A condenser normally works at a low pressure close to a vacuum. When steam touches the cooling tubes it gradually condenses into water, and its volume shrinks by a factor of thousands, which helps the condenser keep its internal pressure low.

[codex:cx_control_drum]
中：控制鼓
英：CONTROL DRUM

[codex:cx_control_drum_a]
中：一种用来调节反应堆反应性的装置，鼓体由铍制成，一侧装有碳化硼吸收材料，可以通过电机控制旋转角度。
英：A device for adjusting the reactivity of the reactor. The drum is made of beryllium, with boron carbide absorber material fitted on one side, and a motor controls its angle of rotation.

[codex:cx_control_drum_b]
中：如果吊装堆芯前没有固定好控制鼓，吊装过程中控制鼓可能发生转动，让吸收材料偏离原来的位置，导致反应堆反应性升高，附近的辐射剂量也可能突然增加。
英：If the control drums are not secured before the core is lifted, a drum may turn during the lift and move the absorber away from its original position. This raises the reactivity of the reactor, and the radiation dose nearby may also rise suddenly.

[codex:cx_control_drum_c]
中：吊装堆芯之前，先用机械锁定装置固定好每一个控制鼓，确认它们不会转动以后，再开始吊装。
英：Before lifting the core, secure every control drum with its mechanical lock, confirm that none of them can turn, and only then begin the lift.

[codex:cx_control_drum_x]
中：控制鼓通过旋转来调节堆芯的中子数量。当碳化硼吸收材料朝向堆芯时，会吸收更多中子，降低反应性；当铍制反射面朝向堆芯时，会将更多中子反射回去，提高反应性。
英：A control drum adjusts the number of neutrons in the core by rotating. When the boron carbide absorber faces the core, it absorbs more neutrons and lowers the reactivity. When the beryllium reflecting face faces the core, it reflects more neutrons back and raises the reactivity.

[codex:cx_shadow_shield]
中：阴影屏蔽板
英：SHADOW SHIELD

[codex:cx_shadow_shield_a]
中：由钨和氢化锂交替叠放制成的屏蔽板，主要用来阻挡反应堆堆芯产生的辐射，保护屏蔽板后方的人员和设备。
英：A shield plate made of alternating layers of tungsten and lithium hydride. It mainly blocks the radiation produced by the reactor core, protecting the people and equipment behind it.

[codex:cx_shadow_shield_b]
中：屏蔽板后方的辐射水平比较低，如果提前把它拆走，原本受到保护的工作区域就会直接暴露在堆芯辐射下。
英：The radiation level behind the shield plate is low. If you remove the plate too early, the work area it used to protect is exposed directly to the radiation from the core.

[codex:cx_shadow_shield_c]
中：先用吊装设备固定好屏蔽板，让它继续挡在堆芯前方，工作人员可以在屏蔽板后面的低辐射区域进行拆卸。
英：First secure the shield plate with lifting equipment so that it stays in front of the core. The crew can then work on the removal in the low-radiation area behind the plate.

[codex:cx_shadow_shield_x]

英：The tungsten in the shield plate mainly blocks gamma rays, and the lithium hydride slows and absorbs neutrons. Under design conditions, the radiation dose rate behind the plate can be reduced to about 1% of the original. A space reactor usually carries this kind of shield plate only in the direction where the crew are, which reduces the weight of the whole installation.

[codex:cx_tf_coil]
中：超导纵场线圈
英：SUPERCONDUCTING TF COIL

[codex:cx_tf_coil_a]
中：线圈外面有一层钢制外壳，里面是用铠装超导导体绕成的线圈，每一匝导体内部都有液氦流过，用来维持超导所需的低温。
英：The coil has a steel case on the outside. Inside is a winding of armoured superconducting conductor, and liquid helium flows through the inside of every turn of conductor to keep the low temperature that superconductivity needs.

[codex:cx_tf_coil_b]
中：线圈内部储存着几十亿焦耳的磁场能量，如果冷却系统失效，或者提前拆掉支撑结构，就可能发生失超，让储存的能量在短时间内大量释放，造成严重损坏。
英：The coil stores several gigajoules of magnetic energy. If the cooling system fails, or the support structure is removed too early, the coil may quench and release a large amount of the stored energy in a short time, causing serious damage.

[codex:cx_tf_coil_c]
中：先打开泄能开关，同时保持冷却系统正常运行，等线圈储存的磁场能量全部释放以后，再开始拆卸。
英：First open the dump switch while keeping the cooling system running normally. Begin removal only after all the magnetic energy stored in the coil has been released.

[codex:cx_tf_coil_x]
中：Nb₃Sn 超导材料在适当条件下，温度低于约 18 K 时可以进入超导状态，电阻降到零。实际运行时通常会用约 4.5 K 的液氦进行冷却。如果线圈发生失超，部分导体就会恢复电阻，电流经过这些位置时会产生大量热量，导致附近的导体也逐渐失去超导状态。严重时，整个过程只需要几秒钟，大量磁场能量就会转化成热量，并产生很大的机械作用力。
英：Under suitable conditions, Nb₃Sn superconducting material enters the superconducting state below about 18 K, and its resistance drops to zero. In actual operation it is usually cooled with liquid helium at about 4.5 K. If the coil quenches, part of the conductor regains resistance, and the current passing through those places produces a large amount of heat, so the neighbouring conductor gradually loses superconductivity too. In serious cases the whole process takes only a few seconds, and a large amount of magnetic energy turns into heat and produces a great mechanical force.

[codex:cx_cs_stack]
中：中心螺线管
英：CENTRAL SOLENOID

[codex:cx_cs_stack_a]
中：由多层饼式线圈叠加组成，线圈中间留有通孔，外面装有拉紧板，上下两端还有预压法兰，用来固定整个结构。
英：It is built from many pancake windings stacked together, with a through bore in the middle, tie plates on the outside, and pre-compression flanges at the top and bottom ends to hold the whole structure.

[codex:cx_cs_stack_b]
中：中心螺线管内部同样储存着大量磁场能量，如果冷却系统失效，就可能发生失超，导致储存的能量在短时间内大量释放。
英：The central solenoid also stores a large amount of magnetic energy. If its cooling system fails, it may quench and release the stored energy in a short time.

[codex:cx_cs_stack_c]
中：先通过泄能系统释放线圈储存的磁场能量，确认泄能完成以后，再拆卸预压法兰和其他固定结构。
英：First release the magnetic energy stored in the coil through the dump system, and confirm that the discharge is complete. Then remove the pre-compression flanges and the other holding structures.

[codex:cx_cs_stack_x]
中：中心螺线管通过改变磁场，在等离子体内部感应出电流，工作原理和变压器类似。运行时，线圈需要承受很大的电磁力，所以必须提前使用预压结构将线圈固定好，防止线圈在受力时发生变形或位移。
英：The central solenoid induces a current inside the plasma by changing the magnetic field, on a principle similar to a transformer. In operation the coil has to withstand very large electromagnetic forces, so a pre-compression structure must be fitted in advance to hold the coil and prevent it from deforming or shifting under load.

[codex:cx_grav_support]
中：线圈支撑
英：COIL SUPPORT

[codex:cx_grav_support_a]
中：由两端的固定块和中间多层柔性薄板组成，主要用来支撑线圈，同时允许线圈在冷却收缩时产生少量位移，避免底座受到过大的拉力。
英：It consists of fixed blocks at the two ends and several layers of thin flexible plates in between. It mainly supports the coil while allowing it a small displacement as it contracts on cooling, so that the base is not subjected to excessive tension.

[codex:cx_grav_support_b]
中：如果线圈还没有完成泄能，就直接切割或者拆卸支撑结构，可能导致线圈发生位移或变形，严重时还会引起失超，释放大量储存的磁场能量。
英：If you cut or remove the support structure before the coil has been discharged, the coil may shift or deform, and in serious cases it may quench and release a large amount of stored magnetic energy.

[codex:cx_grav_support_c]
中：先确认线圈已经完成泄能，再拆卸两端的固定块和中间的柔性支撑板。
英：First confirm that the coil has been discharged, then remove the fixed blocks at the two ends and the flexible support plates in the middle.

[codex:cx_grav_support_x]
中：钢结构从室温冷却到约 4 K 时会发生收缩，几米长的部件可能缩短几毫米。中间的柔性板可以沿一个方向产生少量弯曲，同时在另一个方向保持足够的刚度，这样既能承受线圈的重量，也能适应冷却时产生的收缩，避免底座和连接部位受到损坏。
英：A steel structure shrinks when it is cooled from room temperature to about 4 K, and a part several metres long may shorten by several millimetres. The flexible plates in the middle can bend slightly in one direction while staying sufficiently stiff in the other. This lets them carry the weight of the coil and also accommodate the contraction during cooling, preventing damage to the base and the joints.

英：A neutral beam injector first accelerates deuterium ions to hundreds of keV, and then converts them into uncharged neutral atoms in the neutraliser. [codex:cx_dr_stage]

英：A dilution refrigerator cools by the principle that ³He must absorb heat when it enters the dilute phase, which can bring the mixing chamber down to about 10 mK. The plates are connected by thin coaxial lines and separated by supports of low thermal conductivity, which reduces heat transfer between the temperature layers, so the lower a plate is, the colder it stays.

中：超导量子比特通常需要在约 10 mK 的极低温环境下运行，温度变化、机械振动和外界杂散光都可能干扰量子态，影响计算结果。这类芯片的制造成本很高，也是整艘船上最值钱的单件设备之一。

Such chips are very expensive to make, and this is one of the most valuable single items on the whole ship.

[codex:cx_pulse_tube]

英：A telescope mirror has to keep a very high precision. [codex:cx_wfs]

英：WAVEFRONT SENSOR

[codex:cx_wfs_a]
中：内部装有微透镜阵列和相机，光线经过微透镜阵列后会形成许多光点，相机通过测量这些光点的位置变化，判断光线发生了多大的偏移。
英：Inside are a lenslet array and a camera. After the light passes through the lenslet array it forms many spots, and the camera measures how the positions of these spots shift to determine how far the light has been deflected.

[codex:cx_wfs_b]

[codex:cx_wfs_c]

[codex:cx_wfs_x]
中：这种设备使用的是夏克-哈特曼测量原理，微透镜会把进入的光线分成许多光点，相机测出每个光点偏离正常位置的距离，就能计算出对应位置的波前倾斜程度，再根据测量结果调整镜面，减少光学系统产生的误差。
英：This device works on the Shack-Hartmann principle. The lenslets split the incoming light into many spots, and the camera measures how far each spot is from its normal position. From this the local slope of the wavefront can be calculated, and the mirror is then adjusted according to the result to reduce the errors of the optical system.

[codex:cx_spectrograph]

[codex:cx_spectrograph_b]

[codex:cx_spectrograph_x]

英：A diffraction grating can have thousands of lines per millimetre. After passing through the grating, light of different wavelengths travels in different directions, and the detector can record each of them separately. By analysing the resulting spectral lines you can tell which elements the target contains, and you can also calculate the target’s velocity from the shift of the lines.

[codex:cx_coronagraph]

[codex:cx_coronagraph_c]

英：First measure the temperature of the equipment, wait for it to cool down, and then remove the whole coronagraph.

[codex:cx_coronagraph_x]
中：日冕的亮度大约只有太阳表面的百万分之一，要观察到这么微弱的光线，就得尽量减少其他光线的干扰。所以镜筒内部安装了很多层挡光环，用来阻挡多余的杂散光。
英：The brightness of the corona is only about one millionth of that of the solar surface. To observe light this faint, interference from other light must be reduced as far as possible. So many layers of baffle rings are fitted inside the tube to block the unwanted stray light.

[codex:cx_particle_det]
中：粒子探测器
英：PARTICLE DETECTOR

[codex:cx_particle_det_a]
中：用来检测粒子种类和能量的设备，准直器后面安装着多层硅探测器和吸收板，可以测量粒子穿过不同材料时损失的能量。
英：A device for detecting the kind and energy of particles. Behind the collimator are several layers of silicon detectors and absorber plates, which can measure the energy a particle loses as it passes through different materials.

[codex:cx_particle_det_b]

英：The silicon detectors inside are precision parts and may be damaged by knocks. The bias supply also produces high voltage, so do not remove the device directly while it is still powered.

[codex:cx_particle_det_c]
中：先关闭偏压电源，确认设备已经断电以后，再把整个粒子探测器完整拆下来，搬运时注意避免碰撞。
英：First switch off the bias supply and confirm that the equipment is de-energised. Then remove the whole particle detector, and avoid knocks while carrying it.

[codex:cx_particle_det_x]
中：粒子每穿过一层硅探测器，都会损失一部分能量。通过测量粒子在每一层损失了多少能量，以及它最终能穿透多深，就可以判断粒子的种类和能量大小。
英：Each time a particle passes through a layer of silicon detector, it loses some of its energy. By measuring how much energy the particle loses in each layer, and how deep it finally penetrates, you can determine the kind of the particle and its energy.

[codex:cx_magnetometer_x]

英：The metal materials and electrical equipment on the ship all produce magnetic fields that affect the magnetometer’s measurements, so the sensor head is usually mounted at the end of a long pole away from the hull, and the matching electronics box is mounted at the root of the boom. This reduces the interference of the hull with the measurements.

[codex:cx_lab]

[codex:cx_lab_c]

英：First cut the power to the equipment, then remove the whole instrument, carry it carefully to the equipment recovery mouth, and try not to damage the parts inside.

[codex:cx_lab_x]

英：A gas chromatograph can separate the components of a mixed gas by using the differences in boiling point and polarity between substances. A mass spectrometer measures the mass-to-charge ratio of ions, which is used to analyse which substances are in a sample and their relative amounts.

[codex:cx_immersion_rack]
中：浸没式服务器机柜
英：IMMERSION RACK

[codex:cx_immersion_rack_a]

[codex:cx_immersion_rack_b]

英：If you lift out the rack while the tank still holds coolant, the coolant spills out and short-circuits the neighbouring racks.

[codex:cx_immersion_rack_c]
中：先通过槽上的接口抽干冷却液，再吊出机柜。
英：Drain the coolant through the port on the tank first, then lift out the rack.

[codex:cx_immersion_rack_x]
中：绝缘液使用氟化液或合成油，能够直接接触芯片并带走热量，冷却效率比风冷高得多。绝缘液价格很高，泼洒会造成损失；在失重环境中，泼出的液团还会漂到相邻机柜上。
英：The dielectric fluid is a fluorinated fluid or a synthetic oil. It touches the chips directly and carries away heat, with much higher cooling efficiency than air. The fluid itself is very expensive, so spilling it causes a loss. In free fall, the spilt globules also drift onto the neighbouring racks.

[codex:cx_top_drive]

[codex:cx_drawworks]

英：Its line carries the whole string's pull.

[codex:cx_blocks]

英：CROWN AND TRAVELLING BLOCKS

[codex:cx_blocks_b]

英：While the line is taut, the crown block and the travelling block together form a tensioned pulley system.

[codex:cx_blocks_c]

英：Slacken the line first, then remove the crown block and the travelling block.

[codex:cx_blocks_x]

英：For a pulley system with 10 lines strung, the drawworks pull multiplied by about 10 gives the hook load.

[codex:cx_rotary_table]

英：ROTARY TABLE

[codex:cx_rotary_table_a]

英：Several kilometres of drill pipe can store elastic energy from twisting. When the rotary table stops, the drill string may still be twisted by several turns.

[codex:cx_mud_pump]
中：泥浆泵
英：MUD PUMP

[codex:cx_mud_pump_a]

英：The discharge pressure of a mud pump can reach 35 MPa. The nitrogen bladder inside the pulsation dampener absorbs the pressure fluctuation produced by each stroke.

[codex:cx_shaker]

[codex:cx_roughneck]

英：IRON ROUGHNECK

英：An iron roughneck makes up and breaks out the pipe threads in place of a crew. The upper wrench head spins the connection, and the lower wrench head tightens or loosens it with a torque of tens of thousands of newton-metres.

[codex:cx_bop]
中：防喷器组
英：BLOWOUT PREVENTER

[codex:cx_bop_a]

英：A BOP stack includes an annular preventer, three ram preventers and side outlet valves. One of the rams is a shear ram.

[codex:cx_bop_b]

英：There may still be well pressure below the closed rams.

[codex:cx_bop_c]

英：Bleed the pressure through the choke line or the kill line first, and remove the BOP stack only after confirming there is no pressure.

[codex:cx_bop_x]

英：In a kick, the BOP can shut in the wellhead within a few seconds, and the shear rams can cut the drill pipe and seal the well. The BOP stack is the last barrier of well control, and also one of the most expensive items on a drillship.

[codex:cx_well_manifold]

[codex:cx_ortho_para]

英：ORTHO-PARA CONVERTER

[codex:cx_ortho_para_a]
中：换热器通道内填有氧化铁催化剂，通道浸在冷浴中。
英：The passages of the heat exchanger are packed with iron oxide catalyst, and the passages are immersed in a cold bath.

[codex:cx_ortho_para_b]
中：转化器内有极冷的氢。
英：The converter holds very cold hydrogen.

[codex:cx_ortho_para_c]
中：先让转化器回温并释放压力，再进行拆卸。
英：Let the converter warm up and release the pressure first, then remove it.

[codex:cx_ortho_para_x]
中：常温下的氢有 75% 是正氢。氢液化后，正氢会自发转化为仲氢并释放热量，足以蒸发掉一罐液氢的大半。因此，液化时需要使用催化剂完成转化。
英：Hydrogen at room temperature is 75% ortho-hydrogen. After hydrogen is liquefied, the ortho-hydrogen converts spontaneously to para-hydrogen and gives off heat, enough to boil away most of a tank of liquid hydrogen. So a catalyst is used to complete the conversion during liquefaction.

[codex:cx_arc_furnace]

[codex:cx_arc_furnace_x]

英：Refractory conducts heat poorly, and when the temperature difference between inside and outside is large, thermal stress cracks the material. With the cooling loop working normally, the core takes about two and a half minutes to drop from 760 °C to 150 °C.

[codex:cx_m_munitions]

[codex:cx_m_munitions_b]

[codex:cx_m_munitions_c]

英：You can open the flood valve first to flood the magazine, which makes the rounds scrap and removes the danger. You can also work far away from the rounds.

[codex:cx_m_munitions_x]
中：激光在弹药一定范围内工作时会持续加热弹药，温度达到烤燃温度后，弹药就会起爆。弹药以超过一定速度撞击物体时也会起爆。弹库的热测点显示温度最高的那发弹药的读数。注水后的弹药不再受加热或撞击影响，但也失去价值。
英：When the laser works within a certain range of a round, it keeps heating the round, and once the temperature reaches the cook-off temperature the round explodes. A round also explodes if it hits an object faster than a certain speed. The magazine’s heat test point shows the reading of the hottest round. Rounds that have been flooded are no longer affected by heating or knocks, but they also lose their value.

[codex:cx_m_quench]
中：超导磁体失超
英：SUPERCONDUCTING MAGNET QUENCH

[codex:cx_m_quench_a]
中：八组超导线圈储存着磁能，液氦和冷箱负责维持超导状态。
英：Eight coil packs store magnetic energy, and liquid helium and the cold box keep them superconducting.

[codex:cx_m_quench_b]
中：如果线圈失去冷却并升温超过临界温度，或在线圈仍有磁场时切断支撑，线圈就会失超，瞬间释放能量，并引发相邻线圈连锁失超。
英：If a coil loses its cooling and warms above the critical temperature, or a support is cut while the coil still carries a field, the coil quenches and releases its energy instantly, and triggers a chain quench of the neighbouring coils.

[codex:cx_m_quench_c]
中：先合上泄能开关，保持冷箱供电。等储能读数降到安全值后，再断开冷却并拆卸线圈。
英：Close the dump switch first and keep the cold box powered. When the stored-energy reading has dropped to a safe value, then switch off the cooling and remove the coils.

[codex:cx_m_quench_x]
中：泄能时，电流经泄能电阻按 e^(−t/τ) 衰减，经过几个时间常数后会降到安全值以下。此时线圈升温也不会造成危害。如果先断开冷却，线圈会在储存全部能量时升温超过临界温度。失超释放的热量和电磁力会损毁线圈与周围设备，还会烧掉一部分昂贵的液氦。
英：When dumping, the current decays through the dump resistor as e^(−t/τ), and after a few time constants it falls below the safe value. At that point the coil can warm up without causing harm. If you switch off the cooling first, the coil warms above the critical temperature while still holding all its energy. The heat and electromagnetic forces released by the quench destroy the coil and the surrounding equipment, and also boil off some of the expensive liquid helium.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel]
中：还在转的飞轮
英：A FLYWHEEL STILL SPINNING

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_a]
中：飞轮在开局时的转速为 20,000 rpm，设备配有刹车、冷却系统和转速表。
英：The flywheel starts at 20,000 rpm. The equipment comes with a brake, a cooling system and a tachometer.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_b]
中：如果不开启冷却就直接刹车，卡钳会在 8 秒后烧坏，飞轮此后无法停下。如果在转速达到或超过 300 rpm 时松开飞轮，飞轮会甩出去。
英：If you brake without turning on the cooling, the caliper burns out after 8 s and the flywheel can no longer be stopped. If you free the flywheel at 300 rpm or above, it is thrown out.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_c]

英：Turn on the brake cooling first, then use the brake. Remove the flywheel once the speed has dropped below 300 rpm. Release the rail preload mechanisms one by one from the tail towards the head.

[codex:cx_m_flywheel_x]
中：开启冷却后，刹车每秒降低 250 rpm，飞轮从 20,000 rpm 降下来约需 80 秒；不开冷却时，降速减半。飞轮能量与转速的平方成正比，转速降至 300 rpm 时，能量只剩开局时的 0.02%。断开主电源后，两组电容仍带有 400 V 电压，并为上轨的直线电机供电，拆卸前需要先放电。
英：With cooling on, the brake takes 250 rpm off every second, so it takes about 80 s to bring the flywheel down from 20,000 rpm. Without cooling, the slowdown is half as fast. Flywheel energy is proportional to the square of its speed, so at 300 rpm only 0.02% of the starting energy is left. After the main supply is disconnected, the two capacitor banks still hold 400 V and supply the linear motor on the upper rail, so they must be discharged before removal.

[codex:cx_m_pyro]

[codex:cx_m_magnet]

[codex:cx_m_preload]
中：预紧机构
英：PRELOADED MECHANISMS

[codex:cx_m_preload_a]
中：这些臂、轴、铰链和缆索依靠液压蓄能器或锁销保持支撑，并储存着力。
英：These arms, shafts, hinges and cables are held in support by hydraulic accumulators or locking pins, and they store force.

[codex:cx_m_preload_b]
中：如果锁销尚未拔出或液压系统仍有压力就拆卸枢轴，机构会弹开，损坏自身和撞上的物体。
英：If you remove a pivot while a locking pin is still in or the hydraulic system is still under pressure, the mechanism springs open and damages itself and whatever it hits.

[codex:cx_m_preload_c]
中：先释放蓄能器内的压力，再拔出全部锁销。确认张力表归零后，最后拆卸枢轴。
英：Release the pressure in the accumulator first, then pull out all the locking pins. Once the tension gauge reads zero, remove the pivot last.

[codex:cx_m_preload_x]
中：预紧机构储存弹性能和液压能：拉缆的能量 E = F²L/2EA，蓄能器的能量 E ≈ pV·ln(p/p₀)。卸能顺序始终是“能量—锁—结构”。如果拆除锁销后没有泄压，全部作用力会集中在枢轴上，松开枢轴时就会释放。
英：A preload mechanism stores elastic energy and hydraulic energy. For a pulled cable, the energy is E = F²L/2EA; for an accumulator, E ≈ pV·ln(p/p₀). The order of unloading is always energy, then locks, then structure. If you remove the locking pins without releasing the pressure, all the force acts on the pivot, and it is released as soon as the pivot is loosened.

英：When a drone’s flight controller loses its link, it tries to take off on its own. Only a physically disconnected battery is safe. A drone delivered whole to the equipment bay is worth much more than one melted down for scrap.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank]
中：低温货罐
英：CRYOGENIC CARGO TANKS

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_a]
中：低温货罐安装在真空夹套内，罐内压力会随着温度变化。
英：The cryogenic cargo tanks are fitted inside vacuum jackets, and the pressure in a tank changes with its temperature.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_b]
中：货罐回温后，内部压力会升高。如果安全阀冻住，压力可能持续升高，最终导致货罐爆裂。真空夹套破损后，漏热速度会加快十倍。
英：When a tank warms up, the pressure inside rises. If the relief valve is frozen, the pressure can keep rising until the tank bursts. When the vacuum jacket is damaged, heat leaks in ten times faster.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_c]

英：Keep the chillers running and draw the liquid off through the tank ports. If a relief valve is frozen, heat it to thaw it first.

[codex:cx_m_cryo_tank_x]
中：货罐里有液体时，蒸气压大约每升高 20 °C 翻一倍。真空夹套完好时，漏热速度很慢，在一份合同的作业时间内温度升幅不到 10 °C；夹套破损后，温度上升会快得多。
英：With liquid in the tank, the vapour pressure roughly doubles for every 20 °C rise. While the vacuum jacket is intact, heat leaks in slowly, and the temperature rises by less than 10 °C within the working time of one contract. Once the jacket is damaged, the temperature rises much faster.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff]
中：蒸发升压
英：BOIL-OFF

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_a]
中：低温罐里的液体会持续蒸发，使罐内压力升高。制冷系统运行时，罐压能够保持稳定。
英：The liquid in the cryogenic tanks keeps boiling off, which raises the tank pressure. While the refrigeration system is running, the tank pressure stays stable.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_b]
中：如果先切断电源，制冷系统停止运行，罐压就会升高，可能导致安全阀放空、货物损失，甚至引发爆罐。
英：If you cut the power first, the refrigeration system stops running and the tank pressure rises. This may make the relief valve vent, losing cargo, or even make the tank burst.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_c]
中：保持制冷系统运行，同时用软管抽走液体；抽出的液体越多，罐压越低。最后再切断电源。
英：Keep the refrigeration system running and draw the liquid off through the hose at the same time. The more you draw off, the lower the tank pressure. Cut the power last.

[codex:cx_m_boiloff_x]
中：蒸发后的气体体积是液体的几百倍，因此少量漏热也会使罐压明显升高。保温层破损后，压力上升得更快；安全阀排出的气体也是货物的一部分。
英：Boiled-off gas takes up hundreds of times the volume of the liquid, so even a small heat leak raises the tank pressure noticeably. When the insulation is damaged, the pressure rises faster. The gas vented by the relief valve is part of the cargo too.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve]
中：冻住的安全阀
英：A FROZEN RELIEF VALVE

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_a]
中：安全阀出口结了冰，阀门被冻住，无法正常泄压。
英：One tank's relief valve has an ice plug in its outlet and won't lift.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_b]
中：罐内压力超过安全值，阀门也打不开。压力继续升高，可能导致爆罐。
英：Past its set pressure it doesn't relieve; the tank climbs on until it bursts.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_c]
中：先测阀门温度。如果结冰，就加热解冻；也可以先抽走罐内气体，降低压力。
英：Read each relief valve's temperature: thaw the abnormally cold one with its heater, or draw its tank down first.

[codex:cx_m_frozen_valve_x]
中：泄放的低温气体在出口膨胀降温，空气或水汽在出口冻成冰塞。测温探针能发现它：冻住的阀比其它阀冷得多。
英：Cold gas venting through the outlet expands and chills it, freezing moisture into an ice plug. The probe shows it: the frozen valve reads far colder than the rest.

[codex:cx_m_antimatter]

英：[PLACEHOLDER] ANTIMATTER TRAP

[codex:cx_m_antimatter_a]
中：【占位】储存阱里是几微克反物质，靠环形磁体的磁约束悬在真空里。
英：[PLACEHOLDER] A few micrograms of antimatter held in vacuum by the ring magnets' field.

[codex:cx_m_antimatter_b]

[codex:cx_m_antimatter_c]

[codex:cx_m_antimatter_x]

英：[PLACEHOLDER]

[codex:cx_m_dilution]
中：稀释制冷机按序回温
英：DILUTION FRIDGE WARM-UP

[codex:cx_m_dilution_a]
中：量子芯片放在混合室里，温度接近绝对零度。外面有好几层冷板，靠制冷机一直降温。
英：The qubit chip sits in the mixing chamber at millikelvin, hung under stage after stage.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_b]
中：混合气还没回收就停冷头，气体受热沸腾，可能损坏冷板。芯片温度太低时直接加热，也可能裂开。
英：Stop the cold head before recovering the mixture and it boils off; heat it or touch it while still at millikelvin and the chip cracks.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_c]
中：先停循环泵，回收混合气，再停脉管冷头。等冷板自然回温后再加热，拆卸前锁住隔振台。
英：Stop circulation → recover the mixture → stop the pulse tube → let the stages warm, heaters only past the coldest → lock the isolation table before any cutting.

[codex:cx_m_dilution_x]
中：混合室最冷、回温最慢。在低于热震温度时打开加热器，芯片表面和内部温差太大，硅片会裂；低于脆性温度时任何振动都能让它碎。隔振台没锁，附近的切割会通过台面震到芯片。
英：The mixing chamber is coldest and warms slowest. Heaters switched on below the shock temperature put too much difference between the chip's surface and its body and the silicon cracks; below the brittle temperature any shake breaks it. With the isolation table unlocked, a nearby cut shakes the chip.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain]
中：冷链不能断
英：THE COLD CHAIN

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_a]
中：三个冷舱的温度保持在 −25 °C，由冷机和制冷主机提供冷却。
英：The temperature of the three cold holds is kept at −25 °C, with cooling supplied by the chillers and the refrigeration plant.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_b]
中：如果切断电源或损坏制冷系统，冷舱就会逐渐回温。温度超过 −5 °C 后，货物会解冻，价值只剩原来的 45%。
英：If you cut the power or damage the refrigeration system, the cold holds gradually warm up. Once the temperature goes above −5 °C, the cargo thaws and keeps only 45% of its value.

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_c]

[codex:cx_m_coldchain_x]
中：冷冻货物需要始终保持低温；一旦解冻，价值损失无法挽回。制冷剂需要从储液罐的回收口闭环回收。打开舱顶货舱口之前，还要先给舱室放气。
英：Frozen cargo must be kept cold at all times. Once it thaws, the loss of value cannot be recovered. The refrigerant must be recovered in a closed loop at the receiver’s recovery port. Before opening a hold’s roof hatch, also vent the hold first.

[codex:cx_m_ice]
中：冰体
英：THE ICE

[codex:cx_m_ice_a]
中：整座冰山都是需要回收的货物。每块冰含有 0.6 m³ 的水，可以用加热垫逐块融化。
英：The whole iceberg is cargo to be recovered. Each block contains 0.6 m³ of water and can be melted block by block with the heater pads.

[codex:cx_m_ice_b]

英：If 3 m³ of melt water has built up without being drawn off and the bus is still live, the engine room shorts out. If you cut along a crack while the saddles are still clamping the ice, the ice splits.

[codex:cx_m_ice_c]
中：启动加热垫，融化冰块的同时，从水总管接口抽走融水。等剩余冰体不到一半时，再松开鞍座。
英：Switch on the heater pads, and draw the melt water off at the water header port as the blocks melt. Release the saddles only once less than half of the ice is left.

[codex:cx_m_ice_x]
中：融化 1 kg 的冰需要 334 kJ 的能量。冰的切割速度很慢，因为切口会重新冻结。应变表显示冰体裂纹的扩展程度，初始读数为 90%。
英：Melting 1 kg of ice takes 334 kJ of energy. Ice cuts very slowly, because the kerf freezes over again. The strain gauge shows how far the crack in the ice has spread, and its initial reading is 90%.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque]
中：钻杆残余扭矩与泥浆压力
英：TRAPPED TORQUE AND MUD PRESSURE

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_a]
中：八节立根仍然带有残余扭矩，钻杆内部也有受压的泥浆。
英：The eight stands still hold residual torque, and the inside of the drill pipe holds pressurised mud.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_b]
中：钻杆带有扭矩时卸扣，钻杆可能反弹、扭断并甩打钻台；带压卸扣则会使泥浆喷出，污染设备。
英：If you break out a connection while the pipe is under torque, the pipe may spring back, twist off and whip against the drill floor. If you break it out under pressure, mud sprays out and contaminates the equipment.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_c]
中：关闭转盘刹车释放扭矩，等扭矩表读数低于 5%。再打开泄压阀排出可回收的泥浆，最后拆卸钻杆接头。
英：Release the rotary brake to bleed the torque, until the torque gauge reads below 5%. Then open the relief valve to drain off the recoverable mud, and finally break out the tool joints.

[codex:cx_m_drill_torque_x]
中：扭矩按照时间常数呈指数下降。通过泄压阀排到回收口的泥浆可以作为货物回收。扭矩表和压力表都安装在钻台上。
英：The torque decays exponentially according to a time constant. The mud drained through the relief valve to the recovery port can be recovered as cargo. The torque gauge and the pressure gauge are both mounted on the drill floor.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips]
中：卡瓦与自上而下
英：SLIPS, TOP DOWN

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_a]
中：每节立根下方都有一副卡瓦，用来托住该节立根和下面的整串钻杆。
英：Under each stand there is a set of slips, which holds that stand and the whole drill string below it.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_b]
中：如果先卸下面的钻杆，上面的钻杆会因失去支撑而掉落。如果卡瓦没有安装到位就卸扣，下方整串钻杆会掉进井内。
英：If you break out a lower stand first, the pipe above loses its support and drops. If you break out a connection while the slips are not properly set, the whole drill string below falls into the hole.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_c]
中：从最上面一节开始拆卸。每拆一节之前，都要确认下方的卡瓦已经安装到位，再逐节向下拆卸。
英：Start with the top stand. Before breaking out each stand, check that the slips below it are set, then work downwards stand by stand.

[codex:cx_m_drill_slips_x]
中：卡瓦由楔形牙块组成，钻杆越重，卡瓦夹得越紧。拆卸期间，必须始终使用卡瓦或吊卡固定钻杆。
英：Slips are wedge-shaped dies, and the heavier the pipe, the tighter they grip. During the work, the drill string must always be held by the slips or the elevator.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet]
中：交叉连通罐
英：CROSS-CONNECTED TANKS

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_a]
中：三个储罐通过交叉阀连接到同一根总管，大型泵通过泵房回收口抽取总管里的液体。
英：Three tanks are connected to one header through crossover valves, and the big pump draws the liquid from the header through the pump room’s recovery port.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_b]
中：如果提前断电，回收泵就无法运行。液体抽干后继续运行水泵，会因空转而损坏水泵；如果先排掉罐顶气体再抽液，储罐可能被吸瘪。
英：If you cut the power early, the recovery pump cannot run. If the pump keeps running after the liquid has been drawn off, it runs dry and is damaged. If you vent the ullage gas from the tank tops before drawing the liquid, the tank gets sucked flat.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_c]
中：打开交叉阀，在保持增压的情况下抽完液体并关闭回收口，再关闭增压阀、排出罐顶气体。支管中的残液需要从低点排放口抽走。
英：Open the crossover valves, draw the liquid off while keeping the pressurisation on, and close the recovery port. Then close the pressurisation valve and vent the ullage gas from the tank tops. Draw the residual liquid in the branch lines off at the low-point drains.

[codex:cx_m_tanknet_x]
中：交叉阀打开后，各储罐的液位会通过总管逐渐趋于一致。增压阀打开时，氮气球罐会维持储罐压力，避免抽液时产生真空。有些合同中的交叉阀虽然显示关闭，但实际存在内漏，关闭后相应储罐的液位仍会下降。
英：With the crossover valves open, the liquid levels in the tanks gradually even out through the header. While the pressurisation valve is open, the nitrogen sphere keeps up the tank pressure and prevents a vacuum forming during drawing. In some contracts, a crossover valve reads closed but actually leaks internally, so the level in the corresponding tank keeps falling even with it closed.

[codex:cx_m_immersion]

英：IMMERSION COOLING

[codex:cx_m_waterball]

英：In free fall, surface tension pulls the water into a ball, and the net only cradles it. 30 m³ of water has a mass of 30 tonnes. When the ball bursts, the water drifts apart in all directions, keeping its original speed.

[codex:cx_m_media]

[codex:cx_m_media_a]

[codex:cx_m_media_x]

英：Header port 1 connects to tank 3, port 2 connects to tank 1, and port 3 connects to tank 2. Without sampling, you may connect the wrong one. If you remove the header while the branch lines still hold liquid, all the liquids leak and you are fined by the leaked volume. The separator may hold residual pressure, which must be relieved at the port on the tower.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash]
中：富氧闪燃
英：OXYGEN-RICH FLASH FIRE

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_a]
中：各个舱室的氧气浓度不同，部分舱室的氧气浓度远高于普通空气中的 21%。
英：The oxygen concentration differs between rooms, and in some rooms it is far above the 21% of ordinary air.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_b]
中：舱室有气压且氧气浓度超过阈值时，在附近使用激光会引发全舱闪燃，烧坏设备并损伤无人机。
英：When a room has pressure and its oxygen concentration is over the threshold, using the laser nearby causes a flash fire in the whole room, burning the equipment and damaging the drone.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_c]
中：先检查各舱室的氧气浓度，分别通过各自的接口放气，再使用激光。
英：First check the oxygen concentration in each room and vent each room through its own port, then use the laser.

[codex:cx_m_o2_flash_x]
中：氧气浓度从 21% 升至 35% 以上时，可燃物的点燃温度会明显下降，燃烧速度也会成倍提高，织物和塑料更容易被点燃。打开舱门后，两侧舱室的空气会混合。
英：When oxygen rises from 21% to over 35%, the ignition temperature of combustible materials falls markedly and burning rates increase several times over, so fabrics and plastics ignite more easily. After a door is opened, the air in the rooms on both sides mixes.

[codex:cx_m_composite]
中：复合材料毒烟
英：COMPOSITE FUMES

[codex:cx_m_composite_a]
中：机身板件由碳纤维复合材料制成，并通过螺栓固定。
英：The airframe panels are made of carbon-fibre composite and are fixed with bolts.

[codex:cx_m_composite_b]
中：在有气体的舱室附近用激光切割板件，会产生有毒烟雾，使电子设备价值降低、玻璃起雾。
英：If you cut a panel with the laser near a room that holds air, it produces toxic smoke, which lowers the value of electronics and fogs glass.

[codex:cx_m_composite_c]
中：拧下螺栓，整块拆除板件；也可以先将舱室抽成真空，再进行切割。
英：Unbolt the panels and remove them whole. Alternatively, vent the room to vacuum first and then cut.

[codex:cx_m_composite_x]
中：环氧树脂基体受激光加热后会发生热解，产生一氧化碳、氰化氢和细碳纤维粉尘。将舱室抽成真空时，烟雾会随空气一同排出。
英：The epoxy resin matrix pyrolyses when heated by the laser, producing carbon monoxide, hydrogen cyanide and fine carbon-fibre dust. When the room is vented to vacuum, the smoke leaves with the air.

[codex:cx_m_radiation]
中：辐射阴影
英：RADIATION SHADOW

[codex:cx_m_radiation_a]
中：反应堆虽然已经停止运行，但堆芯附近仍然有很强的辐射，距离堆芯 1 米的位置，辐射剂量率还有 3.2 Sv/h。屏蔽板、掩蔽间和液氢罐都可以阻挡辐射，在它们后面会形成相对安全的区域。
英：Although the reactor has stopped running, the area near the core still has strong radiation. At 1 metre from the core, the radiation dose rate is still 3.2 Sv/h. The shield, the shelter and the liquid hydrogen tanks can all block radiation, and the area behind them is relatively safe.

[codex:cx_m_radiation_b]
中：无人机受到的累计辐射剂量超过 150 mSv 后，电子设备就会开始出现故障。如果使用激光切割设备直接切到堆芯，还会引发核爆炸。
英：Once the drone’s accumulated radiation dose exceeds 150 mSv, its electronics start to fail. If you cut the equipment with the laser straight into the core, it also causes a nuclear explosion.

[codex:cx_m_radiation_c]
中：先用吊车把屏蔽板移动到堆芯前面，利用屏蔽板挡住辐射，再让无人机进入后方区域进行拆卸。操作之前还要锁定控制鼓，最后把整个堆芯从危险品出口运走，不要直接切割堆芯。
英：First use the crane to move the shield in front of the core, use it to block the radiation, then let the drone enter the area behind it to work. Before working, also lock the control drums, and finally send the whole core out through the hazard mouth. Do not cut the core directly.

[codex:cx_m_radiation_x]
中：距离堆芯越远，受到的辐射就越少，剂量率会按照距离平方的反比下降。在距离堆芯 7 米的位置，剂量率大约还有 65 mSv/h，停留一分多钟就可能超过安全剂量。游戏里每经过 1 秒，都会按照现实中的 2 分钟计算辐射剂量。\n屏蔽板可以阻挡辐射，只要把屏蔽板放在堆芯和无人机之间，无人机就能利用后面的辐射阴影进行作业。屏蔽板即使还吊在吊车上，也一样能够发挥作用。剂量探头显示的是当前位置的辐射剂量率，可以根据读数判断作业区域是否安全。
英：The farther you are from the core, the less radiation you receive, and the dose rate falls with the inverse square of the distance. At 7 metres from the core, the dose rate is still about 65 mSv/h, and staying a little over a minute there may exceed the safe dose. In the game, every 1 second that passes counts as 2 minutes of real time for the radiation dose.\nThe shield can block radiation. As long as you put the shield between the core and the drone, the drone can work in the radiation shadow behind it. The shield works even while it is still hanging from the crane. The dose probe shows the radiation dose rate at its current position, and you can judge from the reading whether the work area is safe.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat]
中：停机不等于冷却
英：STOPPED ISN'T COLD

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_a]
中：炉芯或堆芯停止运行后，温度仍可能达到几百度，需要依靠冷却回路降温。
英：After a furnace core or reactor core stops running, its temperature may still reach several hundred degrees, and it must be cooled down by its cooling loop.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_b]
中：高温时切断冷却，热冲击可能使炉芯或堆芯开裂。尚未冷却完成时吊出炉芯或收起散热瓣，也可能造成开裂或变形。
英：If you cut the cooling while it is hot, thermal shock may crack the furnace core or reactor core. Lifting out the core, or folding the radiator petals, before cooling is complete can also cause cracking or deformation.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_c]
中：保持冷却泵供电，并确保冷却回路充满冷却液。观察温度，等炉芯或堆芯充分冷却后，再断电并吊出。
英：Keep the cooling pump powered and make sure the cooling loop is full of coolant. Watch the temperature, and once the furnace core or reactor core has cooled enough, cut the power and lift it out.

[codex:cx_m_decay_heat_x]
中：冷却回路运行时，温度约每秒下降 4 °C。MF-126 炉芯在 350 °C 以上停止冷却会开裂，在 150 °C 以上吊出会开裂；NR-95 堆芯在 60 °C 以上收起散热瓣会变形。
英：While the cooling loop is running, the temperature falls by about 4 °C per second. The MF-126 furnace core cracks if cooling is stopped above 350 °C, and cracks if lifted out above 150 °C. The NR-95 reactor core deforms if the radiator petals are folded above 60 °C.

[codex:cx_m_thermal]
中：近日点余热
英：PERIHELION HEAT

[codex:cx_m_thermal_a]

英：The ship has just passed close to the sun, and the heat tiles and forward hull in each zone are at 300–800 °C.

[codex:cx_m_thermal_b]

英：Cutting a heat tile in a hot zone makes the tile shatter. Equipment taken out of a hot zone may be damaged by the heat. The drone is also damaged if it enters the hottest zone.

[codex:cx_m_thermal_c]

英：Connect the berth cooling loop to speed up the cooling. Check the temperature of each zone with the temperature probe, and start the removal once it has cooled.

[codex:cx_m_thermal_x]
中：在真空环境中，设备只能通过热辐射散热，各区域会以不同速度自然冷却。冷却回路能够接入的区域降温更快。
英：In vacuum, equipment can only lose heat by thermal radiation, and each zone cools naturally at a different rate. Zones that the cooling loop can reach cool faster.

[codex:cx_m_bio]
中：负压与生物污染
英：NEGATIVE PRESSURE AND BIOHAZARD

[codex:cx_m_bio_a]
中：隔离舱的压力低于相邻舱室，舱内的生物废物箱、滤芯和废液罐都带有污染物。
英：The pressure in an isolation room is lower than in the neighbouring rooms, and the biohazard boxes, filters and waste tanks inside it are contaminated.

[codex:cx_m_bio_b]
中：如果切开危险品或在未密封时搬运，污染物会进入舱内空气并随气流扩散。干净舱室受到污染后，设备价值会降低四分之一。
英：If you cut a hazardous item open, or move it without sealing it, the contaminant gets into the room air and spreads with the airflow. When a clean room is contaminated, the value of its equipment drops by a quarter.

[codex:cx_m_bio_c]
中：先密封每件生物危险品，再通过回收软管的滤芯放气，最后打开舱门。
英：Seal every biohazard item first, then vent through the filter on the recovery hose, and finally open the doors.

[codex:cx_m_bio_x]
中：负压使空气流向隔离舱，限制污染物向外扩散。如果打开舱门或切开舱壁，负压条件就会消失，气流方向可能反转。回收软管的过滤器可以过滤空气中的污染物。
英：Negative pressure makes air flow towards the isolation room, which limits contaminants from spreading outwards. If a door is opened or a wall is cut, the negative pressure is lost and the airflow may reverse. The filter on the recovery hose removes contaminants from the air.

[codex:cx_m_balance]
中：按受力顺序释放
英：RELEASE IN ORDER OF LOAD

[codex:cx_m_balance_a]
中：卷扬机缆绳仍然绷紧，两台外挂吊舱分别安装在舰尾的上方和下方。
英：The winch cable is still taut, and the two outboard pods are mounted aft, one above and one below the stern.

[codex:cx_m_balance_b]
中：如果没有松开刹车就拆除卷扬轴，卷筒会被甩出。如果只拆除一台吊舱，另一台吊舱的载荷会使船体扭转。
英：If you remove the winch shaft without releasing the brake, the drum is thrown out. If you remove only one pod, the load of the other pod twists the hull.

[codex:cx_m_balance_c]
中：先拔出刹车销，确认张力表读数归零。拆除吊舱前，在舰尾设置船体支撑，或者同时拆除两台吊舱。
英：Pull the brake pins first and make sure the tension gauge reads zero. Before removing a pod, set the hull support at the stern, or remove both pods together.

[codex:cx_m_balance_x]
中：对称安装的两个重物可以相互平衡。拆掉其中一个后，船体需要承受另一个重物产生的全部力矩；如果超过承载能力，吊梁和支柱就会扭坏。锁喉夹块受到的力来自卷扬机缆绳，需要处理刹车、锁销和蓄能器。
英：Two weights mounted symmetrically balance each other. After one is removed, the hull has to carry the whole moment of the other weight, and if that exceeds its capacity, the beams and struts twist and fail. The load on the throat clamp block comes from the winch cable, so the brake, the locking pins and the accumulator all have to be dealt with.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich]
中：拆三明治
英：UNMAKING THE SANDWICH

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_a]
中：上下两条船体通过 8 个液压夹钳夹住中间的居住舱。
英：The upper and lower hulls clamp the habitat between them with 8 hydraulic clamps.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_b]
中：货舱没有放气就松开夹钳，夹钳会弹开。如果一侧比另一侧多松开两个夹钳，货舱会发生扭转，已经开裂的横梁 2 也会断裂。
英：If you release a clamp before the hold is vented, the clamp springs open. If one side has two more clamps released than the other, the hold twists, and the already cracked beam 2 also breaks.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_c]
中：先给货舱放气，再按以下顺序交叉成对松开夹钳：上1→下4→上4→下1→上2→下3→上3→下2。松开上1和下1后，再拆除横梁 2。
英：Vent the hold first, then release the clamps in crossed pairs in this order: U1→L4→U4→L1→U2→L3→U3→L2. Remove beam 2 after U1 and L1 have been released.

[codex:cx_m_sandwich_x]
中：横梁 2 已经开裂，载荷表读数为 192 kN，其余横梁的读数为 120 kN。如果夹钳仍处于保压状态时整件拆下，钳口会弹开。三套电路相互独立：上部船体使用主电池，下部船体和货舱各有独立电池。
英：Beam 2 is already cracked, and its load cell reads 192 kN, while the other beams read 120 kN. If a clamp is removed whole while it is still holding pressure, its jaw springs open. The three circuits are independent of each other: the upper hull uses the main battery, and the lower hull and the hold each have their own battery.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam]
中：货柜就是承力梁
英：THE CONTAINERS ARE THE BEAM

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_a]
中：货柜通过四角的扭锁相互连接，整列货柜共同构成船体的承力箱梁。
英：The containers are connected to each other by twist-locks at the four corners, and each column of containers together forms a load-bearing box beam of the hull.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_b]
中：外侧货柜仍在时卸空内侧一列，会使该列结构弯曲。同一列货柜如果从下往上拆，上面的货柜会压下来。切断仍然绷紧的拉杆，拉杆会回弹。
英：If you empty an inner column while the outer containers are still there, that column buckles. If you remove containers in a column from the bottom up, the ones above come down. If you cut a tie rod that is still tight, the rod whips back.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_c]
中：从两端向中间卸货，每列从上往下拆。拆除拉杆前，先松开花篮螺丝，确认拉力计读数为 0；带压货柜需要先抽气。
英：Unload from both ends towards the middle, and work each column from the top down. Before removing a tie rod, slacken the turnbuckles and check that the tension gauge reads 0. Pump down pressurised containers first.

[codex:cx_m_boxbeam_x]
中：船坞夹具固定着第 4 列。每列的弯曲程度等于外侧仍在位货柜的数量乘以力臂，再除以本列的刚度；刚度由弦杆和在位货柜数量决定。应变片显示本列弯曲程度占极限的百分比。住人箱的压力为 60 kPa，低温箱为 450 kPa。
英：The berth clamps hold column 4. The bending of each column equals the number of containers still in place outboard of it times the lever arm, divided by the stiffness of the column itself; the stiffness is set by the chords and the number of containers in place. The strain gauges show the bending of the column as a percentage of its limit. Accommodation boxes are at 60 kPa, and cryogenic boxes at 450 kPa.

[codex:cx_m_sail]
中：帆的张力网
英：THE SAIL'S TENSION NET

[codex:cx_m_sail_a]
中：四台绞盘的刹车分别固定四个象限的帆膜和撑索，每根撑索的张力为 12 kN。
英：Four winch brakes hold the film and stays of the four quadrants in place, and the tension in each stay is 12 kN.

[codex:cx_m_sail_b]
中：帆膜仍然绷紧时切割，会产生向两侧扩展的裂口。切断撑索会使角梁甩向吊舱。如果没有接地就接触帆膜，静电会损伤无人机。

[codex:cx_m_sail_c]
中：先连接接地线 GND，从右侧检修门洞进入通道，通过舱门旁的 G-G 接口抽气，再打开舱门 HT-G。随后松开绞盘刹车 W，启动收帆电机 F，将四角的帆膜卷起。
英：First connect the GND earth lead, enter the lane through the service gate on the right, vent through the G-G port beside the hatch, then open hatch HT-G. Then release the winch brakes W and start the furling motors F to roll up the film at the four corners.

[codex:cx_m_sail_x]
中：完整收帆时，先连接左下角的接地线 GND。外框和撑索包围着吊舱，右侧外框留有检修门洞，门洞后方有一条没有帆膜的通道，可以通过这里飞到舱门，避免撞破帆膜。将软管接到舱门旁的 G-G 气口，把舱压抽到 5 kPa 以下，再打开舱门 HT-G。进入后，依次松开 W-NW、W-NE、W-SE、W-SW 四台绞盘的刹车，让四角的帆膜松弛。然后启动 F-NW、F-NE、F-SE、F-SW 四台收帆电机，将四角的帆膜逐块卷上绞盘。最后拆下四个帆卷，送往设备回收口。
英：To furl the sail whole, first connect the earth lead GND at the bottom left. The frame and stays enclose the gondola. The right-hand frame has a service gate, and behind the gate there is a lane with no film, through which you can fly to the hatch without breaking the film. Connect the hose to the G-G port beside the hatch, pump the gondola pressure down below 5 kPa, then open hatch HT-G. Once inside, release the brakes on the four winches W-NW, W-NE, W-SE and W-SW in turn, so that the film at the four corners goes slack. Then start the four furling motors F-NW, F-NE, F-SE and F-SW, and roll the film at each corner onto its winch piece by piece. Finally, remove the four sail rolls and send them to the equipment mouth.

[codex:cx_m_softshell]

英：A fabric shell can only carry tension, not compression. When it is deflated, the gasbag loses its shape. The service bay battery is an independent supply and is still live after the spine battery is cut off. The capacitor also has its own discharge point.

[codex:cx_m_tether]

[codex:cx_m_tether_b]
中：旋转时切断系绳，两个吊舱会沿切线方向甩出。如果不打开冷却就制动，滚轮在 6 秒后会打滑，导致制动器永久失效。

英：Start the brake cooling first, then brake. Once the pods have stopped rotating, release the tensioner and remove the tether. Keep the propellant for now: if the brake fails, you have to fire the attitude control system on pod B in reverse.

[codex:cx_m_tether_x]
中：系绳张力 = 40 kN × (rpm/2)² + 张紧器提供的 8 kN。启动冷却时，制动可使转速每秒下降 0.025 rpm。磨损段只剩 5/7 股；即使吊舱已经停止旋转，如果没有松开张紧器就切割，整根系绳仍会崩断。
英：Tether tension = 40 kN × (rpm/2)² + the 8 kN supplied by the tensioner. With cooling on, the brake can reduce the speed by 0.025 rpm per second. The worn section has only 5 of 7 strands left. Even after the pods have stopped rotating, if you cut it without releasing the tensioner, the whole tether still snaps.

[codex:cx_m_train]

英：The bending gauge on K-4 shows the load still attached behind it, about 160 t when full. If a coolant hose’s valve is still open, coolant sprays out when it is cut. Close the valves first, then drain the residual liquid at the low-point drains.

[codex:cx_m_ore]

[codex:cx_m_ore_x]

[codex:cx_m_glass]

英：A 2 m² viewing window with 100 kPa across it carries a push equal to about 20 tonnes. Loosen its strips and bolts and that pressure can push the glass out. PS-12's central shaft needs extra care: with the hatches on both levels open, the two levels are one space, and venting either one vents the other too, so check both hatches before removal.

[codex:cx_m_nested]

[codex:cx_s_ullage]
中：带压罐与气垫
英：PRESSURISED TANKS AND THE ULLAGE

[codex:cx_s_ullage_a]
中：推进剂储罐的顶部有一层高压气体，也叫增压气。它会对罐内的液体施加压力，让推进剂能够从出口正常排出。
英：The top of a propellant tank holds a layer of high-pressure gas, the pressurant. It presses on the liquid so the propellant can flow out of the outlet.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_b]
中：如果储罐还带着压力就直接切开，高压气体会把里面的推进剂一起喷出来。如果把整个储罐切断，里面的推进剂可能在几秒钟内全部喷出。完成泄压以后，即使储罐出现破口，剩余的液体也只会缓慢渗出。
英：Cut a tank while it is still under pressure and the gas blows the propellant out with it. Sever the whole tank and the propellant can all spray out in seconds. Once the tank is vented, liquid only seeps slowly from a hole.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_c]
中：拆解之前，先关闭增压气体的供应，再通过排气口释放罐内的高压气体。确认储罐完成泄压以后，才能开始抽取推进剂，最后再拆除储罐。
英：Before taking it apart, shut off the pressurant supply, then release the gas through the vent. Only when the tank is confirmed vented, draw off the propellant, and remove the tank last.

[codex:cx_s_ullage_x]
中：增压系统会不断向储罐补充高压气体，所以只打开排气口还不够，只要增压系统没有关闭，罐内压力就很难降下来。正确的处理顺序是先关闭增压气源，再排出罐内的高压气体。完成泄压以后，储罐内部主要剩下推进剂自身的蒸气压，即使切开储罐，液体通常也只会从破口缓慢渗出，不会再因为增压气体的压力而大量喷射。
英：The pressurisation system keeps feeding gas into the tank, so opening the vent alone is not enough: while the system is on, the tank pressure barely falls. The right order is to shut off the pressurant first, then vent the gas in the tank. Once vented, mostly the propellant's own vapour pressure is left, so even if the tank is cut, the liquid usually seeps slowly from the hole instead of being driven out in a jet.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic]
中：自燃推进剂
英：HYPERGOLIC PROPELLANTS

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_a]
中：甲基肼储存在 T-1 和 T-3 罐中，四氧化二氮单独储存在 T-2 罐中。T-2 没有连接燃料总管，需要单独处理。
英：Monomethylhydrazine is stored in tanks T-1 and T-3, and nitrogen tetroxide on its own in T-2. T-2 is not connected to the fuel manifold and has to be handled separately.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_b]
中：这两种推进剂只要接触就会起火，不需要点火，在真空环境中也可能发生爆炸。爆燃造成的伤害范围按照 TNT 当量计算。
英：These two propellants catch fire as soon as they touch, with no ignition needed, and can explode even in vacuum. The blast's damage radius is worked out in TNT equivalent.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_c]
中：通过专用排放口 LD-2 单独抽空 T-2 罐，拆解时注意分开处理两种推进剂，千万不要同时切开它们的储罐或管线。
英：Empty T-2 on its own through its dedicated drain LD-2. Keep the two propellants apart while dismantling, and never cut their tanks or lines at the same time.

[codex:cx_m_hypergolic_x]
中：自燃推进剂不需要额外安装点火器，发动机即使反复启动几百次，也能保持较高的可靠性，所以经常用在航天器的姿态控制和轨道调整上。不过这两种推进剂都有很强的毒性和腐蚀性，一旦发生泄漏，蒸气混合后就可能直接起火。拆解的时候必须把两种推进剂分开排放，等一种处理完成后，再处理另一种，避免发生意外。
英：Hypergolic propellants need no igniter, so an engine stays reliable even after hundreds of restarts; that is why they are common in attitude control and orbit adjustment. But both are highly toxic and corrosive, and if they leak, their mixing vapours can catch fire at once. When dismantling, drain the two separately: finish one before starting the other.

[codex:cx_s_contents]
中：检查容器里的内容物
英：Checking what a container holds

[codex:cx_s_contents_a]
中：灭火瓶、气瓶和储罐里面通常都装着东西，使用测量工具扫描以后，就能在检测结果中看到新增的「内容物」信息。
英：Extinguisher bottles, gas cylinders and tanks usually hold something. Scan one with the measuring tool and a new ‘CONTENTS’ line appears in the reading.

[codex:cx_s_contents_b]
中：气体容器的接口标记为「编号-V」，液体容器的接口标记为「编号-D」。装有液体的储罐通常比较重，如果里面的液体还没有排出，可能会因为重量太大而无法搬动。
英：Gas containers' ports are marked ‘ID-V’ and liquid containers' ports ‘ID-D’. A tank holding liquid is usually heavy, and until it is drained it may be too heavy to move.

[codex:cx_s_contents_c]
中：处理容器之前，先使用测量工具检查里面装的是什么，以及当前的压力有多高。确认情况以后，再决定是排放气体、抽出液体，还是直接把整个容器运走。
英：Before dealing with a container, check with the measuring tool what is inside and how high the pressure is. Then decide whether to vent the gas, draw off the liquid, or take the whole container away.

[codex:cx_s_contents_x]
中：灭火瓶里通常装有压力约为 5.7 MPa 的二氧化碳，氧气瓶里的氧气压力则可以达到 15 MPa。其他容器还可能装有氮气、压缩空气、氦气或氢气，部分设备还带有蓄能器。液体储罐里可能装着水、泥浆、泡沫液、润滑油或者服务推进剂。即使两个容器的外形完全一样，里面装的东西也可能不同，直接切开以后造成的后果自然也不一样。
英：An extinguisher bottle usually holds carbon dioxide at about 5.7 MPa, and an oxygen cylinder can hold oxygen at up to 15 MPa. Other containers may hold nitrogen, compressed air, helium or hydrogen, and some equipment carries accumulators. Liquid tanks may hold water, mud, foam concentrate, lubricating oil or service propellant. Two containers that look exactly alike can hold different things, and cutting them open has different consequences.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich]
中：富氧助燃
英：OXYGEN-RICH AIR

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_a]
中：舱内的氧气浓度超过了正常空气中的 21%，可能是氧气瓶破损造成了泄漏，也可能是舱室本身就处于富氧状态。
英：The oxygen in the room is above the 21% of normal air. A broken oxygen cylinder may have leaked, or the room may be oxygen-rich by design.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_b]
中：氧气浓度越高，周围的材料就越容易被点燃，起火以后也会燃烧得更快。激光切割产生的高温和火花都可能引起火灾，舱内如果还有氢气，危险就更大了。
英：The more oxygen, the more easily things around catch fire, and the faster they burn once alight. The heat and sparks of laser cutting can start a fire, and if there is hydrogen in the room too, the danger is greater.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_c]
中：使用激光切割前，先检测舱内的氧气浓度。如果浓度超标，就先排空舱内气体，处理完成以后再开始切割。
英：Before laser cutting, measure the oxygen in the room. If it is too high, vent the room first, then start cutting.

[codex:cx_s_o2_rich_x]
中：正常空气中的氮气能够稀释氧气，还能吸收燃烧时产生的一部分热量。当氧气浓度超过 30% 时，布料、塑料和润滑油的燃烧速度可能会增加好几倍。在纯氧环境下，就连钢丝都有可能被点燃。
英：The nitrogen in normal air dilutes the oxygen and absorbs part of the heat of burning. Above 30% oxygen, cloth, plastics and lubricating oil can burn several times faster. In pure oxygen, even steel wire can be set alight.
